===== FILE: src\check_box.rs =====
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
//! Check boxes.
//!
//! A check box toggles by itself. The other box does not change.
//! That is the difference from radio buttons.
//! Reuses the existing click command. No second command.

pub fn toggled_on(was_on: bool, now_on: bool, other_before: bool, other_after: bool) -> bool {
    !was_on && now_on && other_before == other_after
}

pub fn toggled_off(was_on: bool, now_on: bool, other_before: bool, other_after: bool) -> bool {
    was_on && !now_on && other_before == other_after
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn off_click_turns_only_that_box_on() {
        assert!(toggled_on(false, true, true, true));
        assert!(toggled_on(false, true, false, false));
        assert!(!toggled_on(false, true, true, false));
        assert!(!toggled_on(false, false, true, true));
    }

    #[test]
    fn second_click_turns_only_that_box_off() {
        assert!(toggled_off(true, false, true, true));
        assert!(!toggled_off(true, true, true, true));
        assert!(!toggled_off(true, false, false, true));
    }
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
//! CLI wiring for `sm`.

use clap::{Parser, Subcommand};

    dropdown_forever_allow, dropdown_open, dropdown_probe, dropdown_select, name_hit_center,
    resolve_click_name, DropdownOpts,
};

/// Exit code when a human touched the mouse during an input command (HUMAN_OVERRIDE).
/// 0 = ok, 1 = error, 2 = usage error (clap), 101 = panic (Rust default). 7 was unused.
pub const EXIT_HUMAN_OVERRIDE: i32 = 7;
/// Exit code when `--expect-hwnd` / `--expect-title` did not match the root window under the
/// press point (HIT_MISS). No input was sent. 8 was unused.
pub const EXIT_HIT_MISS: i32 = 8;
/// Exit code when the target window's process runs at a HIGHER integrity level than sm (UIPI
/// would drop our input). No input was sent. 9 was unused.
pub const EXIT_UIPI_BLOCKED: i32 = 9;

/// Pure exit-code mapping. Priority: HUMAN_OVERRIDE (7) > UIPI_BLOCKED (9) > HIT_MISS (8) >
/// error (1) > ok (0).
pub fn exit_code(human_override: bool, uipi_blocked: bool, hit_miss: bool, ok: bool) -> i32 {
    if human_override {
        EXIT_HUMAN_OVERRIDE
    } else if uipi_blocked {
        EXIT_UIPI_BLOCKED
    } else if hit_miss {
        EXIT_HIT_MISS
    } else if ok {
        0
    } else {
        1
    }
}

#[derive(Parser, Debug)]
#[command(
    name = "sm",
    about = "DAD's Smooth Moves â€” Windows GUI motor (not a web brain)",
    after_help = "Human override: while an input command runs, an observe-only WH_MOUSE_LL hook watches the mouse. \
Any event without LLMHF_INJECTED (a person touched the mouse) stops further input, releases held buttons/keys, \
prints HUMAN_OVERRIDE on stderr and exits 7.\n\
Hit-test: with --expect-hwnd N (root hwnd equal) or --expect-title S (root title contains S, case-insensitive), \
the root window under the press point (WindowFromPoint -> GetAncestor GA_ROOT) is checked before the first \
button-down (click/drag also before moving). Mismatch: no input, HIT_MISS on stderr, exit 8.\n\
--events FILE: reference-only WinEvent log (an event = STARTED, not settled). `sm winevents --ms N --out FILE` listens only.\n\
Settle: --settle-shot PNG on an input verb grabs a pre-action frame, runs the action, waits for the screen to settle \
(first change <=800 ms, then 3 identical 1/8 gray frames, cap 2500 ms, flicker cells masked after 2 timeouts and listed) \
and saves the full-res PNG; --settle-log FILE (default PNG-settle.txt). `sm shot --out PNG [--settle]` captures only. \
Settle only times the PNG; it never acts, and the PNG still judges.\n\
Fingerprint (reference only): `sm fp --out JSON [--rect l,t,r,b | --hwnd N] [--compare JSON] [--threshold 10]` = 64-bit \
dHash (9x8 gradient) of the screen or a region; same state if Hamming distance <= threshold. --fp JSON (with --fp-rect / \
--fp-hwnd) fingerprints the final settled frame of --settle-shot; --fp without --settle-shot is a usage error (exit 2).\n\
UIPI: before the first button-down sm reads the integrity level of the process owning the root window under the press \
point (and its own). Target higher than sm: no input, UIPI_BLOCKED on stderr, exit 9. A failed query = integrity=unknown, \
logged, not blocked. `sm integrity --hwnd N | --x X --y Y` reads it only.\n\
Diff (read-only): `sm diff --before A.png --after B.png [--expect-rect l,t,r,b ...] [--ignore-cells FILE] [--tol 24] \
[--outside-max 200|0.1%] --out diff.json [--viz viz.png]` -> NO_CHANGE | EXPECTED_CHANGE | COLLATERAL | \
NO_EXPECTED_CHANGE (with a region) or CHANGED | NO_CHANGE. Global --expect-hwnd adds the window's LIVE frame bounds; \
to re-judge old pairs use --expect-rect.\n\
Exit codes: 0 ok | 1 error | 2 usage | 7 HUMAN_OVERRIDE | 8 HIT_MISS | 9 UIPI_BLOCKED | 101 panic \
(priority 7 > 9 > 8 > 1 > 0)"
)]
pub struct Cli {
    /// Write the low-level mouse path seen during the command (t x y flags injected) plus the verdict.
    #[arg(long, global = true)]
    pub trace: Option<String>,
    /// Write reference-only WinEvents (foreground, move/size, minimize, switch, snap preview) seen
    /// during the command: +ms t EVENT hwnd title frame. An event means STARTED, not settled.
    #[arg(long, global = true)]
    pub events: Option<String>,
    /// Before the first button-down, the root window under the point must have this hwnd
    /// (decimal or 0x hex). Mismatch: no input, HIT_MISS, exit 8.
    #[arg(long, global = true)]
    pub expect_hwnd: Option<String>,
    /// Like --expect-hwnd but the root window title must contain this text (case-insensitive).
    #[arg(long, global = true)]
    pub expect_title: Option<String>,
    /// Input verbs: grab a pre-action frame, run the action, settle, then save this full-res PNG.
    /// Settle only times the PNG; it never acts. Verdict SETTLED | NO_CHANGE | TIMEOUT in the log.
    #[arg(long, global = true)]
    pub settle_shot: Option<String>,
    /// Settle log file for --settle-shot / `shot --settle` (default: PNG name with -settle.txt).
    #[arg(long, global = true)]
    pub settle_log: Option<String>,
    /// With --settle-shot: write a dHash fingerprint JSON of the final settled frame (reference only).
    #[arg(long, global = true)]
    pub fp: Option<String>,
    /// Region for --fp: screen rect l,t,r,b (default whole screen).
    #[arg(long, global = true, allow_hyphen_values = true)]
    pub fp_rect: Option<String>,
    /// Region for --fp: this window's DWM extended frame bounds (read only).
    #[arg(long, global = true)]
    pub fp_hwnd: Option<String>,
    #[command(subcommand)]
    pub cmd: Commands,
}

        #[arg(long)]

    /// (DWM extended frame bounds). --compare JSON adds distance / threshold / same_state.
    Fp {
        #[arg(long)]
        out: String,
        #[arg(long, conflicts_with = "hwnd", allow_hyphen_values = true)]
        rect: Option<String>,
        #[arg(long)]
        hwnd: Option<String>,
        #[arg(long)]
        compare: Option<String>,
        #[arg(long, default_value_t = crate::dhash::DEFAULT_THRESHOLD)]
        threshold: u32,
    },
}

/// Arguments of `sm diff` (parsed values).
pub struct DiffArgs {
    pub before: String,
    pub after: String,
    pub expect_rect: Vec<String>,
    pub expect_hwnd: Option<String>,
    pub ignore_cells: Option<String>,
    pub tol: u8,
    pub outside_max: String,
    pub cell: usize,
    pub out: String,
    pub viz: Option<String>,
}

/// Build the diff parameters (pure except reading the ignore file and, with --expect-hwnd, the
/// window's live frame bounds). Returns (params, expect sources).
pub fn diff_params(a: &DiffArgs, frame: impl Fn(isize) -> Result<(i32, i32, i32, i32), String>) -> Result<(crate::diff::Params, Vec<String>), String> {
    let mut p = crate::diff::Params { tol: a.tol, cell: a.cell, ..Default::default() };
    p.outside_max = crate::diff::OutsideMax::parse(&a.outside_max)?;
    let mut src = Vec::new();
    for r in &a.expect_rect {
        let rr = crate::dhash::parse_rect(r).map_err(|e| e.replace("--rect", "--expect-rect"))?;
        p.expect.push(rr);
        src.push(format!("rect {r}"));
    }
    if let Some(h) = &a.expect_hwnd {
        let hw = crate::hittest::parse_hwnd(h)?;
        let fb = frame(hw)?;
        p.expect.push(fb);
        src.push(format!("hwnd {hw} LIVE frame bounds {},{},{},{} (read now, not at capture time)", fb.0, fb.1, fb.2, fb.3));
    }
    if let Some(f) = &a.ignore_cells {
        let t = std::fs::read_to_string(f).map_err(|e| format!("read {f}: {e}"))?;
        p.ignore = crate::diff::parse_ignore(&t)?;
    }
    Ok((p, src))
}

/// `sm diff`: decode both PNGs, compare, write JSON (+ optional viz PNG). Read-only.
pub fn diff_verb(a: &DiffArgs) -> Result<String, String> {
    let (p, src) = diff_params(a, crate::shot::frame_bounds)?;
    let before = crate::png_dec::read_file(&a.before)?;
    let after = crate::png_dec::read_file(&a.after)?;
    let r = crate::diff::diff(&before, &after, &p)?;
    let ts = crate::shot::local_timestamp().0;
    let json = crate::diff::to_json(&r, &p, &a.before, &a.after, &src, &ts);
    std::fs::write(&a.out, json).map_err(|e| format!("write {}: {e}", a.out))?;
    let mut msg = format!("{} json={}", crate::diff::summary(&r), a.out);
    if let Some(v) = &a.viz {
        let img = crate::diff::viz(&after, &r, &p);
        let png = crate::png_enc::encode_rgb(after.w, after.h, &img)?;
        std::fs::write(v, png).map_err(|e| format!("write {v}: {e}"))?;
        msg.push_str(&format!(" viz={v}"));
    }
    if !p.ignore.is_empty() {
        let l: Vec<&str> = p.ignore.iter().map(|c| c.label.as_str()).collect();
        msg.push_str(&format!(" ignored_cells={}", l.join(";")));
    }
    Ok(msg)
}

/// Region from `--rect` / `--hwnd` (at most one).
pub fn fp_region(rect: Option<&str>, hwnd: Option<&str>) -> Result<crate::dhash::Region, String> {
    use crate::dhash::Region;
    match (rect, hwnd) {
        (Some(_), Some(_)) => Err("use only one of the rect / hwnd region options".into()),
        (Some(r), None) => {
            let (l, t, rr, b) = crate::dhash::parse_rect(r)?;
            Ok(Region::Rect(l, t, rr, b))
        }
        (None, Some(h)) => Ok(Region::Hwnd(crate::hittest::parse_hwnd(h).map_err(|e| e.replace("--expect-hwnd", "--hwnd"))?)),
        (None, None) => Ok(Region::Screen),
    }
}

/// Validate the global --fp flags (before any input). Err = usage error (exit 2).
pub fn fp_global_region(
    fp: Option<&str>,
    settle_shot: Option<&str>,
    rect: Option<&str>,
    hwnd: Option<&str>,
) -> Result<Option<crate::dhash::Region>, String> {
    match (fp, settle_shot) {
        (None, _) if rect.is_some() || hwnd.is_some() => Err("--fp-rect / --fp-hwnd need --fp".into()),
        (None, _) => Ok(None),
        (Some(_), None) => Err("--fp is only valid together with --settle-shot".into()),
        (Some(_), Some(_)) => fp_region(rect, hwnd).map(Some),
    }
}


#[derive(Subcommand, Debug)]
pub enum JobCmd {
    /// Hide the front window by the button named Minimize.
    Hide,
    /// Fill the front window by the button named Maximize.
    Fill,
    /// Restore the front window by the button named Restore.
    Restore,
    /// Move the front restored window by its title bar.
    #[command(name = "title-move")]
    TitleMove,
    /// Drag the one vertical scrollbar thumb.
    #[command(name = "scroll-bar")]
    ScrollBar,
    /// Still click the CheckBox named Oak. Does not click Pine.
    Check {
        name: String,
    },
    /// Still click the RadioButton named Beta. Does not click Alpha.
    Radio {
        name: String,
    },
    /// Still click the Button named Apply. Does not click Ping.
    Apply,
    /// Open the combo and still click one list item.
    Pick {
        name: String,
    },
    /// Open the File menu and still click Mark. Does not click Close or Ping.
    Mark,
    /// Double-click the Button named Ping through settle-double.
    #[command(name = "double")]
    Double {
        name: String,
    },
    /// Click the multiline edit and type through the human path.
    Type {
        name: String,
    },
}

#[derive(Subcommand, Debug)]
pub enum DropdownCmd {
    /// Find ComboBox and click its arrow (raw UIA pixels).
    /// Scoped to `--process` / `--title-match`. Refuses if the result is not exclusive.
    Open {
        #[arg(long, default_value = "")]
        name_contains: String,
        #[arg(long, default_value = "brave")]

        #[arg(long, default_value = "")]
        title_match: String,

    /// List matching ComboBoxes (no click). Same scope as open.
    Probe {
        #[arg(long, default_value = "")]
        name_contains: String,
        #[arg(long, default_value = "brave")]
        process: String,
        #[arg(long, default_value = "")]
        title_match: String,
    },
    /// Select menu/list item by name contains (Brave lasting = forever)
    Select {
        #[arg(long)]
        item: String,
    },
    /// Open â†’ select forever â†’ Allow
    ForeverAllow {
        #[arg(long, default_value = "Remember")]
        name_contains: String,
        #[arg(long, default_value = "brave")]
        process: String,
        #[arg(long, default_value = "")]
        title_match: String,
    },
}

impl Commands {
    /// True for commands that inject pointer/keyboard input (guarded by the human-override hook).
    pub fn injects_input(&self) -> bool {
        match self {
            Commands::Pos
            | Commands::Platform
            | Commands::Winevents { .. }
            | Commands::Hittest { .. }
            | Commands::Shot { .. }
            | Commands::Fp { .. }
            | Commands::Diff { .. }
            | Commands::Integrity { .. } => false,
            Commands::Dropdown { action } => !matches!(action, DropdownCmd::Probe { .. }),
            _ => true,
        }
    }
}

/// Run with the human-override guard around input commands. Returns the process exit code.
/// The hook watch is an RAII guard: it is removed (unhook + thread join) on return, error, or panic.
/// Also arms the pre-action hit-test (--expect-*) and the reference-only WinEvent log (--events).
pub fn run_guarded(cli: Cli) -> i32 {
    use crate::override_guard::{self, HumanWatch, Verdict};
    let expect = match crate::hittest::parse_expect(
        cli.expect_hwnd.as_deref(),
        cli.expect_title.as_deref(),
    ) {
        Ok(e) => e,
        Err(m) => {
            eprintln!("error: {m}");
            return 2;
        }
    };
    let fp_out = cli.fp.clone();
    let fp_reg = match fp_global_region(
        cli.fp.as_deref(),
        cli.settle_shot.as_deref(),
        cli.fp_rect.as_deref(),
        cli.fp_hwnd.as_deref(),
    ) {
        Ok(r) => r,
        Err(m) => {
            eprintln!("error: {m}");
            return 2;
        }
    };
    let trace = cli.trace.clone();
    let events = cli.events.clone();
    crate::hittest::arm(expect, trace.is_some() || events.is_some());
    // Verb name only (never the arguments: `type --text` could carry a secret).
    let what = format!("{:?}", cli.cmd)
        .split([' ', '{', '('])
        .next()
        .unwrap_or("?")
        .to_ascii_lowercase();
    // Settle before capture (order 14): pre-action frame BEFORE any input. Times the PNG only.
    let injects = cli.cmd.injects_input();
    let settle_shot = cli.settle_shot.clone();
    let settle_log = cli.settle_log.clone();
    let settle_pre = match (&settle_shot, injects) {
        (Some(_), true) => match crate::shot::pre_frame(&crate::settle::Params::default()) {
            Ok(s) => Some(s),
            Err(e) => {
                eprintln!("warning: settle pre-frame failed ({e}); phase 1 skipped");
                None
            }
        },
        (Some(_), false) => {
            eprintln!("warning: --settle-shot applies to input verbs only (use `sm shot --out`)");
            None
        }
        _ => None,
    };
    let ev_watch = if events.is_some() && cli.cmd.injects_input() {
        Some(crate::winevents::EventWatch::start())
    } else {
        None
    };
    let watch = if cli.cmd.injects_input() {
        Some(HumanWatch::start(trace.is_some()))
    } else {
        None
    };
    let res = std::panic::catch_unwind(std::panic::AssertUnwindSafe(|| run(cli)));
    let verdict = override_guard::verdict();
    let hook_lost = watch.as_ref().and_then(|w| w.watchdog());
    drop(watch);
    if let Some(w) = &hook_lost {
        eprintln!("warning: {w}");
    }
    if let (Some(png), true, true) = (&settle_shot, injects, res.is_ok()) {
        match crate::shot::settle_and_save_frame(settle_pre.as_ref(), png, settle_log.as_deref(), &what) {
            Ok((line, _, frame)) => {
                println!("{line}");
                // Reference-only fingerprint of the final settled full-res frame.
                if let (Some(out), Some(reg)) = (&fp_out, &fp_reg) {
                    match crate::shot::fingerprint_frame(&frame, reg, None, crate::dhash::DEFAULT_THRESHOLD)
                        .and_then(|fp| crate::shot::write_fp(out, &fp))
                    {
                        Ok(l) => println!("{l}"),
                        Err(e) => eprintln!("warning: fp failed: {e}"),
                    }
                }
            }
            Err(e) => eprintln!("warning: settle-shot failed: {e}"),
        }
    }
    let hit_lines = crate::hittest::lines();
    if let (Some(ev), Some(p)) = (ev_watch, &events) {
        std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(crate::winevents::TAIL_MS));
        let (t0, hooks, recs) = ev.finish();
        if let Err(e) = crate::winevents::write_events(p, &what, t0, hooks, &recs, &hit_lines) {
            eprintln!("warning: events write failed {p}: {e}");
        }
    }
    if let Some(p) = &trace {
        if let Err(e) = override_guard::write_trace(p, verdict, hook_lost.as_deref(), &hit_lines) {
            eprintln!("warning: trace write failed {p}: {e}");
        }
    }
    let res = match res {
        Ok(r) => r,
        Err(panic) => std::panic::resume_unwind(panic),
    };
    let code = exit_code(
        verdict == Verdict::HumanOverride,
        crate::uipi::blocked(),
        crate::hittest::missed(),
        res.is_ok(),
    );
    match code {
        EXIT_HUMAN_OVERRIDE => eprintln!(
            "HUMAN_OVERRIDE human mouse input seen during the command; input stopped, held buttons/keys released (exit {EXIT_HUMAN_OVERRIDE})"
        ),
        EXIT_HIT_MISS => eprintln!("{}", crate::hittest::miss_message()),
        EXIT_UIPI_BLOCKED => eprintln!("{}", crate::uipi::message()),
        1 => {
            if let Err(e) = &res {
                eprintln!("error: {e}");
            }
        }
        _ => {}
    }
    code
}

/// Press point of click: the target x,y after the pre-move, or the current pointer
/// (drag shorthand, --no-move, or x=y=0).
fn click_press_point(x: i32, y: i32, btn: ClickButton, no_move: bool) -> (i32, i32) {
    if btn == ClickButton::Drag || no_move || (x == 0 && y == 0) {
        let p = cursor_pos();
        (p.x, p.y)
    } else {
        (x, y)
    }
}

/// Parse argv without running the command (no SendInput).
#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum CliParse {
    Ok(Cli),
    Help,
    Version,
    Err(String),
}

pub fn parse_cli<I, T>(args: I) -> CliParse
where
    I: IntoIterator<Item = T>,
    T: Into<std::ffi::OsString> + Clone,
{
    match Cli::try_parse_from(args) {
        Ok(cli) => CliParse::Ok(cli),
        Err(e) => match e.kind() {
            clap::error::ErrorKind::DisplayHelp
            | clap::error::ErrorKind::DisplayHelpOnMissingArgumentOrSubcommand => CliParse::Help,
            clap::error::ErrorKind::DisplayVersion => CliParse::Version,
            _ => CliParse::Err(e.to_string()),
        },
    }
}


fn parse_mod(s: &str) -> Result<u16, String> {
    match s.to_ascii_lowercase().as_str() {
        "ctrl" | "control" => Ok(0x11),
        "shift" => Ok(0x10),
        other => Err(format!("unknown --mod {other}, use ctrl or shift")),
    }
}

pub fn run(cli: Cli) -> Result<(), String> {
    let expect_hwnd = cli.expect_hwnd.clone();
    match cli.cmd {
        Commands::Pos => {
            let p = cursor_pos();
            println!("x={} y={}", p.x, p.y);
            Ok(())
        }
        Commands::Humove {
            x,
            y,
            algo,
            profile,
            steps,
            step_ms,
        } => {
            humove(x, y, Algo::parse(&algo), Profile::parse(&profile), steps, step_ms);
            let p = cursor_pos();
            println!("humove done x={} y={} algo={} profile={}", p.x, p.y, algo, profile);
            Ok(())
        }
        Commands::Click {
            x,
            y,
            button,
            algo,
            profile,
            hover_ms,
            no_move,
            modifier,
        } => {
            let btn = ClickButton::parse(&button);
            let vk = match modifier.as_deref() {
                None => None,
                Some(s) => Some(parse_mod(s)?),
            };
            // Hit-test before ANY input (no move, no modifier) when --expect-* is given.
            let (px, py) = click_press_point(x, y, btn, no_move);
            crate::hittest::precheck(px, py)?;
            let msg = click_mod(
                x,
                y,
                btn,
                Algo::parse(&algo),
                Profile::parse(&profile),
                hover_ms,
                no_move,
                vk,
            );
            if crate::hittest::missed() {
                return Err(crate::hittest::miss_message());
            }
            if msg.starts_with("settle-double FAIL") || msg.starts_with("drag FAIL") {
                println!("{msg}");
                return Err(msg);
            }
            println!("{msg}");
            Ok(())
        }
        Commands::Drag {
            x1,
            y1,
            x2,
            y2,
            algo,
            profile,
        } => {
            // Hit-test the drag start before ANY input when --expect-* is given.
            crate::hittest::precheck(x1, y1)?;
            let r = drag_from_to(
                x1,
                y1,
                x2,
                y2,
                Algo::parse(&algo),
                Profile::parse(&profile),
            );
            if crate::hittest::missed() {
                return Err(crate::hittest::miss_message());
            }
            let msg = r?;
            println!("{msg}");
            Ok(())
        }
        Commands::Scroll { notches } => {
            scroll_notches(notches);
            println!("scrolled notches={notches}");
            Ok(())
        }
        Commands::Idle { ms } => {
            idle_fidget(ms);
            println!("idle done ms={ms}");
            Ok(())
        }
        Commands::Type {
            text,
            pace,
            typo_chance,
            no_typos,
        } => {
            let msg = type_human(&text, Pace::parse(&pace), typo_chance, no_typos);
            println!("{msg}");
            Ok(())
        }
        Commands::ClickName { contains, hover_ms } => {
            let hwnd = match cli.expect_hwnd.as_deref() {
                Some(s) => Some(crate::hittest::parse_hwnd(s)?),
                None => None,
            };
            // Resolve under hwnd/foreground first; hit-test before any motor input.
            let hit = resolve_click_name(&contains, hwnd)?;
            let (cx, cy) = name_hit_center(&hit);
            crate::hittest::precheck(cx, cy)?;
            let motor = click_at(
                cx,
                cy,
                ClickButton::Left,
                Algo::Gust,
                Profile::Average,
                hover_ms,
                false,
            );
            if crate::hittest::missed() {
                return Err(crate::hittest::miss_message());
            }
            println!(
                "click-name: '{}' type={} at {cx},{cy} process='{}' window='{}' rect={},{} {}x{}",
                hit.name, hit.control_type, hit.process, hit.window_title,
                hit.x, hit.y, hit.width, hit.height
            );
            println!("{motor}");
            Ok(())
        }
        Commands::Dropdown { action } => match action {
            DropdownCmd::Open {
                name_contains,
                process,
                title_match,
            } => {
                let opts = DropdownOpts {
                    process_name: process,
                    window_title_match: title_match,
                    combo_name_contains: name_contains,
                    ..DropdownOpts::default()
                };
                let msg = dropdown_open(&opts)?;
                println!("{msg}");
                Ok(())
            }
            DropdownCmd::Probe {
                name_contains,
                process,
                title_match,
            } => {
                let opts = DropdownOpts {
                    process_name: process,
                    window_title_match: title_match,
                    combo_name_contains: name_contains,
                    ..DropdownOpts::default()
                };
                let msg = dropdown_probe(&opts)?;
                println!("{msg}");
                Ok(())
            }
            DropdownCmd::Select { item } => {
                let msg = dropdown_select(&item, &DropdownOpts::default())?;
                println!("{msg}");
                Ok(())
            }
            DropdownCmd::ForeverAllow {
                name_contains,
                process,
                title_match,
            } => {
                let opts = DropdownOpts {
                    process_name: process,
                    window_title_match: title_match,
                    combo_name_contains: name_contains,
                    ..DropdownOpts::default()
                };
                let msg = dropdown_forever_allow(&opts)?;
                println!("{msg}");
                Ok(())
            }
        },
        Commands::Job { action } => match action {
            JobCmd::Hide => {
                let msg = crate::job::hide()?;
                println!("{msg}");
                Ok(())
            }
            JobCmd::Fill => {
                let msg = crate::job::fill()?;
                println!("{msg}");
                match crate::job::restore_down() {
                    Ok(s) => println!("{s}"),
                    Err(e) => println!("restore left as is: {e}"),
                }
                Ok(())
            }
            JobCmd::Restore => {
                let msg = crate::job::restore()?;
                println!("{msg}");
                Ok(())
            }
            JobCmd::TitleMove => {
                let msg = crate::job::title_move()?;
                println!("{msg}");
                Ok(())
            }
            JobCmd::ScrollBar => {
                let msg = crate::job::scroll_bar()?;
                println!("{msg}");
                Ok(())
            }
            JobCmd::Check { name } => {
                if name != "Oak" {
                    return Err(format!("only Oak, not clicked name={name}"));
                }
                let msg = crate::job::check_oak()?;
                println!("{msg}");
                Ok(())
            }
            JobCmd::Radio { name } => {
                if name != "Beta" {
                    return Err(format!("only Beta, not clicked name={name}"));
                }
                let msg = crate::job::radio_beta()?;
                println!("{msg}");
                Ok(())
            }
            JobCmd::Apply => {
                let msg = crate::job::apply()?;
                println!("{msg}");
                Ok(())
            }
            JobCmd::Pick { name } => {
                if name != "Green" {
                    return Err(format!("only Green, not clicked name={name}"));
                }
                let msg = crate::job::pick_green()?;
                println!("{msg}");
                Ok(())
            }
            JobCmd::Mark => {
                let msg = crate::job::mark()?;
                println!("{msg}");
                Ok(())
            }
            JobCmd::Double { name } => {
                if name != "Ping" {
                    return Err(format!("only Ping, not clicked name={name}"));
                }
                let msg = crate::job::double_ping()?;
                println!("{msg}");
                Ok(())
            }
            JobCmd::Type { name } => {
                if name != "Oak" {
                    return Err("only Oak, not typed".into());
                }
                let msg = crate::job::type_oak()?;
                println!("{msg}");
                Ok(())
            }
        },
        Commands::Platform => {
            println!("{}", crate::platform::current_name());
            Ok(())
        }
        Commands::Key {
            hold,
            tap,
            taps,
            dry_run,
        } => {
            let msg = crate::key_chord::key_chord(hold.as_deref(), &tap, taps, dry_run)?;
            println!("{msg}");
            Ok(())
        }
        Commands::Winevents { ms, out } => {
            let msg = crate::winevents::listen(ms, &out)?;
            println!("{msg}");
            Ok(())
        }
        Commands::Hittest { x, y } => {
            // Verb result on stdout only. A mismatch is marked silently; run_guarded prints
            // the one HIT_MISS line on stderr and exits 8 (no duplicate line).
            let h = crate::hittest::probe(x, y);
            let e = crate::hittest::expect();
            let (line, miss) = crate::hittest::verb_result(&h, &e);
            println!("{line}");
            if miss {
                crate::hittest::mark_miss("probe", h);
            }
            Ok(())
        }
        Commands::Fp {
            out,
            rect,
            hwnd,
            compare,
            threshold,
        } => {
            let reg = fp_region(rect.as_deref(), hwnd.as_deref())?;
            if reg == crate::dhash::Region::Screen {
                eprintln!("note: whole-screen fp is layout-only (a 10%-of-screen minimize moved it by 4); use --rect or --hwnd for state checks");
            }
            let msg = crate::shot::fp_verb(&out, &reg, compare.as_deref(), threshold)?;
            println!("{msg}");
            Ok(())
        }
        Commands::Diff {
            before,
            after,
            expect_rect,
            ignore_cells,
            tol,
            outside_max,
            cell,
            out,
            viz,
        } => {
            let msg = diff_verb(&DiffArgs {
                before,
                after,
                expect_rect,
                expect_hwnd,
                ignore_cells,
                tol,
                outside_max,
                cell,
                out,
                viz,
            })?;
            println!("{msg}");
            Ok(())
        }
        Commands::Integrity { hwnd, x, y } => {
            let h = match (hwnd, x, y) {
                (Some(h), _, _) => crate::hittest::parse_hwnd(&h)
                    .map_err(|e| e.replace("--expect-hwnd", "--hwnd"))?,
                (None, Some(x), Some(y)) => crate::uipi::root_at(x, y),
                _ => return Err("integrity: give --hwnd N or --x X --y Y".into()),
            };
            println!("{}", crate::uipi::verb_line(&crate::uipi::probe_hwnd(h)));
            Ok(())
        }
        Commands::Coordcheck { points, dwell_ms } => {

            let pts = crate::coords::parse_points(&points)?;
            let msg = crate::coords::coordcheck(&pts, dwell_ms.clamp(10, 2000))?;
            println!("{msg}");
            Ok(())
        }
        Commands::Shot { out, settle } => {
            let msg = if settle {
                crate::shot::settle_and_save(None, &out, cli.settle_log.as_deref(), "shot")?.0
            } else {
                crate::shot::plain_shot(&out)?
            };
            println!("{msg}");
            Ok(())
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn parse(args: &[&str]) -> Result<Cli, clap::error::Error> {
        Cli::try_parse_from(args)
    }

    #[test]
    fn scroll_notches_space_negative() {
        let cli = parse(&["sm", "scroll", "--notches", "-2"]).expect("space form");
        match cli.cmd {
            Commands::Scroll { notches } => assert_eq!(notches, -2),
            other => panic!("expected Scroll, got {other:?}"),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn scroll_notches_equals_negative_clap_gotcha() {
        // clap treats values starting with '-' as flags unless allow_hyphen_values /
        // allow_negative_numbers is set. Equals-attached `--notches=-2` is the gotcha form.
        let cli = parse(&["sm", "scroll", "--notches=-2"]).expect("equals form --notches=-2");
        match cli.cmd {
            Commands::Scroll { notches } => assert_eq!(notches, -2),
            other => panic!("expected Scroll, got {other:?}"),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn scroll_notches_zero_and_pos() {
        let z = parse(&["sm", "scroll", "--notches", "0"]).unwrap();
        let p = parse(&["sm", "scroll", "--notches=2"]).unwrap();
        match (z.cmd, p.cmd) {
            (Commands::Scroll { notches: a }, Commands::Scroll { notches: b }) => {
                assert_eq!(a, 0);
                assert_eq!(b, 2);
            }
            _ => panic!("expected two Scroll commands"),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn unknown_subcommand_errors() {
        assert!(parse(&["sm", "nosuch"]).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn humove_required_xy() {
        assert!(parse(&["sm", "humove"]).is_err());
        let cli = parse(&["sm", "humove", "--x", "400", "--y", "300", "--algo", "wind"]).unwrap();
        match cli.cmd {
            Commands::Humove { x, y, algo, .. } => {
                assert_eq!((x, y), (400, 300));
                assert_eq!(algo, "wind");
            }
            other => panic!("expected Humove, got {other:?}"),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn dropdown_probe_fixture_scope_parses() {
        let cli = parse(&[
            "sm",
            "dropdown",
            "probe",
            "--name-contains",
            "SM-Remember",
            "--process",
            "powershell",
            "--title-match",
            "SM-REG-FIXTURE",
        ])
        .unwrap();
        match cli.cmd {
            Commands::Dropdown {
                action:
                    DropdownCmd::Probe {
                        name_contains,
                        process,
                        title_match,
                    },
            } => {
                assert_eq!(name_contains, "SM-Remember");
                assert_eq!(process, "powershell");
                assert_eq!(title_match, "SM-REG-FIXTURE");
            }
            other => panic!("expected Probe, got {other:?}"),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn key_chord_parses_default_one_tap() {
        assert!(parse(&["sm", "key", "--hold", "alt"]).is_err());
        let cli = parse(&["sm", "key", "--hold", "alt", "--tap", "tab"]).unwrap();
        match cli.cmd {
            Commands::Key {
                hold,
                tap,
                taps,
                dry_run,
            } => {
                assert_eq!(
                    (hold.as_deref(), tap.as_str(), taps, dry_run),
                    (Some("alt"), "tab", 1, false)
                );
            }
            other => panic!("expected Key, got {other:?}"),
        }
        let cli = parse(&["sm", "key", "--hold", "ctrl", "--tap", "tab", "--taps", "2", "--dry-run"])
            .unwrap();
        match cli.cmd {
            Commands::Key { taps, dry_run, .. } => assert_eq!((taps, dry_run), (2, true)),
            other => panic!("expected Key, got {other:?}"),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn key_bare_tap_parses() {
        assert!(parse(&["sm", "key"]).is_err());
        let cli = parse(&["sm", "key", "--tap", "esc"]).unwrap();
        match cli.cmd {
            Commands::Key { hold, tap, taps, .. } => {
                assert_eq!((hold, tap.as_str(), taps), (None, "esc", 1));
            }
            other => panic!("expected Key, got {other:?}"),
        }
        let cli = parse(&["sm", "key", "--hold", "none", "--tap", "esc"]).unwrap();
        match cli.cmd {
            Commands::Key { hold, .. } => assert_eq!(hold.as_deref(), Some("none")),
            other => panic!("expected Key, got {other:?}"),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn trace_is_global_and_optional() {
        let cli = parse(&["sm", "humove", "--x", "1", "--y", "2"]).unwrap();
        assert!(cli.trace.is_none());
        let cli = parse(&["sm", "humove", "--x", "1", "--y", "2", "--trace", "t.txt"]).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(cli.trace.as_deref(), Some("t.txt"));
        let cli = parse(&["sm", "--trace", "u.txt", "click"]).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(cli.trace.as_deref(), Some("u.txt"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn input_commands_are_guarded() {
        assert!(parse(&["sm", "humove", "--x", "1", "--y", "2"]).unwrap().cmd.injects_input());
        assert!(parse(&["sm", "click"]).unwrap().cmd.injects_input());
        assert!(parse(&["sm", "key", "--tap", "esc"]).unwrap().cmd.injects_input());
        assert!(!parse(&["sm", "pos"]).unwrap().cmd.injects_input());
        assert!(!parse(&["sm", "platform"]).unwrap().cmd.injects_input());
        assert!(!parse(&["sm", "dropdown", "probe"]).unwrap().cmd.injects_input());
        assert_ne!(EXIT_HUMAN_OVERRIDE, 0);
        assert_ne!(EXIT_HUMAN_OVERRIDE, 1);
        assert_ne!(EXIT_HUMAN_OVERRIDE, 2);
    }

    #[test]
    fn exit_code_mapping_7_and_8() {
        assert_eq!(EXIT_HUMAN_OVERRIDE, 7);
        assert_eq!(EXIT_HIT_MISS, 8);
        // exit_code(human, uipi, hit, ok)
        assert_eq!(exit_code(false, false, false, true), 0);
        assert_eq!(exit_code(false, false, false, false), 1);
        assert_eq!(exit_code(true, false, false, true), 7);
        assert_eq!(exit_code(false, false, true, false), 8);
        assert_eq!(exit_code(false, false, true, true), 8);
        // a human touch outranks a hit miss
        assert_eq!(exit_code(true, false, true, false), 7);
    }

    #[test]
    fn exit_code_mapping_9_uipi_priority() {
        assert_eq!(EXIT_UIPI_BLOCKED, 9);
        // human > uipi > hit
        assert_eq!(exit_code(true, true, true, false), EXIT_HUMAN_OVERRIDE);
        assert_eq!(exit_code(false, true, true, false), EXIT_UIPI_BLOCKED);
        assert_eq!(exit_code(false, true, false, true), EXIT_UIPI_BLOCKED);
        assert_eq!(exit_code(false, false, true, true), EXIT_HIT_MISS);
    }

    #[test]
    fn events_expect_flags_and_new_verbs_parse() {
        let cli = parse(&[
            "sm", "drag", "--x1", "577", "--y1", "635", "--x2", "697", "--y2", "635",
            "--expect-hwnd", "393774", "--events", "e.txt",
        ])
        .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(cli.expect_hwnd.as_deref(), Some("393774"));
        assert_eq!(cli.events.as_deref(), Some("e.txt"));
        assert!(cli.expect_title.is_none());
        let cli = parse(&["sm", "click", "--x", "1", "--y", "2", "--expect-title", "SM-LIVE-A"]).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(cli.expect_title.as_deref(), Some("SM-LIVE-A"));
        let cli = parse(&["sm", "winevents", "--ms", "500", "--out", "w.txt"]).unwrap();
        assert!(!cli.cmd.injects_input());
        match cli.cmd {
            Commands::Winevents { ms, out } => assert_eq!((ms, out.as_str()), (500, "w.txt")),
            other => panic!("expected Winevents, got {other:?}"),
        }
        let cli = parse(&["sm", "hittest", "--x", "5", "--y", "6"]).unwrap();
        assert!(!cli.cmd.injects_input());
        assert!(parse(&["sm", "winevents", "--ms", "5"]).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn shot_and_settle_flags_parse() {
        let cli = parse(&["sm", "shot", "--out", "a.png"]).unwrap();
        assert!(!cli.cmd.injects_input());
        match &cli.cmd {
            Commands::Shot { out, settle } => assert_eq!((out.as_str(), *settle), ("a.png", false)),
            other => panic!("expected Shot, got {other:?}"),
        }
        let cli = parse(&["sm", "shot", "--out", "b.png", "--settle", "--settle-log", "b.txt"]).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(cli.settle_log.as_deref(), Some("b.txt"));
        assert!(matches!(cli.cmd, Commands::Shot { settle: true, .. }));
        assert!(parse(&["sm", "shot"]).is_err());
        let cli = parse(&[
            "sm", "click", "--x", "459", "--y", "444", "--expect-hwnd", "393774",
            "--settle-shot", "after.png",
        ])
        .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(cli.settle_shot.as_deref(), Some("after.png"));
        assert!(cli.settle_log.is_none());
        assert!(cli.cmd.injects_input());
    }

    #[test]
    fn fp_verb_and_global_flags() {
        use crate::dhash::Region;
        let cli = parse(&["sm", "fp", "--out", "a.json"]).unwrap();
        assert!(!cli.cmd.injects_input());
        match &cli.cmd {
            Commands::Fp { out, rect, hwnd, compare, threshold } => {
                assert_eq!((out.as_str(), rect.is_none(), hwnd.is_none(), compare.is_none(), *threshold), ("a.json", true, true, true, 10));
            }
            other => panic!("expected Fp, got {other:?}"),
        }
        let cli = parse(&["sm", "fp", "--out", "b.json", "--hwnd", "393774", "--compare", "a.json", "--threshold", "6"]).unwrap();
        assert!(matches!(cli.cmd, Commands::Fp { threshold: 6, .. }));
        assert!(parse(&["sm", "fp", "--out", "c.json", "--rect", "1,2,30,40", "--hwnd", "5"]).is_err());
        assert!(parse(&["sm", "fp", "--out", "c.json", "--rect", "-10,2,30,40"]).is_ok());
        assert_eq!(fp_region(Some("48,429,574,842"), None), Ok(Region::Rect(48, 429, 574, 842)));
        assert_eq!(fp_region(None, Some("393774")), Ok(Region::Hwnd(393774)));
        assert_eq!(fp_region(None, None), Ok(Region::Screen));
        // global --fp: only with --settle-shot (else usage error, exit 2)
        assert!(fp_global_region(Some("f.json"), None, None, None).is_err());
        assert!(fp_global_region(None, Some("a.png"), Some("1,2,30,40"), None).is_err());
        assert!(fp_global_region(Some("f.json"), Some("a.png"), Some("1,2,30,40"), Some("5")).is_err());
        assert_eq!(fp_global_region(None, Some("a.png"), None, None), Ok(None));
        assert_eq!(fp_global_region(Some("f.json"), Some("a.png"), None, Some("393774")), Ok(Some(Region::Hwnd(393774))));
        let cli = parse(&["sm", "coordcheck"]).unwrap();
        assert!(cli.cmd.injects_input()); // moves the pointer: guarded by the human-override hook
        assert!(matches!(cli.cmd, Commands::Coordcheck { dwell_ms: 80, .. }));
        let cli = parse(&["sm", "click", "--x", "1", "--y", "2", "--settle-shot", "a.png", "--fp", "a.json", "--fp-hwnd", "393774"]).unwrap();
        assert_eq!((cli.fp.as_deref(), cli.fp_hwnd.as_deref()), (Some("a.json"), Some("393774")));
    }

    #[test]
    fn drag_required_xy_pair() {
        assert!(parse(&["sm", "drag"]).is_err());
        assert!(parse(&["sm", "drag", "--x1", "100", "--y1", "100"]).is_err());
        let cli = parse(&[
            "sm",
            "drag",
            "--x1",
            "100",
            "--y1",
            "100",
            "--x2",
            "180",
            "--y2",
            "100",
            "--algo",
            "wind",
        ])
        .unwrap();
        match cli.cmd {
            Commands::Drag {
                x1,
                y1,
                x2,
                y2,
                algo,
                ..
            } => {
                assert_eq!((x1, y1, x2, y2), (100, 100, 180, 100));
                assert_eq!(algo, "wind");
            }
            other => panic!("expected Drag, got {other:?}"),
        }
    }


    #[test]
    fn diff_and_integrity_parse() {
        let cli = parse(&[
            "sm",
            "--expect-hwnd",
            "393774",
            "diff",
            "--before",
            "a.png",
            "--after",
            "b.png",
            "--expect-rect",
            "48,429,574,842",
            "--out",
            "d.json",
        ])
        .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(cli.expect_hwnd.as_deref(), Some("393774"));
        match cli.cmd {
            Commands::Diff { ref before, ref after, ref expect_rect, ref out, .. } => {
                assert_eq!(before, "a.png");
                assert_eq!(after, "b.png");
                assert_eq!(expect_rect.as_slice(), &["48,429,574,842".to_string()]);
                assert_eq!(out, "d.json");
            }
            other => panic!("expected Diff, got {other:?}"),
        }
        assert!(!cli.cmd.injects_input());
        let cli2 = parse(&["sm", "integrity", "--hwnd", "393774"]).unwrap();
        assert!(matches!(cli2.cmd, Commands::Integrity { .. }));
        assert!(!cli2.cmd.injects_input());
    }

    #[test]
    fn click_name_parses_contains_and_expect_hwnd() {

        assert_eq!(cli.settle_shot.as_deref(), Some("after.png"));

                assert_eq!(contains, "Login with email");
                assert_eq!(*hover_ms, 280);
            }
            other => panic!("expected ClickName, got {other:?}"),
        }
        assert!(cli.cmd.injects_input());
    }

===== FILE: src\click.rs =====
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
//! Click helpers (move then hybrid click).

use crate::native::{cursor_pos, ensure_dpi, set_cursor_pos, sleep_ms};
use crate::path::{humove, Algo, Profile};
use crate::platform::windows::Windows;
use crate::platform::{Keys, Pointer};

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum ClickButton {
    Left,
    Right,
    Double,
    Drag,
}

impl ClickButton {
    pub fn parse(s: &str) -> Self {
        match s.to_ascii_lowercase().as_str() {
            "right" | "r" => ClickButton::Right,
            "double" | "dbl" => ClickButton::Double,
            "drag" => ClickButton::Drag,
            _ => ClickButton::Left,
        }
    }
}

/// RAII release for a held key. Drop sends key-up. Shared by `click --mod` and `key`.
pub(crate) struct ModUp(pub(crate) u16);

impl Drop for ModUp {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        // Always release, including when the click path returns an error string.
        let _ = Windows.key_up(self.0);
    }
}

/// Same settle sequence as the old hybrid left click. Presses go through Windows.
fn left_press(x: i32, y: i32) {
    ensure_dpi();
    set_cursor_pos(x, y);
    sleep_ms(35);
    let _ = Windows.move_to(x, y);
    sleep_ms(20);
    set_cursor_pos(x, y);
    sleep_ms(20);
    let _ = Windows.left_down();
    sleep_ms(45);
    let _ = Windows.left_up();
}

pub fn click_at(
    x: i32,
    y: i32,
    button: ClickButton,
    algo: Algo,
    profile: Profile,
    hover_ms: u64,
    no_move: bool,
) -> String {
    click_mod(x, y, button, algo, profile, hover_ms, no_move, None)
}

/// `modifier` is a virtual key, or None for today's click. Key down, click, key up.
pub fn click_mod(
    x: i32,
    y: i32,
    button: ClickButton,
    algo: Algo,
    profile: Profile,
    hover_ms: u64,
    no_move: bool,
    modifier: Option<u16>,
) -> String {
    let _up = modifier.map(|vk| {
        let _ = Windows.key_down(vk);
        sleep_ms(40);
        ModUp(vk)
    });
    // Drag holds first, then moves. A pre-move would release the lesson (hold, move, release).
    if button != ClickButton::Drag && !no_move && (x != 0 || y != 0) {
        humove(x, y, algo, profile, 0, 0);
        if hover_ms > 0 {
            sleep_ms(hover_ms);
        }
    } else if button != ClickButton::Drag && hover_ms > 0 {
        sleep_ms(hover_ms);
    }
    if crate::override_guard::aborted() {
        // Held modifier (if any) is released by ModUp on return.
        return "HUMAN_OVERRIDE before press, no click sent".to_string();
    }
    let p = cursor_pos();
    match button {
        ClickButton::Right => {
            let _ = Windows.right_click(p.x, p.y);
            let p = cursor_pos();
            format!("rclicked x={} y={}", p.x, p.y)
        }
        ClickButton::Double => {
            // Existing command. settle-double adds a still pointer and a sub-500 ms gap.
            // Do not add a second double-click command.
            let _ = p;
            match crate::settle_double::settle_double() {
                Ok(msg) => msg,
                Err(msg) => msg,
            }
        }
        ClickButton::Left => {
            left_press(p.x, p.y);
            let p = cursor_pos();
            format!("clicked x={} y={}", p.x, p.y)
        }
        ClickButton::Drag => {
            // From-current shorthand. Explicit start->end is `sm drag` / drag_from_to.
            match crate::drag_drop::drag_hold_move_release(x, y, algo, profile) {
                Ok(msg) => msg,
                Err(msg) => msg,
            }
        }
    }
}


===== FILE: src\coords.rs =====
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
//! Coordinate mapping check (order F). Reference / read-only: `sm coordcheck` moves only
//! sm's own pointer (no clicks) and compares with GetCursorPos.
//!
//! sm's forward mapping for MOUSEEVENTF_ABSOLUTE (primary monitor, `native::send_abs_move`),
//! exact since order 17b:
//!   n = ((p - origin) * 65536 + size - 1) / size   (integer ceil division, i64; capped 65535)
//! (before 17b: n = (p - origin) * 65535 / (size - 1), which landed 1 px short at some small
//! coordinates). The same formula applies to MOUSEEVENTF_VIRTUALDESK with the virtual-screen
//! origin/size. Live 15-coordcheck showed Windows inverts with the floor model
//!   p = origin + floor(n * size / 65536)
//! under which the ceil formula round-trips with 0 px error at every pixel (unit-tested
//! exhaustively). Humove itself uses SetCursorPos (exact pixels); the absolute path is used
//! between two SetCursorPos calls in `click_hybrid`.

/// sm's forward mapping (screen px -> normalized 0..65535), exact under Windows' floor inverse.
pub fn to_abs(p: i32, origin: i32, size: i32) -> i32 {
    let size = size.max(1) as i64;
    let d = ((p - origin) as i64).clamp(0, size - 1);
    ((d * 65536 + size - 1) / size).min(65535) as i32
}

/// The pre-17b mapping, kept for the regression test only.
pub fn to_abs_old(p: i32, origin: i32, size: i32) -> i32 {
    (((p - origin) as i64) * 65535 / (size.max(2) as i64 - 1)) as i32
}

/// Inverse model "round".
pub fn from_abs_round(n: i32, origin: i32, size: i32) -> i32 {
    let d = 65535i64;
    origin + ((n as i64 * (size.max(2) as i64 - 1) + d / 2) / d) as i32
}

/// Inverse model "floor".
pub fn from_abs_floor(n: i32, origin: i32, size: i32) -> i32 {
    origin + ((n as i64 * size.max(2) as i64) >> 16) as i32
}

/// Default live points for a 1920x1080 primary screen: corners, mid-edges, center, odd points.
pub const DEFAULT_POINTS: &str =
    "960,540;0,0;960,0;1919,0;1919,540;1919,1079;960,1079;0,1079;0,540;333,777;1,1;2,2;3,3;8,8;10,10;12,12;13,13;1918,1078;960,540";

pub fn parse_points(s: &str) -> Result<Vec<(i32, i32)>, String> {
    let mut v = Vec::new();
    for part in s.split(';').map(str::trim).filter(|p| !p.is_empty()) {
        let xy: Vec<&str> = part.split(',').map(str::trim).collect();
        if xy.len() != 2 {
            return Err(format!("bad point '{part}' (want x,y;x,y)"));
        }
        let x = xy[0].parse().map_err(|_| format!("bad point '{part}'"))?;
        let y = xy[1].parse().map_err(|_| format!("bad point '{part}'"))?;
        v.push((x, y));
    }
    if v.is_empty() || v.len() > 64 {
        return Err("need 1..64 points".into());
    }
    Ok(v)
}

#[cfg(all(windows, feature = "win32"))]
fn dpi_report() -> String {
    use windows::Win32::Foundation::HANDLE;
    use windows::Win32::UI::HiDpi::{
        GetAwarenessFromDpiAwarenessContext, GetDpiForSystem, GetProcessDpiAwareness,
        GetThreadDpiAwarenessContext,
    };
    use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::IsProcessDPIAware;
    crate::native::ensure_dpi();
    unsafe {
        let thread = GetAwarenessFromDpiAwarenessContext(GetThreadDpiAwarenessContext()).0;
        let proc_ = GetProcessDpiAwareness(HANDLE::default())
            .map(|a| a.0.to_string())
            .unwrap_or_else(|e| format!("err {e}"));
        format!(
            "dpi thread_awareness={thread} (0 unaware, 1 system, 2 per-monitor) process_awareness={proc_} IsProcessDPIAware={} system_dpi={} (sm sets SetThreadDpiAwarenessContext(PER_MONITOR_AWARE_V2) per thread in native::ensure_dpi)",
            IsProcessDPIAware().as_bool(),
            GetDpiForSystem()
        )
    }
}

#[cfg(all(windows, feature = "win32"))]
fn screen_report() -> (String, i32, i32) {
    use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::{
        GetSystemMetrics, SM_CMONITORS, SM_CXSCREEN, SM_CXVIRTUALSCREEN, SM_CYSCREEN,
        SM_CYVIRTUALSCREEN, SM_XVIRTUALSCREEN, SM_YVIRTUALSCREEN,
    };
    unsafe {
        let (w, h) = (GetSystemMetrics(SM_CXSCREEN), GetSystemMetrics(SM_CYSCREEN));
        (
            format!(
                "screen primary={}x{} virtual=({},{}) {}x{} monitors={}",
                w,
                h,
                GetSystemMetrics(SM_XVIRTUALSCREEN),
                GetSystemMetrics(SM_YVIRTUALSCREEN),
                GetSystemMetrics(SM_CXVIRTUALSCREEN),
                GetSystemMetrics(SM_CYVIRTUALSCREEN),
                GetSystemMetrics(SM_CMONITORS)
            ),
            w,
            h,
        )
    }
}

/// Live check: for each point, (a) abs move only (MOUSEEVENTF_ABSOLUTE, sm's formula) then
/// GetCursorPos, (b) SetCursorPos then GetCursorPos. No clicks. Pointer returns to its start.
#[cfg(all(windows, feature = "win32"))]
pub fn coordcheck(points: &[(i32, i32)], dwell_ms: u64) -> Result<String, String> {
    use crate::native::{cursor_pos, send_abs_move, set_cursor_pos, sleep_ms};
    let (scr, w, h) = screen_report();
    let mut out = vec![
        "# sm coordcheck (order F): pointer moves only, no clicks; judge = GetCursorPos".to_string(),
        scr,
        dpi_report(),
        "target | abs n | abs-move -> GetCursorPos | d | SetCursorPos -> GetCursorPos | d".to_string(),
    ];
    let start = cursor_pos();
    let (mut worst_abs, mut worst_set) = (0i32, 0i32);
    for &(x, y) in points {
        if crate::override_guard::aborted() {
            out.push("stopped: HUMAN_OVERRIDE".into());
            break;
        }
        let (nx, ny) = (to_abs(x, 0, w), to_abs(y, 0, h));
        send_abs_move(x, y);
        sleep_ms(dwell_ms);
        let a = cursor_pos();
        set_cursor_pos(x, y);
        sleep_ms(dwell_ms);
        let s = cursor_pos();
        let (dax, day, dsx, dsy) = (a.x - x, a.y - y, s.x - x, s.y - y);
        worst_abs = worst_abs.max(dax.abs()).max(day.abs());
        worst_set = worst_set.max(dsx.abs()).max(dsy.abs());
        out.push(format!(
            "({x},{y}) | ({nx},{ny}) | ({},{}) | ({dax:+},{day:+}) | ({},{}) | ({dsx:+},{dsy:+})",
            a.x, a.y, s.x, s.y
        ));
    }
    set_cursor_pos(start.x, start.y);
    out.push(format!(
        "worst abs-move error={worst_abs} px, worst SetCursorPos error={worst_set} px; pointer returned to ({},{})",
        start.x, start.y
    ));
    Ok(out.join("\n"))
}

#[cfg(not(all(windows, feature = "win32")))]
pub fn coordcheck(_points: &[(i32, i32)], _dwell_ms: u64) -> Result<String, String> {
    Err("coordcheck needs Windows".into())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn worst(origin: i32, size: i32, pts: &[i32]) -> (i32, i32) {
        let (mut r, mut f) = (0, 0);
        for &p in pts {
            let n = to_abs(p, origin, size);
            assert!((0..=65535).contains(&n), "p={p} n={n}");
            r = r.max((from_abs_round(n, origin, size) - p).abs());
            f = f.max((from_abs_floor(n, origin, size) - p).abs());
        }
        (r, f)
    }

    #[test]
    fn exact_every_pixel_under_floor_inverse_1920x1080() {
        assert_eq!(to_abs(0, 0, 1920), 0);
        assert_eq!(to_abs(1, 0, 1920), 35); // old formula gave 34 -> Windows put it at x=0
        assert_eq!(to_abs(1919, 0, 1920), 65502);
        assert_eq!(to_abs(1079, 0, 1080), 65476);
        let all_x: Vec<i32> = (0..1920).collect();
        let all_y: Vec<i32> = (0..1080).collect();
        let (rx, fx) = worst(0, 1920, &all_x);
        let (ry, fy) = worst(0, 1080, &all_y);
        assert_eq!((fx, fy), (0, 0), "floor model (Windows, live 15-coordcheck) must be exact");
        assert!(rx <= 1 && ry <= 1, "round model within 1 px: {rx} {ry}");
        // the points that were -1 live in 15-coordcheck are now exact
        for p in [1, 2, 3, 8, 10, 12, 13] {
            assert_eq!(from_abs_floor(to_abs(p, 0, 1920), 0, 1920), p);
            assert_eq!(from_abs_floor(to_abs_old(p, 0, 1920), 0, 1920), p - 1, "old x {p}");
        }
        for p in [1, 2, 3, 8, 12] {
            assert_eq!(from_abs_floor(to_abs(p, 0, 1080), 0, 1080), p);
        }
        // old formula: 27 columns / 9 rows off by one (matches live)
        let old_bad_x = (0..1920).filter(|&p| from_abs_floor(to_abs_old(p, 0, 1920), 0, 1920) != p).count();
        let old_bad_y = (0..1080).filter(|&p| from_abs_floor(to_abs_old(p, 0, 1080), 0, 1080) != p).count();
        assert_eq!((old_bad_x, old_bad_y), (27, 9));
    }

    #[test]
    fn virtual_desk_offset_multi_monitor_exact() {
        // left 1920x1080 at (-1920,0) + primary 1920x1080 at (0,0): virtual (-1920,0) 3840x1080
        let pts: Vec<i32> = (-1920..1920).collect();
        let (r, f) = worst(-1920, 3840, &pts);
        assert_eq!(f, 0);
        assert!(r <= 1, "{r}");
        assert_eq!(to_abs(-1920, -1920, 3840), 0);
        // stacked 2560x1440 above: virtual y (-1440) .. 1080, height 2520
        let ys: Vec<i32> = (-1440..1080).collect();
        let (r, f) = worst(-1440, 2520, &ys);
        assert_eq!(f, 0);
        assert!(r <= 1, "{r}");
        // odd sizes (e.g. 1366, 1600x900 at 125%, 3000) also exact
        for size in [1366, 900, 3000, 2] {
            let all: Vec<i32> = (0..size).collect();
            assert_eq!(worst(0, size, &all).1, 0, "size {size}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn default_points_parse() {
        let p = parse_points(DEFAULT_POINTS).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(p.len(), 19);
        assert!(p.contains(&(1919, 1079)) && p.contains(&(0, 540)));
        assert!(parse_points("1,2;3").is_err());
        assert!(parse_points("").is_err());
    }
}


===== FILE: src\dhash.rs =====
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
//! dHash state fingerprints (order E). Pure logic, no Win32. REFERENCE ONLY: a fingerprint
//! never decides an action; the PNG (plus DWM frame bounds) still judges.
//!
//! Classic gradient dHash (9x8):
//! 1. Crop to the region (screen coords, clipped to the captured frame).
//! 2. Downscale to 9x8 grayscale by box average: cell (i,j) covers x in
//!    [x0 + i*w/9, x0 + (i+1)*w/9), y in [y0 + j*h/8, y0 + (j+1)*h/8) (integer bounds),
//!    gray = (77R + 150G + 29B) >> 8, mean rounded to an integer 0..255 (like a u8 resize).
//! 3. Bit convention: for each row j (0 = top) and pair i = 0..7,
//!    bit = 1 if gray(i,j) < gray(i+1,j) (brighter to the right), else 0.
//!    Row-major, MSB first: bit 63 = row 0 pair 0, bit 0 = row 7 pair 7. Hex = 16 lowercase chars.
//! 4. Hamming distance = popcount(a ^ b). Same state if distance <= threshold (default 10).

/// Default same-state threshold (Hamming distance, inclusive).
pub const DEFAULT_THRESHOLD: u32 = 10;

/// Algorithm label written into the JSON.
pub const ALGO: &str = "dhash-gradient-9x8: bit=1 if round(mean gray left) < round(mean gray right); row-major, row 0 = top, bit 63 = row 0 pair 0";

/// Clip a screen rect (l,t,r,b) to a frame at (fx,fy) of size fw x fh.
/// Returns the clipped screen rect, or an error if it is too small (< 9x8 px) or off-frame.
pub fn clip_rect(
    rect: (i32, i32, i32, i32),
    frame: (i32, i32, usize, usize),
) -> Result<(i32, i32, i32, i32), String> {
    let (fx, fy, fw, fh) = frame;
    let l = rect.0.max(fx);
    let t = rect.1.max(fy);
    let r = rect.2.min(fx + fw as i32);
    let b = rect.3.min(fy + fh as i32);
    if r - l < 9 || b - t < 8 {
        return Err(format!(
            "fp: region ({},{},{},{}) is off-screen or smaller than 9x8 px after clipping to the screen ({fx},{fy},{},{}) (minimized window?)",
            rect.0,
            rect.1,
            rect.2,
            rect.3,
            fx + fw as i32,
            fy + fh as i32
        ));
    }
    Ok((l, t, r, b))
}

/// 9x8 grayscale box-average of the region. `bgra` is top-down, `stride` bytes per row,
/// frame origin (fx,fy) in screen coords; `rect` must already be clipped.
pub fn gray_9x8(
    bgra: &[u8],
    stride: usize,
    frame_xy: (i32, i32),
    rect: (i32, i32, i32, i32),
) -> [u8; 72] {
    let x0 = (rect.0 - frame_xy.0) as usize;
    let y0 = (rect.1 - frame_xy.1) as usize;
    let w = (rect.2 - rect.0) as usize;
    let h = (rect.3 - rect.1) as usize;
    let mut out = [0u8; 72];
    for j in 0..8 {
        let ya = y0 + j * h / 8;
        let yb = y0 + (j + 1) * h / 8;
        for i in 0..9 {
            let xa = x0 + i * w / 9;
            let xb = x0 + (i + 1) * w / 9;
            let mut sum = 0u64;
            let mut n = 0u64;
            for y in ya..yb {
                let row = &bgra[y * stride..];
                for x in xa..xb {
                    let p = &row[x * 4..x * 4 + 3];
                    sum += ((77 * p[2] as u32 + 150 * p[1] as u32 + 29 * p[0] as u32) >> 8) as u64;
                    n += 1;
                }
            }
            out[j * 9 + i] = ((sum * 2 + n.max(1)) / (2 * n.max(1))) as u8; // rounded mean
        }
    }
    out
}

/// 64 gradient bits from a 9x8 gray grid (see module doc for the convention).
pub fn bits_from_gray(g: &[u8; 72]) -> u64 {
    let mut h = 0u64;
    for j in 0..8 {
        for i in 0..8 {
            h <<= 1;
            if g[j * 9 + i] < g[j * 9 + i + 1] {
                h |= 1;
            }
        }
    }
    h
}

/// dHash of a region of a BGRA frame.
pub fn dhash(
    bgra: &[u8],
    stride: usize,
    frame: (i32, i32, usize, usize),
    rect: (i32, i32, i32, i32),
) -> Result<(u64, (i32, i32, i32, i32)), String> {
    let r = clip_rect(rect, frame)?;
    Ok((bits_from_gray(&gray_9x8(bgra, stride, (frame.0, frame.1), r)), r))
}

pub fn hamming(a: u64, b: u64) -> u32 {
    (a ^ b).count_ones()
}

/// (distance, same_state) with same_state = distance <= threshold.
pub fn compare(a: u64, b: u64, threshold: u32) -> (u32, bool) {
    let d = hamming(a, b);
    (d, d <= threshold)
}

pub fn to_hex(h: u64) -> String {
    format!("{h:016x}")
}

pub fn from_hex(s: &str) -> Result<u64, String> {
    let t = s.trim();
    if t.len() != 16 {
        return Err(format!("fp: hash must be 16 hex chars, got '{t}'"));
    }
    u64::from_str_radix(t, 16).map_err(|_| format!("fp: bad hex hash '{t}'"))
}

/// Parse `--rect l,t,r,b` (screen coords, negatives allowed).
pub fn parse_rect(s: &str) -> Result<(i32, i32, i32, i32), String> {
    let v: Vec<&str> = s.split(',').map(|x| x.trim()).collect();
    if v.len() != 4 {
        return Err(format!("bad --rect '{s}' (want l,t,r,b)"));
    }
    let mut n = [0i32; 4];
    for (k, x) in v.iter().enumerate() {
        n[k] = x.parse().map_err(|_| format!("bad --rect '{s}' (want l,t,r,b integers)"))?;
    }
    if n[2] <= n[0] || n[3] <= n[1] {
        return Err(format!("bad --rect '{s}' (need r > l and b > t)"));
    }
    Ok((n[0], n[1], n[2], n[3]))
}

/// Region mode of a fingerprint.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Region {
    Screen,
    Rect(i32, i32, i32, i32),
    Hwnd(isize),
}

impl Region {
    pub fn mode(&self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Region::Screen => "screen",
            Region::Rect(..) => "rect",
            Region::Hwnd(_) => "hwnd",
        }
    }
}

/// One fingerprint record (the JSON file).
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Fingerprint {
    pub hash: u64,
    pub mode: String,
    /// Region actually hashed, screen coords (l,t,r,b), after clipping.
    pub rect: (i32, i32, i32, i32),
    pub hwnd: Option<isize>,
    pub timestamp: String,
    pub unix_ms: u64,
    /// Captured virtual screen (x, y, w, h).
    pub screen: (i32, i32, usize, usize),
    pub compare: Option<Comparison>,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Comparison {
    pub file: String,
    pub other_hash: u64,
    pub distance: u32,
    pub threshold: u32,
    pub same_state: bool,
}

fn esc(s: &str) -> String {
    let mut o = String::with_capacity(s.len() + 2);
    for c in s.chars() {
        match c {
            '"' => o.push_str("\\\""),
            '\\' => o.push_str("\\\\"),
            '\n' => o.push_str("\\n"),
            '\r' => o.push_str("\\r"),
            '\t' => o.push_str("\\t"),
            c if (c as u32) < 0x20 => o.push_str(&format!("\\u{:04x}", c as u32)),
            c => o.push(c),
        }
    }
    o
}

impl Fingerprint {
    pub fn to_json(&self) -> String {
        let (l, t, r, b) = self.rect;
        let (sx, sy, sw, sh) = self.screen;
        let mut v = vec![
            "  \"tool\": \"sm fp\"".to_string(),
            format!("  \"hash\": \"{}\"", to_hex(self.hash)),
            format!("  \"algo\": \"{}\"", esc(ALGO)),
            format!("  \"mode\": \"{}\"", esc(&self.mode)),
            format!("  \"rect\": [{l}, {t}, {r}, {b}]"),
            format!(
                "  \"hwnd\": {}",
                self.hwnd.map(|h| h.to_string()).unwrap_or_else(|| "null".into())
            ),
            format!("  \"timestamp\": \"{}\"", esc(&self.timestamp)),
            format!("  \"unix_ms\": {}", self.unix_ms),
            format!("  \"screen\": [{sx}, {sy}, {sw}, {sh}]"),
        ];
        if let Some(c) = &self.compare {
            v.push(format!("  \"compare_file\": \"{}\"", esc(&c.file)));
            v.push(format!("  \"compare_hash\": \"{}\"", to_hex(c.other_hash)));
            v.push(format!("  \"distance\": {}", c.distance));
            v.push(format!("  \"threshold\": {}", c.threshold));
            v.push(format!("  \"same_state\": {}", c.same_state));
        }
        format!("{{\n{}\n}}\n", v.join(",\n"))
    }

    /// stdout summary line.
    pub fn summary(&self) -> String {
        let (l, t, r, b) = self.rect;
        let mut s = format!(
            "fp hash={} mode={} rect=({l},{t},{r},{b}){}",
            to_hex(self.hash),
            self.mode,
            self.hwnd.map(|h| format!(" hwnd={h}")).unwrap_or_default()
        );
        if let Some(c) = &self.compare {
            s.push_str(&format!(
                " compare={} distance={} threshold={} same_state={}",
                to_hex(c.other_hash),
                c.distance,
                c.threshold,
                c.same_state
            ));
        }
        s.push_str(" (reference only; the PNG judges)");
        s
    }

    /// Parse a JSON file written by [`Fingerprint::to_json`] (minimal reader for our own format).
    pub fn from_json(s: &str) -> Result<Fingerprint, String> {
        let hash = from_hex(&json_string(s, "hash")?)?;
        let nums = |k: &str| -> Result<Vec<i64>, String> {
            let raw = json_raw(s, k)?;
            raw.trim_matches(|c| c == '[' || c == ']')
                .split(',')
                .map(|x| x.trim().parse::<i64>().map_err(|_| format!("fp json: bad {k}")))
                .collect()
        };
        let r = nums("rect")?;
        let sc = nums("screen")?;
        if r.len() != 4 || sc.len() != 4 {
            return Err("fp json: rect/screen need 4 numbers".into());
        }
        let hwnd = match json_raw(s, "hwnd")?.trim() {
            "null" => None,
            x => Some(x.parse::<isize>().map_err(|_| "fp json: bad hwnd".to_string())?),
        };
        let compare = if json_raw(s, "distance").is_ok() {
            Some(Comparison {
                file: json_string(s, "compare_file")?,
                other_hash: from_hex(&json_string(s, "compare_hash")?)?,
                distance: json_raw(s, "distance")?.trim().parse().map_err(|_| "fp json: bad distance")?,
                threshold: json_raw(s, "threshold")?.trim().parse().map_err(|_| "fp json: bad threshold")?,
                same_state: json_raw(s, "same_state")?.trim() == "true",
            })
        } else {
            None
        };
        Ok(Fingerprint {
            hash,
            mode: json_string(s, "mode")?,
            rect: (r[0] as i32, r[1] as i32, r[2] as i32, r[3] as i32),
            hwnd,
            timestamp: json_string(s, "timestamp")?,
            unix_ms: json_raw(s, "unix_ms")?.trim().parse().map_err(|_| "fp json: bad unix_ms")?,
            screen: (sc[0] as i32, sc[1] as i32, sc[2] as usize, sc[3] as usize),
            compare,
        })
    }
}

/// Raw value text after `"key":` (up to the next top-level `,` / `}` / newline; arrays kept whole).
fn json_raw<'a>(s: &'a str, key: &str) -> Result<&'a str, String> {
    // A key is `"key"` followed by `:` (the same text as a string VALUE, e.g. "mode": "hwnd", is skipped).
    let pat = format!("\"{key}\"");
    let mut from = 0;
    let rest = loop {
        let i = s[from..].find(&pat).ok_or_else(|| format!("fp json: no \"{key}\""))? + from;
        let after = s[i + pat.len()..].trim_start();
        if let Some(r) = after.strip_prefix(':') {
            break r.trim_start();
        }
        from = i + pat.len();
    };
    let end = if rest.starts_with('[') {
        rest.find(']').map(|e| e + 1)
    } else if rest.starts_with('"') {
        let b = rest.as_bytes();
        let mut k = 1;
        while k < b.len() && b[k] != b'"' {
            if b[k] == b'\\' {
                k += 1;
            }
            k += 1;
        }
        Some((k + 1).min(b.len()))
    } else {
        rest.find([',', '}', '\n'])
    }
    .unwrap_or(rest.len());
    Ok(&rest[..end])
}

fn json_string(s: &str, key: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
    let raw = json_raw(s, key)?;
    let inner = raw
        .strip_prefix('"')
        .and_then(|x| x.strip_suffix('"'))
        .ok_or_else(|| format!("fp json: \"{key}\" is not a string"))?;
    let mut o = String::new();
    let mut it = inner.chars();
    while let Some(c) = it.next() {
        if c == '\\' {
            match it.next() {
                Some('n') => o.push('\n'),
                Some('r') => o.push('\r'),
                Some('t') => o.push('\t'),
                Some('u') => {
                    let hex: String = it.by_ref().take(4).collect();
                    if let Some(ch) = u32::from_str_radix(&hex, 16).ok().and_then(char::from_u32) {
                        o.push(ch);
                    }
                }
                Some(x) => o.push(x),
                None => {}
            }
        } else {
            o.push(c);
        }
    }
    Ok(o)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    const W: usize = 360; // 9 x 40
    const H: usize = 160; // 8 x 20

    /// Gradient background (brighter to the right) plus flat rectangles (l,t,r,b,gray).
    fn layout(rects: &[(usize, usize, usize, usize, u8)]) -> Vec<u8> {
        let mut b = vec![0u8; W * H * 4];
        for y in 0..H {
            for x in 0..W {
                let mut g = (40 + x * 180 / W) as u8;
                for &(l, t, r, bb, v) in rects {
                    if x >= l && x < r && y >= t && y < bb {
                        g = v;
                    }
                }
                let i = (y * W + x) * 4;
                b[i..i + 4].copy_from_slice(&[g, g, g, 255]);
            }
        }
        b
    }

    fn noisy(mut b: Vec<u8>, seed: u32) -> Vec<u8> {
        let mut s = seed;
        for px in b.chunks_exact_mut(4) {
            s = s.wrapping_mul(1103515245).wrapping_add(12345);
            if (s >> 16) % 10 == 0 {
                // 10% of pixels, +-1..3 gray levels
                let d = ((s >> 8) % 7) as i32 - 3;
                for c in &mut px[..3] {
                    *c = (*c as i32 + d).clamp(0, 255) as u8;
                }
            }
        }
        b
    }

    fn h(b: &[u8]) -> u64 {
        dhash(b, W * 4, (0, 0, W, H), (0, 0, W as i32, H as i32)).unwrap().0
    }

    const WIN_A: (usize, usize, usize, usize, u8) = (40, 20, 160, 140, 30);
    const WIN_B: (usize, usize, usize, usize, u8) = (200, 40, 320, 120, 230);

    #[test]
    fn identical_frames_distance_zero() {
        let a = layout(&[WIN_A, WIN_B]);
        let b = layout(&[WIN_A, WIN_B]);
        assert_eq!(compare(h(&a), h(&b), DEFAULT_THRESHOLD), (0, true));
        // gradient background: all bits 1; flat window interiors: 0
        assert_eq!(h(&layout(&[])), u64::MAX);
    }

    #[test]
    fn small_noise_stays_same_state() {
        let base = layout(&[WIN_A, WIN_B]);
        for seed in [1u32, 7, 42, 99] {
            let d = hamming(h(&base), h(&noisy(base.clone(), seed)));
            assert!(d <= DEFAULT_THRESHOLD, "seed {seed} distance {d}");
        }
        // a flat frame with noise stays too (rounded means)
        let flat = {
            let mut b = vec![0u8; W * H * 4];
            for px in b.chunks_exact_mut(4) {
                px.copy_from_slice(&[128, 128, 128, 255]);
            }
            b
        };
        assert!(hamming(h(&flat), h(&noisy(flat.clone(), 5))) <= DEFAULT_THRESHOLD);
    }

    #[test]
    fn different_layout_goes_above_threshold() {
        let base = h(&layout(&[WIN_A, WIN_B]));
        // window A moved right onto B's spot, B removed
        let moved = h(&layout(&[(200, 20, 320, 140, 30)]));
        let (d, same) = compare(base, moved, DEFAULT_THRESHOLD);
        assert!(d > DEFAULT_THRESHOLD && !same, "moved distance {d}");
        // window A removed
        let (d, same) = compare(base, h(&layout(&[WIN_B])), DEFAULT_THRESHOLD);
        assert!(d > DEFAULT_THRESHOLD && !same, "removed distance {d}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn region_crop_matches_standalone_and_clips() {
        let big = layout(&[WIN_A, WIN_B]);
        // region = window B area, frame origin at (-100,50) in screen coords
        let fr = (-100, 50, W, H);
        let rect = (-100 + 200, 50 + 40, -100 + 320, 50 + 120);
        let (hb, r) = dhash(&big, W * 4, fr, rect).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(r, rect);
        // same pixels as a standalone 120x80 frame
        let (cw, ch) = (120usize, 80usize);
        let mut crop = vec![0u8; cw * ch * 4];
        for y in 0..ch {
            let src = ((40 + y) * W + 200) * 4;
            crop[y * cw * 4..(y + 1) * cw * 4].copy_from_slice(&big[src..src + cw * 4]);
        }
        let (hc, _) = dhash(&crop, cw * 4, (0, 0, cw, ch), (0, 0, cw as i32, ch as i32)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(hb, hc);
        // partly off-screen clips; fully off-screen (minimized, parked) errors
        assert_eq!(clip_rect((-500, 0, 100, 100), (0, 0, 1920, 1080)).unwrap(), (0, 0, 100, 100));
        assert!(clip_rect((-31993, -32000, -31847, -31972), (0, 0, 1920, 1080)).is_err());
        assert_eq!(parse_rect("48,429,574,842"), Ok((48, 429, 574, 842)));
        assert_eq!(parse_rect("-10, 0, 5, 9"), Ok((-10, 0, 5, 9)));
        assert!(parse_rect("1,2,3").is_err());
        assert!(parse_rect("5,5,1,9").is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn bit_convention_and_hex() {
        // one row brighter to the right in the first pair only
        let mut g = [100u8; 72];
        g[1] = 101; // row 0: gray(0) < gray(1) -> bit 63
        assert_eq!(bits_from_gray(&g), 1u64 << 63);
        g[8 + 9 * 7] = 200; // row 7 pair 7 -> bit 0
        assert_eq!(bits_from_gray(&g), (1u64 << 63) | 1);
        assert_eq!(to_hex(0xf0e1d2c3b4a59687), "f0e1d2c3b4a59687");
        assert_eq!(from_hex("f0e1d2c3b4a59687"), Ok(0xf0e1d2c3b4a59687));
        assert!(from_hex("xyz").is_err());
        assert_eq!(compare(0, 0b1111111111, 10), (10, true));
        assert_eq!(compare(0, 0b11111111111, 10), (11, false));
    }

    #[test]
    fn json_round_trip() {
        let fp = Fingerprint {
            hash: 0x8f0f0f0f0f0f0f0e,
            mode: "hwnd".into(),
            rect: (48, 429, 574, 842),
            hwnd: Some(393774),
            timestamp: "2026-09-24T16:40:12.123 local".into(),
            unix_ms: 1790286012123,
            screen: (0, 0, 1920, 1080),
            compare: Some(Comparison {
                file: "C:\\proof\\before \"A\".json".into(),
                other_hash: 0x8f0f0f0f0f0f0f0f,
                distance: 1,
                threshold: 10,
                same_state: true,
            }),
        };
        let j = fp.to_json();
        println!("{j}");
        assert!(j.contains("\"hash\": \"8f0f0f0f0f0f0f0e\""));
        assert!(j.contains("\"same_state\": true"));
        assert_eq!(Fingerprint::from_json(&j).unwrap(), fp);
        let plain = Fingerprint { hwnd: None, mode: "screen".into(), compare: None, ..fp.clone() };
        let j2 = plain.to_json();
        assert!(j2.contains("\"hwnd\": null") && !j2.contains("distance"));
        assert_eq!(Fingerprint::from_json(&j2).unwrap(), plain);
        assert!(plain.summary().starts_with("fp hash=8f0f0f0f0f0f0f0e mode=screen rect=(48,429,574,842)"));
    }
}


===== FILE: src\diff.rs =====
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
//! Region-aware PNG diff (order 17c). Pure and read-only: it compares two decoded images.
//!
//! - A pixel is CHANGED if any RGB channel differs by more than `tol` (default 24).
//! - Ignore cells (from a settle log `masked_cells=` list or a plain list) are removed first and
//!   always listed in the JSON; nothing is masked silently.
//! - Blobs: the image is cut into `cell` x `cell` squares (default 16); a square with at least one
//!   changed pixel is marked; marked squares are joined 8-neighbour; each blob reports the tight
//!   full-res bounding box of its changed pixels, its px count and how many of them are inside /
//!   outside the expected region.
//! - Expected region = union of the given rects (repeat --expect-rect; --expect-hwnd adds the live
//!   frame bounds). Image pixels == screen pixels (sm shots cover the virtual screen at (0,0)).
//! - Verdicts: NO_CHANGE (no changed px after the ignore mask). With an expected region:
//!   COLLATERAL if changed px outside > outside_max (outside blobs listed), else EXPECTED_CHANGE
//!   if changed px inside > 0, else NO_EXPECTED_CHANGE (only a few px changed, all outside and
//!   under the limit). Without a region: CHANGED or NO_CHANGE.
//! - outside_max: default 200 px; `N` px or `P%` of the outside pixel count (for example 0.1%).

use crate::png_dec::Rgb;

pub const DEFAULT_TOL: u8 = 24;
pub const DEFAULT_CELL: usize = 16;
pub const DEFAULT_OUTSIDE_MAX: &str = "200";

/// Screen/image rect, right/bottom exclusive.
pub type Rect = (i32, i32, i32, i32);

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct IgnoreCell {
    pub label: String,
    pub rect: Rect,
}

/// Outside threshold: absolute px or a percentage of the outside pixel count.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum OutsideMax {
    Px(u64),
    Pct(f64),
}

impl OutsideMax {
    pub fn parse(s: &str) -> Result<OutsideMax, String> {
        let t = s.trim();
        if let Some(p) = t.strip_suffix('%') {
            let v: f64 = p.trim().parse().map_err(|_| format!("bad --outside-max '{s}'"))?;
            if !(0.0..=100.0).contains(&v) {
                return Err(format!("bad --outside-max '{s}' (0..100%)"));
            }
            Ok(OutsideMax::Pct(v))
        } else {
            Ok(OutsideMax::Px(t.parse().map_err(|_| format!("bad --outside-max '{s}' (px or N%)"))?))
        }
    }
    pub fn limit(&self, outside_px: u64) -> u64 {
        match *self {
            OutsideMax::Px(n) => n,
