// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
//! Minimize.
//!
//! The dash in the title bar is HTMINBUTTON, never the square and never Close.
//! Reuses the existing click command. No second command.
//! Pass only when the window is iconic and the process is still alive.

pub const HT_MINBUTTON: i32 = 8;
pub const HT_MAXBUTTON: i32 = 9;
pub const HT_CLOSE: i32 = 20;

pub fn is_minimize_button(hit: i32) -> bool {
    hit == HT_MINBUTTON
}

pub fn is_close_button(hit: i32) -> bool {
    hit == HT_CLOSE
}

pub fn click_would_hit_close(hit: i32) -> bool {
    hit == HT_CLOSE
}

/// Dash is left of the square, which is left of Close.
pub fn caption_order_ok(min_x: i32, max_x: i32, close_x: i32) -> bool {
    min_x < max_x && max_x < close_x
}

/// Minimized and still running. Closed is not a pass.
pub fn minimize_passed(iconic: bool, alive: bool) -> bool {
    iconic && alive
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn dash_is_not_close_or_the_square() {
        assert!(is_minimize_button(HT_MINBUTTON));
        assert!(!is_minimize_button(HT_CLOSE));
        assert!(!is_minimize_button(HT_MAXBUTTON));
        assert!(click_would_hit_close(HT_CLOSE));
        assert!(!click_would_hit_close(HT_MINBUTTON));
    }

    #[test]
    fn dash_sits_left_of_square_and_close() {
        assert!(caption_order_ok(678, 742, 806));
        assert!(!caption_order_ok(806, 742, 678));
        assert!(!caption_order_ok(742, 742, 806));
    }

    #[test]
    fn closed_window_is_not_minimized() {
        assert!(minimize_passed(true, true));
        assert!(!minimize_passed(true, false));
        assert!(!minimize_passed(false, true));
    }
}


===== FILE: src\native.rs =====
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
//! Win32 cursor / SendInput / DPI helpers.

use std::time::Duration;

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Point {
    pub x: i32,
    pub y: i32,
}

#[cfg(all(windows, feature = "win32"))]
mod win {
    use super::Point;
    use std::mem::size_of;
    use windows::Win32::Foundation::POINT;
    use windows::Win32::UI::HiDpi::{
        SetThreadDpiAwarenessContext, DPI_AWARENESS_CONTEXT_PER_MONITOR_AWARE_V2,
    };
    use windows::Win32::UI::Input::KeyboardAndMouse::{
        SendInput, INPUT, INPUT_0, INPUT_KEYBOARD, INPUT_MOUSE, KEYBDINPUT, KEYEVENTF_KEYUP,
        KEYEVENTF_UNICODE, MOUSEEVENTF_ABSOLUTE, MOUSEEVENTF_LEFTDOWN, MOUSEEVENTF_LEFTUP,
        MOUSEEVENTF_MOVE, MOUSEEVENTF_RIGHTDOWN, MOUSEEVENTF_RIGHTUP, MOUSEEVENTF_WHEEL,
        MOUSEINPUT, VIRTUAL_KEY,
    };
    use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::{
        GetCursorPos, GetSystemMetrics, SetCursorPos, SM_CXSCREEN, SM_CYSCREEN,
    };

    pub fn ensure_dpi() {
        unsafe {
            let _ = SetThreadDpiAwarenessContext(DPI_AWARENESS_CONTEXT_PER_MONITOR_AWARE_V2);
        }
    }

    pub fn cursor_pos() -> Point {
        ensure_dpi();
        unsafe {
            let mut pt = POINT::default();
            let _ = GetCursorPos(&mut pt);
            Point { x: pt.x, y: pt.y }
        }
    }

    /// After a human override (or a hit-test miss), stop issuing new input.
    fn blocked() -> bool {
        crate::override_guard::aborted() || crate::hittest::missed() || crate::uipi::blocked()
    }

    pub fn set_cursor_pos(x: i32, y: i32) {
        if blocked() {
            return;
        }
        ensure_dpi();
        unsafe {
            let _ = SetCursorPos(x, y);
        }
    }

    fn screen_size() -> (i32, i32) {
        unsafe {
            let w = GetSystemMetrics(SM_CXSCREEN).max(2);
            let h = GetSystemMetrics(SM_CYSCREEN).max(2);
            (w, h)
        }
    }

    // Same formula as before, now shared with the unit-tested `coords::to_abs` (order F).
    fn abs_x(x: i32) -> i32 {
        let (sx, _) = screen_size();
        crate::coords::to_abs(x, 0, sx)
    }

    fn abs_y(y: i32) -> i32 {
        let (_, sy) = screen_size();
        crate::coords::to_abs(y, 0, sy)
    }

    fn send_mouse(flags: u32, dx: i32, dy: i32, data: u32) {
        // Button-up flags always pass so a held button is released.
        let release_only = flags & !(MOUSEEVENTF_LEFTUP.0 | MOUSEEVENTF_RIGHTUP.0) == 0;
        // A hit-test miss / UIPI block happens before our first button-down, so nothing is held:
        // send nothing at all (not even a stray button-up).
        if crate::hittest::missed() || crate::uipi::blocked() {
            return;
        }
        if blocked() && !release_only {
            return;
        }
        // Pre-action hit-test gate (--expect-*): checked right before the first button-down.
        let down = flags & (MOUSEEVENTF_LEFTDOWN.0 | MOUSEEVENTF_RIGHTDOWN.0) != 0;
        if down && !crate::hittest::gate_down() {
            return;
        }
        unsafe {
            let mut input = INPUT {
                r#type: INPUT_MOUSE,
                Anonymous: INPUT_0 {
                    mi: MOUSEINPUT {
                        dx,
                        dy,
                        mouseData: data,
                        dwFlags: windows::Win32::UI::Input::KeyboardAndMouse::MOUSE_EVENT_FLAGS(
                            flags,
                        ),
                        time: 0,
                        dwExtraInfo: 0,
                    },
                },
            };
            let _ = SendInput(&[input], size_of::<INPUT>() as i32);
            // silence unused mut warning path
            let _ = &mut input;
        }
    }

    pub fn send_abs_move(x: i32, y: i32) {
        send_mouse(
            MOUSEEVENTF_MOVE.0 | MOUSEEVENTF_ABSOLUTE.0,
            abs_x(x),
            abs_y(y),
            0,
        );
    }

    pub fn mouse_btn(flags: u32) {
        send_mouse(flags, 0, 0, 0);
    }

    pub fn click_hybrid(x: i32, y: i32, right: bool) {
        ensure_dpi();
        set_cursor_pos(x, y);
        sleep_ms(35);
        send_abs_move(x, y);
        sleep_ms(20);
        set_cursor_pos(x, y);
        sleep_ms(20);
        if right {
            mouse_btn(MOUSEEVENTF_RIGHTDOWN.0);
            sleep_ms(45);
            mouse_btn(MOUSEEVENTF_RIGHTUP.0);
        } else {
            mouse_btn(MOUSEEVENTF_LEFTDOWN.0);
            sleep_ms(45);
            mouse_btn(MOUSEEVENTF_LEFTUP.0);
        }
    }

    /// Left down/up at the current pointer. Does not call SetCursorPos or abs-move.
    pub fn left_click_still() {
        mouse_btn(MOUSEEVENTF_LEFTDOWN.0);
        sleep_ms(20);
        mouse_btn(MOUSEEVENTF_LEFTUP.0);
    }

    /// Hold / release. Does not move the pointer.
    pub fn left_down() {
        mouse_btn(MOUSEEVENTF_LEFTDOWN.0);
    }

    pub fn left_up() {
        mouse_btn(MOUSEEVENTF_LEFTUP.0);
    }

    pub fn scroll_wheel(notches: i32) {
        ensure_dpi();
        // positive notches = scroll up (away from user) matching common CLI expectation
        const WHEEL_DELTA: i32 = 120;
        let data = (notches.saturating_mul(WHEEL_DELTA)) as u32;
        send_mouse(MOUSEEVENTF_WHEEL.0, 0, 0, data);
    }

    pub fn send_unicode_char(ch: u16, up: bool) {
        if blocked() && !up {
            return;
        }
        crate::override_guard::note_key_sent();
        unsafe {
            let mut flags = KEYEVENTF_UNICODE;
            if up {
                flags |= KEYEVENTF_KEYUP;
            }
            let input = INPUT {
                r#type: INPUT_KEYBOARD,
                Anonymous: INPUT_0 {
                    ki: KEYBDINPUT {
                        wVk: VIRTUAL_KEY(0),
                        wScan: ch,
                        dwFlags: flags,
                        time: 0,
                        dwExtraInfo: 0,
                    },
                },
            };
            let _ = SendInput(&[input], size_of::<INPUT>() as i32);
        }
    }

    pub fn send_vk(vk: u16, up: bool) {
        use windows::Win32::UI::Input::KeyboardAndMouse::KEYEVENTF_KEYUP;
        if blocked() && !up {
            return;
        }
        crate::override_guard::note_key_sent();
        unsafe {
            let flags = if up {
                KEYEVENTF_KEYUP
            } else {
                windows::Win32::UI::Input::KeyboardAndMouse::KEYBD_EVENT_FLAGS(0)
            };
            let input = INPUT {
                r#type: INPUT_KEYBOARD,
                Anonymous: INPUT_0 {
                    ki: KEYBDINPUT {
                        wVk: VIRTUAL_KEY(vk),
                        wScan: 0,
                        dwFlags: flags,
                        time: 0,
                        dwExtraInfo: 0,
                    },
                },
            };
            let _ = SendInput(&[input], size_of::<INPUT>() as i32);
        }
    }

    fn sleep_ms(ms: u64) {
        std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(ms));
    }
}

#[cfg(all(windows, feature = "win32"))]
pub use win::*;

#[cfg(not(all(windows, feature = "win32")))]
mod stub {
    use super::Point;
    pub fn ensure_dpi() {}
    pub fn cursor_pos() -> Point {
        Point { x: 0, y: 0 }
    }
    pub fn set_cursor_pos(_x: i32, _y: i32) {}
    pub fn send_abs_move(_x: i32, _y: i32) {}
    pub fn click_hybrid(_x: i32, _y: i32, _right: bool) {}
    pub fn left_click_still() {}
    pub fn left_down() {}
    pub fn left_up() {}
    pub fn scroll_wheel(_notches: i32) {}
    pub fn send_unicode_char(_ch: u16, _up: bool) {}
    pub fn send_vk(_vk: u16, _up: bool) {}
}

#[cfg(not(all(windows, feature = "win32")))]
pub use stub::*;

pub fn sleep_ms(ms: u64) {
    std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(ms));
}


===== FILE: src\override_guard.rs =====
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
//! Human-override guard: an OBSERVE-ONLY WH_MOUSE_LL hook that watches for a real
//! human touching the mouse while `sm` is injecting input.
//!
//! Because the laptop is shared, any low-level mouse event WITHOUT LLMHF_INJECTED
//! during a motion/click command means a person grabbed the pointer. When that
//! happens the motion loops stop issuing input (they poll [`aborted`]), held
//! buttons/keys are released by their existing RAII guards, and `sm` exits with a
//! distinct code (`cli::EXIT_HUMAN_OVERRIDE` = 7) after printing
//! `HUMAN_OVERRIDE` on stderr.
//!
//! The hook callback is OBSERVE-ONLY: it always calls CallNextHookEx and never
//! blocks, swallows, or edits an event. It only reads MSLLHOOKSTRUCT and pushes a
//! record, so it returns well under the ~1000 ms Windows hook timeout.

use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, AtomicU64, Ordering};

/// LLMHF_INJECTED. Our own SendInput events carry this; SetCursorPos moves raise
/// no low-level event at all, so a clear (non-injected) event is a human.
pub const LLMHF_INJECTED: u32 = 0x0000_0001;

/// Set true by the hook the moment a non-injected event is seen.
static ABORT: AtomicBool = AtomicBool::new(false);
/// Count of events the hook actually observed (for the HOOK_LOST watchdog).
static HOOK_COUNT: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
/// GetTickCount at hook install. Events stamped earlier were queued before the
/// hook became active and are ignored for the verdict.
static START_T: std::sync::atomic::AtomicU32 = std::sync::atomic::AtomicU32::new(0);
static START_SET: AtomicBool = AtomicBool::new(false);
/// sm injected keyboard input this command (keys advance GetLastInputInfo but never
/// reach a mouse hook, so the HOOK_LOST watchdog must not fire for them).
static KEYS_SENT: AtomicBool = AtomicBool::new(false);

/// Called by native keyboard senders.
pub fn note_key_sent() {
    KEYS_SENT.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed);
}

/// True once a human (non-injected) event has been seen this command.
/// Motion/click loops poll this between steps and stop issuing input.
pub fn aborted() -> bool {
    ABORT.load(Ordering::Relaxed)
}

/// Clear state. Called right before the hook is installed so events queued
/// before the hook became active cannot leak into this command's verdict.
pub fn reset() {
    ABORT.store(false, Ordering::Relaxed);
    HOOK_COUNT.store(0, Ordering::Relaxed);
    START_SET.store(false, Ordering::Relaxed);
    KEYS_SENT.store(false, Ordering::Relaxed);
}

fn mark_start(t: u32) {
    START_T.store(t, Ordering::Relaxed);
    START_SET.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed);
}

/// Returns true if the event counted toward the verdict (not stale).
fn on_event(flags: u32, t: u32) -> bool {
    if START_SET.load(Ordering::Relaxed) && is_stale(t, START_T.load(Ordering::Relaxed)) {
        return false;
    }
    HOOK_COUNT.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
    if is_human_event(flags) {
        ABORT.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }
    true
}

/// How many events the hook observed this command.
pub fn events_seen() -> u64 {
    HOOK_COUNT.load(Ordering::Relaxed)
}

// ---- Pure decision logic (unit-tested; platform-independent) ---------------

/// A non-injected event is a human touch.
pub fn is_human_event(flags: u32) -> bool {
    flags & LLMHF_INJECTED == 0
}

/// Event tick earlier than the hook-install tick (wrap-safe) = queued before the
/// hook was active; ignore it.
pub fn is_stale(event_t: u32, start_t: u32) -> bool {
    (event_t.wrapping_sub(start_t) as i32) < 0
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Verdict {
    Clean,
    HumanOverride,
}

/// Fold a stream of MSLLHOOKSTRUCT flag values into a verdict. Any single
/// non-injected event -> HumanOverride; a stream of only injected events -> Clean.
pub fn decide<I: IntoIterator<Item = u32>>(flags: I) -> Verdict {
    for f in flags {
        if is_human_event(f) {
            return Verdict::HumanOverride;
        }
    }
    Verdict::Clean
}

/// Watchdog decision (pure): last-input tick advanced, hook saw no mouse events,
/// and sm sent no keyboard input -> hook probably lost.
pub fn hook_lost(tick_advanced: bool, events: u64, keys_sent: bool) -> bool {
    tick_advanced && events == 0 && !keys_sent
}

/// Verdict for the command that just ran, from the live abort flag.
pub fn verdict() -> Verdict {
    if aborted() {
        Verdict::HumanOverride
    } else {
        Verdict::Clean
    }
}

/// One recorded low-level mouse event (for `--trace`).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
pub struct TraceRec {
    pub t: u32,
    pub x: i32,
    pub y: i32,
    pub flags: u32,
    pub injected: bool,
}

// ---- Windows hook implementation -------------------------------------------

#[cfg(all(windows, feature = "win32"))]
mod imp {
    use super::*;
    use std::sync::mpsc;
    use std::sync::atomic::AtomicU32;
    use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
    use std::thread::JoinHandle;
    use windows::Win32::Foundation::{HINSTANCE, HWND, LPARAM, LRESULT, WPARAM};
    use windows::Win32::System::LibraryLoader::GetModuleHandleW;
    use windows::Win32::System::SystemInformation::GetTickCount;
    use windows::Win32::System::Threading::GetCurrentThreadId;
    use windows::Win32::UI::Input::KeyboardAndMouse::{GetLastInputInfo, LASTINPUTINFO};
    use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::{
        CallNextHookEx, GetMessageW, PostThreadMessageW, SetWindowsHookExW, UnhookWindowsHookEx,
        HHOOK, MSG, MSLLHOOKSTRUCT, WH_MOUSE_LL, WM_QUIT,
    };

    static TRACING: AtomicBool = AtomicBool::new(false);
    static TRACE: Mutex<Vec<TraceRec>> = Mutex::new(Vec::new());

    pub(super) fn last_input_tick() -> u32 {
        unsafe {
            let mut lii = LASTINPUTINFO {
                cbSize: std::mem::size_of::<LASTINPUTINFO>() as u32,
                dwTime: 0,
            };
            let _ = GetLastInputInfo(&mut lii);
            lii.dwTime
        }
    }

    /// OBSERVE-ONLY callback. Reads the event, records it, and always chains.
    unsafe extern "system" fn ll_mouse(code: i32, wparam: WPARAM, lparam: LPARAM) -> LRESULT {
        if code >= 0 {
            let info = &*(lparam.0 as *const MSLLHOOKSTRUCT);
            let flags = info.flags;
            let counted = on_event(flags, info.time);
            if counted && TRACING.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
                if let Ok(mut v) = TRACE.lock() {
                    v.push(TraceRec {
                        t: info.time,
                        x: info.pt.x,
                        y: info.pt.y,
                        flags,
                        injected: !is_human_event(flags),
                    });
                }
            }
        }
        CallNextHookEx(HHOOK::default(), code, wparam, lparam)
    }

    /// RAII watch: installs the hook on a dedicated thread with its own message
    /// loop; Drop posts WM_QUIT and joins the thread (removes the hook), even on
    /// error or panic unwind.
    pub struct HumanWatch {
        tid: Arc<AtomicU32>,
        handle: Option<JoinHandle<()>>,
        start_tick: u32,
    }

    impl HumanWatch {
        pub fn start(trace: bool) -> Self {
            reset();
            TRACING.store(trace, Ordering::Relaxed);
            if let Ok(mut v) = TRACE.lock() {
                v.clear();
            }
            let start_tick = last_input_tick();
            let tid = Arc::new(AtomicU32::new(0));
            let tid_thread = tid.clone();
            let (ready_tx, ready_rx) = mpsc::channel::<bool>();
            let handle = std::thread::spawn(move || {
                unsafe {
                    tid_thread.store(GetCurrentThreadId(), Ordering::Relaxed);
                    let hmod = GetModuleHandleW(windows::core::PCWSTR::null())
                        .map(|m| HINSTANCE(m.0))
                        .unwrap_or_default();
                    let hook = SetWindowsHookExW(WH_MOUSE_LL, Some(ll_mouse), hmod, 0);
                    let installed = hook.is_ok();
                    if installed {
                        mark_start(GetTickCount());
                    }
                    let _ = ready_tx.send(installed);
                    if let Ok(h) = hook {
                        let mut msg = MSG::default();
                        while GetMessageW(&mut msg, HWND::default(), 0, 0).0 > 0 {}
                        let _ = UnhookWindowsHookEx(h);
                    }
                }
            });
            // Block until the hook is installed so events during the command are caught.
            let _ = ready_rx.recv();
            HumanWatch {
                tid,
                handle: Some(handle),
                start_tick,
            }
        }

        /// Watchdog: if GetLastInputInfo advanced during the command but the hook
        /// saw nothing, the hook was probably dropped by Windows. Warning only.
        pub fn watchdog(&self) -> Option<String> {
            let end_tick = last_input_tick();
            if hook_lost(end_tick != self.start_tick, events_seen(), KEYS_SENT.load(Ordering::Relaxed)) {
                Some(format!(
                    "HOOK_LOST last-input tick advanced {}->{} but hook saw 0 events",
                    self.start_tick, end_tick
                ))
            } else {
                None
            }
        }
    }

    impl Drop for HumanWatch {
        fn drop(&mut self) {
            let tid = self.tid.load(Ordering::Relaxed);
            if tid != 0 {
                unsafe {
                    let _ = PostThreadMessageW(tid, WM_QUIT, WPARAM(0), LPARAM(0));
                }
            }
            if let Some(h) = self.handle.take() {
                let _ = h.join();
            }
        }
    }

    pub(super) fn snapshot_trace() -> Vec<TraceRec> {
        TRACE.lock().map(|v| v.clone()).unwrap_or_default()
    }
}

#[cfg(all(windows, feature = "win32"))]
pub use imp::HumanWatch;

// ---- Non-Windows / stub build ----------------------------------------------

#[cfg(not(all(windows, feature = "win32")))]
mod imp {
    use super::*;
    pub struct HumanWatch;
    impl HumanWatch {
        pub fn start(_trace: bool) -> Self {
            reset();
            HumanWatch
        }
        pub fn watchdog(&self) -> Option<String> {
            None
        }
    }
    pub(super) fn snapshot_trace() -> Vec<TraceRec> {
        Vec::new()
    }
    pub(super) fn last_input_tick() -> u32 {
        0
    }
}

#[cfg(not(all(windows, feature = "win32")))]
pub use imp::HumanWatch;

/// Write the trace log to `path`: a header, one line per observed event
/// (`t x y flags injected=yes|no`), then the final verdict.
pub fn write_trace(
    path: &str,
    verdict: Verdict,
    hook_lost: Option<&str>,
    extra: &[String],
) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    use std::io::Write;
    let mut f = std::fs::File::create(path)?;
    writeln!(f, "# sm --trace : low-level mouse path (observe-only WH_MOUSE_LL)")?;
    writeln!(f, "# t x y flags injected")?;
    let recs = imp::snapshot_trace();
    for r in &recs {
        writeln!(
            f,
            "{} {} {} 0x{:X} injected={}",
            r.t,
            r.x,
            r.y,
            r.flags,
            if r.injected { "yes" } else { "no" }
        )?;
    }
    for x in extra {
        writeln!(f, "# {x}")?;
    }
    writeln!(f, "events={}", recs.len())?;
    writeln!(f, "hook_lost={}", hook_lost.unwrap_or("no"))?;
    writeln!(
        f,
        "verdict={}",
        match verdict {
            Verdict::Clean => "clean",
            Verdict::HumanOverride => "HUMAN_OVERRIDE",
        }
    )?;
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn injected_flag_is_not_human() {
        assert!(!is_human_event(LLMHF_INJECTED));
        assert!(!is_human_event(LLMHF_INJECTED | 0x10));
        assert!(is_human_event(0));
        assert!(is_human_event(0x10)); // LLMHF_LOWER_IL_INJECTED absent = still human here
    }

    #[test]
    fn stream_of_injected_only_passes() {
        let injected = [LLMHF_INJECTED, LLMHF_INJECTED, LLMHF_INJECTED];
        assert_eq!(decide(injected), Verdict::Clean);
    }

    #[test]
    fn one_non_injected_event_aborts() {
        // Our own move/click events are injected; a single clear one is a human.
        let stream = [LLMHF_INJECTED, LLMHF_INJECTED, 0, LLMHF_INJECTED];
        assert_eq!(decide(stream), Verdict::HumanOverride);
    }

    #[test]
    fn events_before_hook_install_are_stale() {
        assert!(is_stale(999, 1000));
        assert!(!is_stale(1000, 1000));
        assert!(!is_stale(1005, 1000));
        // tick wrap: start just before wrap, event just after
        assert!(!is_stale(3, u32::MAX - 2));
        assert!(is_stale(u32::MAX - 5, 2));
    }

    #[test]
    fn watchdog_hook_lost_rules() {
        assert!(hook_lost(true, 0, false));
        assert!(!hook_lost(false, 0, false)); // no input at all
        assert!(!hook_lost(true, 3, false)); // hook saw events
        assert!(!hook_lost(true, 0, true)); // keyboard-only command
    }

    #[test]
    fn empty_stream_is_clean() {
        let none: [u32; 0] = [];
        assert_eq!(decide(none), Verdict::Clean);
    }
}


===== FILE: src\path.rs =====
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
//! Human-like pointer paths (gust / bezier / natural / hybrid / linear / minjerk).
//!
//! Pure planning/sample helpers in this module do **not** call SetCursorPos / SendInput.
//! `humove` is the I/O wrapper.
//!
//! **Default `Algo::Gust` (few-pulse backlog):** Item #1 Fitts duration + Item #2 one
//! asymmetric ballistic velocity pulse + Item #3 optional 1â€“2 smaller corrective pulses
//! + Item #4 small mean-reverting lateral drift toward the aim line (does not add speed
//! pulses) + Item #5 irregular sample-time gaps (not a fixed metronome) + Item #6
//! explicit end speed damp (last pixels slow). Few-pulse series #1â€“#6 complete.
//! Legacy `sample_gust` remains for tests; not the default path.

use crate::native::{cursor_pos, ensure_dpi, set_cursor_pos, sleep_ms, Point};
use crate::override_guard::aborted;
use rand::Rng;

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Algo {
    Gust,
    Bezier,
    Natural,
    Hybrid,
    Linear,
    MinJerk,
}

impl Algo {
    pub fn parse(s: &str) -> Self {
        match s.to_ascii_lowercase().as_str() {
            "gust" | "wind" => Algo::Gust,
            "bezier" => Algo::Bezier,
            "natural" => Algo::Natural,
            "hybrid" => Algo::Hybrid,
            "linear" => Algo::Linear,
            "minjerk" => Algo::MinJerk,
            _ => Algo::Gust,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Profile {
    Average,
    Granny,
    Gamer,
}

impl Profile {
    pub fn parse(s: &str) -> Self {
        match s.to_ascii_lowercase().as_str() {
            "granny" => Profile::Granny,
            "gamer" => Profile::Gamer,
            _ => Profile::Average,
        }
    }
}

/// Profile knobs that scale step count, delay, noise, and gust physics.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct ProfileKnobs {
    pub step_ms_mul: f64,
    pub steps_mul: f64,
    pub noise_mul: f64,
    pub curve_mul: f64,
    pub overshoot_p: f64,
    pub overshoot_amt: f64,
    pub gust_pull: f64,
    pub gust_noise: f64,
    pub gust_damp: f64,
    pub gust_max_step: f64,
    pub gust_near: f64,
}

pub fn profile_knobs(p: Profile) -> ProfileKnobs {
    match p {
        Profile::Granny => ProfileKnobs {
            step_ms_mul: 2.2,
            steps_mul: 1.35,
            noise_mul: 1.8,
            curve_mul: 1.55,
            overshoot_p: 0.72,
            overshoot_amt: 1.4,
            gust_pull: 0.42,
            gust_noise: 2.8,
            gust_damp: 0.78,
            gust_max_step: 11.0,
            gust_near: 28.0,
        },
        Profile::Gamer => ProfileKnobs {
            step_ms_mul: 0.55,
            steps_mul: 0.75,
            noise_mul: 0.45,
            curve_mul: 0.55,
            overshoot_p: 0.12,
            overshoot_amt: 0.6,
            gust_pull: 0.72,
            gust_noise: 0.9,
            gust_damp: 0.86,
            gust_max_step: 22.0,
            gust_near: 18.0,
        },
        Profile::Average => ProfileKnobs {
            step_ms_mul: 1.0,
            steps_mul: 1.0,
            noise_mul: 1.0,
            curve_mul: 1.0,
            overshoot_p: 0.35,
            overshoot_amt: 1.0,
            gust_pull: 0.55,
            gust_noise: 1.8,
            gust_damp: 0.82,
            gust_max_step: 16.0,
            gust_near: 24.0,
        },
    }
}

/// Planned sample count and per-step delay (ms) for a move of `dist` pixels.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct StepPlan {
    pub n_steps: i32,
    pub step_ms: i32,
}

/// Default Fitts intercept `a` (ms): floor time for a zero-distance / trivial aim.
pub const FITTS_A_MS: f64 = 80.0;
/// Default Fitts slope `b` (ms per bit of index of difficulty).
pub const FITTS_B_MS: f64 = 60.0;
/// Default target width W (px) when the caller does not name a control size.
/// Clicks on typical UI chrome are often ~16â€“32 px; 24 is the mid default.
pub const DEFAULT_TARGET_WIDTH_PX: f64 = 24.0;
/// Clamp floor for planned move duration (ms).
pub const FITTS_MIN_MS: i32 = 40;
/// Clamp ceiling for planned move duration (ms).
pub const FITTS_MAX_MS: i32 = 2500;

/// Fitts-style duration: `T = a + b * log2(D/W + 1)` (milliseconds).
///
/// Clamps: `D < 0` â†’ 0; `W <= 0` â†’ [`DEFAULT_TARGET_WIDTH_PX`]; result to
/// [`FITTS_MIN_MS`]..=[`FITTS_MAX_MS`]. No NaN/inf (non-finite inputs use the floor).
pub fn fitts_duration_ms(dist_px: f64, width_px: f64, a_ms: f64, b_ms: f64) -> i32 {
    let d = if dist_px.is_finite() && dist_px > 0.0 {
        dist_px
    } else {
        0.0
    };
    let w = if width_px.is_finite() && width_px > 0.0 {
        width_px
    } else {
        DEFAULT_TARGET_WIDTH_PX
    };
    let a = if a_ms.is_finite() { a_ms.max(0.0) } else { FITTS_A_MS };
    let b = if b_ms.is_finite() { b_ms.max(0.0) } else { FITTS_B_MS };
    // log2(D/W + 1); argument is always >= 1 so log2 is finite and >= 0.
    let id = (d / w + 1.0).log2();
    let t = a + b * id;
    if !t.is_finite() {
        return FITTS_MIN_MS;
    }
    t.round().clamp(FITTS_MIN_MS as f64, FITTS_MAX_MS as f64) as i32
}

/// Profile-scaled Fitts `a`/`b`: granny slower, gamer faster (duration only; no pulse physics).
pub fn fitts_ab_for_profile(prof: Profile) -> (f64, f64) {
    let (am, bm) = match prof {
        Profile::Granny => (1.35, 1.35),
        Profile::Gamer => (0.70, 0.70),
        Profile::Average => (1.0, 1.0),
    };
    (FITTS_A_MS * am, FITTS_B_MS * bm)
}

/// Fitts duration for `dist` px using the default target width and profile-scaled a/b.
pub fn fitts_duration_for_profile(prof: Profile, dist_px: f64) -> i32 {
    let (a, b) = fitts_ab_for_profile(prof);
    fitts_duration_ms(dist_px, DEFAULT_TARGET_WIDTH_PX, a, b)
}

/// Shape exponents for the asymmetric ballistic speed bell (Beta-like).
/// Mode = (A-1)/(A+B-2) â‰ˆ 0.255 â€” faster rise, softer fall (peak before midpoint).
const BALLISTIC_SHAPE_A: f64 = 2.2;
const BALLISTIC_SHAPE_B: f64 = 4.5;

/// Normalized-time peak of [`ballistic_bell`] (must be < 0.5 for asymmetric rise/fall).
pub fn ballistic_peak_u() -> f64 {
    (BALLISTIC_SHAPE_A - 1.0) / (BALLISTIC_SHAPE_A + BALLISTIC_SHAPE_B - 2.0)
}

/// Unnormalized asymmetric ballistic **speed** shape on u âˆˆ [0, 1].
/// Zero at the endpoints; single peak before the midpoint (faster rise, softer fall).
pub fn ballistic_bell(u: f64) -> f64 {
    if !(u.is_finite()) {
        return 0.0;
    }
    let u = u.clamp(0.0, 1.0);
    if u <= 0.0 || u >= 1.0 {
        return 0.0;
    }
    u.powf(BALLISTIC_SHAPE_A - 1.0) * (1.0 - u).powf(BALLISTIC_SHAPE_B - 1.0)
}

/// One sample along a few-pulse path (no cursor I/O).
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct BallisticSample {
    pub x: i32,
    pub y: i32,
    /// Normalized time within this pulse in (0, 1].
    pub u: f64,
    /// Along-track speed shape at u (bell Ã— amplitude); for pulse counting / tests.
    pub speed: f64,
    /// Sleep gap after this sample (ms). Same value as [`Self::dt_ms`] after item #5.
    pub delay_ms: i32,
    /// Irregular sample gap (ms) â€” item #5. Live path sleeps this (not a fixed metronome).
    pub dt_ms: i32,
    /// 0 = main ballistic, 1..=2 = corrective pulses (item #3).
    pub pulse_index: u8,
    /// Signed offset (px) along the overall aim-line perpendicular after item #4 drift.
    pub lateral_px: f64,
}

/// Fraction of Fitts `n_steps` reserved as a correction tail (shared by 0â€“2 pulses).
/// Ballistic uses the rest. Documented choice: corrections share a reserved tail,
/// they do not extend past the Fitts step budget.
pub const CORRECT_TAIL_FRAC: f64 = 0.22;
/// Residual error (px) at/below which we emit **0** corrective pulses (total = 1).
pub const CORRECT_ERR_EPS_PX: f64 = 3.0;
/// After the first correction, only fire a second if residual still exceeds this.
pub const CORRECT2_ERR_EPS_PX: f64 = 2.0;
/// Ballistic intentional undershoot as a fraction of D (clamped).
pub const BALLISTIC_UNDERSHOOT_FRAC: f64 = 0.10;
pub const BALLISTIC_UNDERSHOOT_MIN_PX: f64 = 6.0;
pub const BALLISTIC_UNDERSHOOT_MAX_PX: f64 = 36.0;

/// Mean-reversion strength per sample toward the aim line (item #4).
pub const DRIFT_REVERT: f64 = 0.32;
/// Base Gaussian kick (px) before Profile `noise_mul` (item #4).
pub const DRIFT_NOISE_PX: f64 = 0.65;
/// Cap |lateral| as a fraction of path distance (then also absolute cap).
pub const DRIFT_MAX_FRAC: f64 = 0.045;
pub const DRIFT_MAX_PX: f64 = 16.0;

/// Relative jitter for irregular gaps before renormalize (Â± this fraction of mean step).
pub const GAP_JITTER_FRAC: f64 = 0.38;
/// Clamp for a single sample gap (ms) after jitter + renormalize.
pub const GAP_MIN_MS: i32 = 1;
pub const GAP_MAX_MS: i32 = 48;

/// Fraction of samples (from the end) covered by explicit end damp (item #6).
pub const END_DAMP_FRAC: f64 = 0.20;
/// Speed scale at the final sample after end damp (soft floor; not zero).
pub const END_DAMP_MIN_SCALE: f64 = 0.25;

/// Integrate one asymmetric velocity pulse along the straight aim line.
///
/// `speed_scale` scales the bell (corrections < 1 so peaks stay smaller than ballistic).
/// `pulse_index` tags the sample. When `snap_end`, the last sample is exactly `(tx, ty)`.
fn sample_pulse_segment(
    x0: i32,
    y0: i32,
    tx: i32,
    ty: i32,
    n_steps: i32,
    step_ms: i32,
    speed_scale: f64,
    pulse_index: u8,
    snap_end: bool,
) -> Vec<BallisticSample> {
    let dist = distance_i(x0, y0, tx, ty);
    if dist < 0.5 || n_steps <= 0 {
        return Vec::new();
    }
    let n = n_steps.max(1) as usize;
    let delay = step_ms.max(1);
    let scale = if speed_scale.is_finite() && speed_scale > 0.0 {
        speed_scale
    } else {
        1.0
    };
    let mut weights = Vec::with_capacity(n);
    let mut total_w = 0.0;
    for i in 1..=n {
        let u = i as f64 / n as f64;
        let w = ballistic_bell(u) * scale;
        weights.push((u, w));
        total_w += w;
    }
    if total_w <= 1e-18 {
        return vec![BallisticSample {
            x: tx,
            y: ty,
            u: 1.0,
            speed: 0.0,
            delay_ms: delay,
            dt_ms: delay,
            pulse_index,
            lateral_px: 0.0,
        }];
    }
    let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(n);
    let mut cum = 0.0;
    for (u, w) in weights {
        cum += w;
        let frac = (cum / total_w).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
        let x = x0 as f64 + (tx - x0) as f64 * frac;
        let y = y0 as f64 + (ty - y0) as f64 * frac;
        out.push(BallisticSample {
            x: x.round() as i32,
            y: y.round() as i32,
            u,
            speed: w,
            delay_ms: delay,
            dt_ms: delay,
            pulse_index,
            lateral_px: 0.0,
        });
    }
    if snap_end {
        if let Some(last) = out.last_mut() {
            last.x = tx;
            last.y = ty;
            last.u = 1.0;
        }
    }
    out
}

/// One ballistic pulse covering the full segment to `(tx, ty)` (item #2 helper / tests).
pub fn sample_ballistic_pulse(
    x0: i32,
    y0: i32,
    tx: i32,
    ty: i32,
    n_steps: i32,
    step_ms: i32,
) -> Vec<BallisticSample> {
    sample_pulse_segment(x0, y0, tx, ty, n_steps, step_ms, 1.0, 0, true)
}

/// Few-pulse path: ballistic + optional corrections + lateral drift (items #2â€“#4).
///
/// **Time budget:** Fitts `n_steps` from [`plan_steps`]. A reserved tail
/// ([`CORRECT_TAIL_FRAC`]) is for corrections; the ballistic uses the remainder.
/// Corrections never add steps beyond that budget.
///
/// **Geometry:** ballistic intentionally undershoots by ~10% of D (clamped). If the
/// residual â‰¤ [`CORRECT_ERR_EPS_PX`], emit 0 corrections (total pulses = 1). Else emit
/// 1 smaller pulse; a 2nd only if residual still exceeds [`CORRECT2_ERR_EPS_PX`].
///
/// **Lateral drift (#4):** after the on-line pulses are built, a small Ornsteinâ€“Uhlenbeck
/// offset in the aim-line perpendicular is applied (mean-reverts to 0). Scaled by
/// `noise_mul` (Profile). Envelope fades at the ends so the path returns toward the
/// line; final sample snaps to target. Drift does **not** change `speed` (no extra pulses).
///
/// **Irregular gaps (#5):** each sample gets a jittered `dt_ms` / `delay_ms` around the
/// Fitts-derived mean `step_ms`, then gaps are renormalized so
/// `sum(dt) â‰ˆ n_samples * step_ms` (planned Fitts slice for these samples). Not a fixed
/// metronome. The live wrapper sleeps each sample's `dt_ms`.
///
/// **End damp (#6):** after gaps, the last [`END_DAMP_FRAC`] of samples get an explicit
/// speed envelope (smoothstep down to [`END_DAMP_MIN_SCALE`]). Matching `dt` stretch
/// keeps wall-clock progress slow on the last pixels; dts are renormalized so the
/// Fitts sum budget is preserved. Does not add a velocity pulse.
///
/// `seed`: `Some(s)` for deterministic tests; `None` uses thread RNG in the live path.
/// Order: gaps â†’ end damp â†’ lateral drift.
pub fn sample_few_pulse_path(
    x0: i32,
    y0: i32,
    tx: i32,
    ty: i32,
    n_steps: i32,
    step_ms: i32,
    noise_mul: f64,
    seed: Option<u64>,
) -> Vec<BallisticSample> {
    let dist = distance_i(x0, y0, tx, ty);
    if dist < 1.5 || n_steps <= 0 {
        return Vec::new();
    }
    let n_steps = n_steps.max(6);
    let tail = ((n_steps as f64) * CORRECT_TAIL_FRAC)
        .round()
        .clamp(0.0, (n_steps - 4) as f64) as i32;
    let n_bal = (n_steps - tail).max(4);

    let undershoot = (dist * BALLISTIC_UNDERSHOOT_FRAC).clamp(
        BALLISTIC_UNDERSHOOT_MIN_PX,
        BALLISTIC_UNDERSHOOT_MAX_PX,
    );
    let cover = ((dist - undershoot) / dist).clamp(0.55, 0.995);
    let mx = (x0 as f64 + (tx - x0) as f64 * cover).round() as i32;
    let my = (y0 as f64 + (ty - y0) as f64 * cover).round() as i32;

    let mut out = sample_pulse_segment(x0, y0, mx, my, n_bal, step_ms, 1.0, 0, true);
    let mut cx = out.last().map(|s| s.x).unwrap_or(x0);
    let mut cy = out.last().map(|s| s.y).unwrap_or(y0);
    let mut rem = tail;
    let mut corr_i: u8 = 0;

    while corr_i < 2 && rem >= 2 {
        let err = distance_i(cx, cy, tx, ty);
        let eps = if corr_i == 0 {
            CORRECT_ERR_EPS_PX
        } else {
            CORRECT2_ERR_EPS_PX
        };
        if err <= eps {
            break;
        }
        let n_this = if corr_i == 0 && rem >= 4 {
            let take = ((rem as f64) * 0.62).round() as i32;
            take.clamp(2, rem)
        } else {
            rem
        };
        let amp = (err / dist).clamp(0.12, 0.55);
        let last_corr = corr_i == 1 || n_this >= rem;
        let (ax, ay, snap) = if last_corr || err <= CORRECT_ERR_EPS_PX * 2.0 {
            (tx, ty, true)
        } else {
            let c2 = ((err - CORRECT2_ERR_EPS_PX.max(1.0)) / err).clamp(0.5, 0.95);
            let ax = (cx as f64 + (tx - cx) as f64 * c2).round() as i32;
            let ay = (cy as f64 + (ty - cy) as f64 * c2).round() as i32;
            (ax, ay, true)
        };
        let pulse = sample_pulse_segment(
            cx,
            cy,
            ax,
            ay,
            n_this,
            step_ms,
            amp,
            corr_i + 1,
            snap,
        );
        if pulse.is_empty() {
            break;
        }
        cx = pulse.last().unwrap().x;
        cy = pulse.last().unwrap().y;
        rem -= n_this;
        corr_i += 1;
        out.extend(pulse);
    }

    if let Some(last) = out.last_mut() {
        last.x = tx;
        last.y = ty;
    }

    // Items #5 â†’ #6 â†’ #4: irregular gaps, end damp, then lateral drift.
    match seed {
        Some(s) => {
            use rand::rngs::StdRng;
            use rand::SeedableRng;
            let mut rng = StdRng::seed_from_u64(s);
            apply_irregular_gaps(&mut out, step_ms, &mut rng);
            apply_end_damp(&mut out);
            apply_lateral_drift(&mut out, x0, y0, tx, ty, noise_mul, &mut rng);
        }
        None => {
            let mut rng = rand::thread_rng();
            apply_irregular_gaps(&mut out, step_ms, &mut rng);
            apply_end_damp(&mut out);
            apply_lateral_drift(&mut out, x0, y0, tx, ty, noise_mul, &mut rng);
        }
    }
    out
}

/// Assign irregular per-sample gaps around `mean_step_ms`, renormalized to the Fitts slice.
///
/// **Budget rule:** target total = `n_samples * mean_step_ms` (the Fitts-derived mean step
/// times how many samples this path actually emitted). Raw gaps are
/// `mean * (1 Â± GAP_JITTER_FRAC * U[-1,1])`, then scaled so the sum matches the target
/// (clamped per sample to [`GAP_MIN_MS`]..=[`GAP_MAX_MS`]). Rounding may leave a few ms
/// of error vs the exact target.
pub fn apply_irregular_gaps(
    samples: &mut [BallisticSample],
    mean_step_ms: i32,
    rng: &mut impl Rng,
) {
    if samples.is_empty() {
        return;
    }
    let mean = mean_step_ms.max(1) as f64;
    let n = samples.len();
    let target_total = mean * n as f64;
    let mut raw = Vec::with_capacity(n);
    for _ in 0..n {
        let u = rng.gen::<f64>() * 2.0 - 1.0; // [-1, 1]
        let mul = (1.0 + GAP_JITTER_FRAC * u).max(0.22);
        raw.push(mean * mul);
    }
    let sum: f64 = raw.iter().sum();
    let scale = if sum > 1e-9 {
        target_total / sum
    } else {
        1.0
    };
    for (s, r) in samples.iter_mut().zip(raw.iter()) {
        let dt = (r * scale)
            .round()
            .clamp(GAP_MIN_MS as f64, GAP_MAX_MS as f64) as i32;
        let dt = dt.max(1);
        s.delay_ms = dt;
        s.dt_ms = dt;
    }
}

/// Explicit end speed damp (item #6): last pixels are slow.
///
/// Scales `speed` on the last [`END_DAMP_FRAC`] of samples from 1 â†’ [`END_DAMP_MIN_SCALE`]
/// with a smoothstep. Stretches `dt_ms` by the inverse scale so wall-clock travel on those
/// samples is slower, then renormalizes all gaps to keep `sum(dt) â‰ˆ` pre-damp total
/// (Fitts slice preserved). Does not invent a new speed pulse.
pub fn apply_end_damp(samples: &mut [BallisticSample]) {
    let n = samples.len();
    if n < 5 {
        return;
    }
    let damp_n = ((n as f64) * END_DAMP_FRAC).round().clamp(2.0, (n - 2) as f64) as usize;
    let start = n - damp_n;
    let pre_sum: i64 = samples.iter().map(|s| s.dt_ms as i64).sum();

    for i in start..n {
        let t = (i - start) as f64 / (damp_n - 1).max(1) as f64;
        // Smoothstep ease-in of damp: scale goes 1 â†’ MIN.
        let e = t * t * (3.0 - 2.0 * t);
        let scale = 1.0 + (END_DAMP_MIN_SCALE - 1.0) * e;
        let scale = scale.clamp(END_DAMP_MIN_SCALE, 1.0);
        samples[i].speed *= scale;
        let stretch = (1.0 / scale).clamp(1.0, 1.0 / END_DAMP_MIN_SCALE);
        let dt = ((samples[i].dt_ms as f64) * stretch)
            .round()
            .clamp(GAP_MIN_MS as f64, GAP_MAX_MS as f64) as i32;
        let dt = dt.max(1);
        samples[i].dt_ms = dt;
        samples[i].delay_ms = dt;
    }

    // Renormalize gaps to preserve the pre-damp Fitts-ish total (within clamp).
    let post_sum: i64 = samples.iter().map(|s| s.dt_ms as i64).sum();
    if post_sum > 0 && pre_sum > 0 && post_sum != pre_sum {
        let scale = pre_sum as f64 / post_sum as f64;
        for s in samples.iter_mut() {
            let dt = ((s.dt_ms as f64) * scale)
                .round()
                .clamp(GAP_MIN_MS as f64, GAP_MAX_MS as f64) as i32;
            let dt = dt.max(1);
            s.dt_ms = dt;
            s.delay_ms = dt;
        }
    }
}

/// Small OU lateral offset toward the overall aim line. Leaves `speed` unchanged.
pub fn apply_lateral_drift(
    samples: &mut [BallisticSample],
    x0: i32,
    y0: i32,
    tx: i32,
    ty: i32,
    noise_mul: f64,
    rng: &mut impl Rng,
) {
    if samples.is_empty() {
        return;
    }
    let dx = (tx - x0) as f64;
    let dy = (ty - y0) as f64;
    let dist = (dx * dx + dy * dy).sqrt();
    if dist < 1.5 {
        return;
    }
    let (_, _, px, py) = unit_perp(dx, dy, dist);
    let nmul = if noise_mul.is_finite() {
        noise_mul.clamp(0.0, 3.0)
    } else {
        1.0
    };
    let noise_amp = DRIFT_NOISE_PX * nmul;
    let max_off = (dist * DRIFT_MAX_FRAC)
        .clamp(2.0, DRIFT_MAX_PX)
        * (0.55 + 0.45 * nmul.clamp(0.0, 1.5));
    if noise_amp < 1e-9 {
        for s in samples.iter_mut() {
            s.lateral_px = 0.0;
        }
        if let Some(last) = samples.last_mut() {
            last.x = tx;
            last.y = ty;
            last.lateral_px = 0.0;
        }
        return;
    }
    let n = samples.len();
    let mut offset = 0.0_f64;
    for (i, s) in samples.iter_mut().enumerate() {
        let progress = (i + 1) as f64 / n as f64;
        // Fade at ends: mid-move envelope peaks at progress=0.5.
        let envelope = (4.0 * progress * (1.0 - progress)).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
        offset += -DRIFT_REVERT * offset + gauss(rng) * noise_amp;
        offset = offset.clamp(-max_off, max_off);
        let lat = offset * envelope;
        s.lateral_px = lat;
        s.x = (s.x as f64 + px * lat).round() as i32;
        s.y = (s.y as f64 + py * lat).round() as i32;
    }
    if let Some(last) = samples.last_mut() {
        last.x = tx;
        last.y = ty;
        last.lateral_px = 0.0;
    }
}

/// Count local maxima in along-track `speed`. Few-pulse paths â†’ 1..=3 on typical moves.
pub fn count_speed_pulses(samples: &[BallisticSample]) -> usize {
    if samples.is_empty() {
        return 0;
    }
    if samples.len() == 1 {
        return if samples[0].speed > 0.0 { 1 } else { 0 };
    }
    let mut n = 0usize;
    for i in 0..samples.len() {
        let s = samples[i].speed;
        let left = if i == 0 {
            f64::NEG_INFINITY
        } else {
            samples[i - 1].speed
        };
        let right = if i + 1 >= samples.len() {
            f64::NEG_INFINITY
        } else {
            samples[i + 1].speed
        };
        if s > left && s > right && s > 0.0 {
            n += 1;
        } else if s > left && (s - right).abs() < 1e-15 && s > 0.0 {
            // plateau start
            if i == 0 || samples[i - 1].speed < s - 1e-15 {
                n += 1;
            }
        }
    }
    n
}

/// Max speed among samples with the given `pulse_index` (0 if none).
pub fn pulse_peak_speed(samples: &[BallisticSample], pulse_index: u8) -> f64 {
    samples
        .iter()
        .filter(|s| s.pulse_index == pulse_index)
        .map(|s| s.speed)
        .fold(0.0_f64, f64::max)
}

/// Auto step count before profile mul: `round(18 + dist/45)` clamped to 12..=48.
pub fn auto_base_steps(dist: f64) -> i32 {
    (18.0 + dist / 45.0).round().clamp(12.0, 48.0) as i32
}

/// Step count + delay.
///
/// - `steps > 0` / `step_ms > 0` still override those sides (with profile mul on the override).
/// - When `step_ms == 0` (the click/humove default), total move time comes from Fitts
///   (`a + b*log2(D/W+1)` with default W), then `step_ms = total_ms / n_steps` (min 1).
/// - When `steps == 0`, `n_steps` still comes from [`auto_base_steps`] Ã— profile.
pub fn plan_steps(prof: Profile, dist: f64, steps: i32, step_ms: i32) -> StepPlan {
    let k = profile_knobs(prof);
    let base_steps = if steps > 0 {
        steps
    } else {
        auto_base_steps(dist)
    };
    let n_steps = ((base_steps as f64) * k.steps_mul).round().max(6.0) as i32;
    let ms = if step_ms > 0 {
        ((step_ms as f64) * k.step_ms_mul).round().max(1.0) as i32
    } else {
        let total = fitts_duration_for_profile(prof, dist);
        (total as f64 / n_steps as f64).round().max(1.0) as i32
    };
    StepPlan {
        n_steps,
        step_ms: ms,
    }
}

fn resolve_steps_ms(prof: Profile, dist: f64, steps: i32, step_ms: i32) -> (i32, i32, ProfileKnobs) {
    let plan = plan_steps(prof, dist, steps, step_ms);
    (plan.n_steps, plan.step_ms, profile_knobs(prof))
}

/// Euclidean distance between two points.
pub fn distance(x0: f64, y0: f64, x1: f64, y1: f64) -> f64 {
    let dx = x1 - x0;
    let dy = y1 - y0;
    (dx * dx + dy * dy).sqrt()
}

pub fn distance_i(x0: i32, y0: i32, x1: i32, y1: i32) -> f64 {
    distance(x0 as f64, y0 as f64, x1 as f64, y1 as f64)
}

/// Sum of segment lengths. Empty / single-point paths are 0.
pub fn polyline_length(pts: &[(i32, i32)]) -> f64 {
    pts.windows(2)
        .map(|w| distance_i(w[0].0, w[0].1, w[1].0, w[1].1))
        .sum()
}

/// Hermite smoothstep `tÂ²(3-2t)` used by bezier / linear / natural.
pub fn smoothstep(t: f64) -> f64 {
    t * t * (3.0 - 2.0 * t)
}

/// Minimum-jerk ease `10tÂ³ - 15tâ´ + 6tâµ`.
pub fn min_jerk_ease(t: f64) -> f64 {
    let t2 = t * t;
    let t3 = t2 * t;
    let t4 = t3 * t;
    let t5 = t4 * t;
    10.0 * t3 - 15.0 * t4 + 6.0 * t5
}

/// Unit vector along `(dx,dy)` and a perpendicular. `dist` must be > 0.
pub fn unit_perp(dx: f64, dy: f64, dist: f64) -> (f64, f64, f64, f64) {
    let inv = 1.0 / dist;
    let ux = dx * inv;
    let uy = dy * inv;
    (ux, uy, -uy, ux)
}

/// Cubic BÃ©zier at eased parameter `e` (typically `smoothstep(t)`).
pub fn cubic_bezier_2d(
    e: f64,
    p0: (f64, f64),
    c1: (f64, f64),
    c2: (f64, f64),
    p3: (f64, f64),
) -> (f64, f64) {
    let u = 1.0 - e;
    let nx = u * u * u * p0.0 + 3.0 * u * u * e * c1.0 + 3.0 * u * e * e * c2.0 + e * e * e * p3.0;
    let ny = u * u * u * p0.1 + 3.0 * u * u * e * c1.1 + 3.0 * u * e * e * c2.1 + e * e * e * p3.1;
    (nx, ny)
}

/// Unsigned curve magnitude before side sign. Clamped 14..=90 times `curve_mul`.
pub fn bezier_curve_mag(dist: f64, curve_mul: f64) -> f64 {
    (dist * 0.13).clamp(14.0, 90.0) * curve_mul
}

/// Deterministic bow side from integer parity of the four coordinates.
pub fn bezier_side(x0: f64, y0: f64, tx: f64, ty: f64) -> f64 {
    if ((x0 + y0 + tx + ty) as i32) & 1 == 0 {
        1.0
    } else {
        -1.0
    }
}

pub fn overshoot_eligible(dist: f64) -> bool {
    dist >= 160.0
}

/// Extra pixels past the target when overshoot is taken (caller still gates RNG + eligibility).
pub fn bezier_overshoot_amt(dist: f64, overshoot_amt: f64) -> f64 {
    (dist * 0.02).clamp(4.0, 14.0) * overshoot_amt
}

/// Per-step sleep multiplier for bezier (`t` in 0..=1).
pub fn bezier_speed_mul(t: f64) -> f64 {
    let mut speed = 1.30 - 0.65 * (std::f64::consts::PI * t).sin();
    if t > 0.82 {
        speed = speed.max(1.55);
    }
    speed
}

/// Sample cubic-bezier pointer path. No cursor I/O.
///
/// Returns `n_steps` rounded points (`i = 1..=n_steps`). Empty if `dist < 1.5` or `n_steps <= 0`.
/// `overshoot_amt` is the extra pixels along the unit vector (0 = land on target).
pub fn sample_bezier(
    x0: i32,
    y0: i32,
    tx: i32,
    ty: i32,
    n_steps: i32,
    curve_mul: f64,
    overshoot_amt: f64,
) -> Vec<(i32, i32)> {
    let x0f = x0 as f64;
    let y0f = y0 as f64;
    let dist = distance(x0f, y0f, tx as f64, ty as f64);
    if dist < 1.5 || n_steps <= 0 {
        return Vec::new();
    }
    let (ux, uy, px, py) = unit_perp(tx as f64 - x0f, ty as f64 - y0f, dist);
    let curve_mag = bezier_curve_mag(dist, curve_mul) * bezier_side(x0f, y0f, tx as f64, ty as f64);
    let end_x = tx as f64 + ux * overshoot_amt;
    let end_y = ty as f64 + uy * overshoot_amt;
    let edx = end_x - x0f;
    let edy = end_y - y0f;
    let c1x = x0f + edx * 0.28 + px * curve_mag;
    let c1y = y0f + edy * 0.28 + py * curve_mag;
    let c2x = x0f + edx * 0.72 + px * (curve_mag * 0.55);
    let c2y = y0f + edy * 0.72 + py * (curve_mag * 0.55);
    let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(n_steps as usize);
    for i in 1..=n_steps {
        let t = i as f64 / n_steps as f64;
        let e = smoothstep(t);
        let (nx, ny) = cubic_bezier_2d(
            e,
            (x0f, y0f),
            (c1x, c1y),
            (c2x, c2y),
            (end_x, end_y),
        );
        out.push((nx.round() as i32, ny.round() as i32));
    }
    out
}

/// Hard cap on gust physics iterations (then snap to target).
pub const GUST_MAX_ITERS: i32 = 1800;

pub fn gust_pull_scale(pull: f64, dist: f64) -> f64 {
    pull * (0.55 + 0.45 * (dist / 220.0).min(1.0))
}

pub fn gust_velocity_cap(dist: f64, max_step: f64, near: f64) -> f64 {
    if dist < near {
        (max_step * 0.35).max(2.5)
    } else {
        max_step
    }
}

/// One gust display frame. `dist` is remaining distance *before* the step (drives delay).
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct GustFrame {
    pub x: i32,
    pub y: i32,
    pub dist: f64,
    pub delay_ms: i32,
}

/// Simulate gust kinematics. No cursor I/O.
///
/// At most [`GUST_MAX_ITERS`] frames. Does not include the final snap to `(tx, ty)`.
pub fn sample_gust(
    x0: i32,
    y0: i32,
    tx: i32,
    ty: i32,
    k: ProfileKnobs,
    rng: &mut impl Rng,
    ms: i32,
) -> Vec<GustFrame> {
    let mut frames = Vec::new();
    let mut cx = x0 as f64;
    let mut cy = y0 as f64;
    let pull = k.gust_pull;
    let noise_base = k.gust_noise * k.noise_mul;
    let damp = k.gust_damp;
    let max_step = k.gust_max_step;
    let near = k.gust_near;
    let mut vx = 0.0;
    let mut vy = 0.0;
    let mut gust_x = 0.0;
    let mut gust_y = 0.0;
    for _ in 0..GUST_MAX_ITERS {
        let dx = tx as f64 - cx;
        let dy = ty as f64 - cy;
        let dist = (dx * dx + dy * dy).sqrt();
        if dist < 1.0 {
            break;
        }
        let (ux, uy, px, py) = unit_perp(dx, dy, dist);
        let pull_scale = gust_pull_scale(pull, dist);
        let ax = ux * pull_scale * dist.min(max_step);
        let ay = uy * pull_scale * dist.min(max_step);
        let fade = (dist / (near * 2.5).max(12.0)).min(1.0);
        let impulse_along = (rng.gen::<f64>() * 2.0 - 1.0) * noise_base * 0.35 * fade;
        let impulse_perp = (rng.gen::<f64>() * 2.0 - 1.0) * noise_base * fade;
        gust_x = 0.72 * gust_x + 0.28 * (ux * impulse_along + px * impulse_perp);
        gust_y = 0.72 * gust_y + 0.28 * (uy * impulse_along + py * impulse_perp);
        vx = (vx + ax + gust_x) * damp;
        vy = (vy + ay + gust_y) * damp;
        let vmag = (vx * vx + vy * vy).sqrt();
        let cap = gust_velocity_cap(dist, max_step, near);
        if vmag > cap && vmag > 1e-4 {
            vx *= cap / vmag;
            vy *= cap / vmag;
        }
        cx += vx;
        cy += vy;
        let delay = if dist > 90.0 {
            ms
        } else {
            ((ms as f64) * 1.4).round().max(1.0) as i32
        };
        frames.push(GustFrame {
            x: cx.round() as i32,
            y: cy.round() as i32,
            dist,
            delay_ms: delay,
        });
    }
    frames
}

/// Gust sample with a fixed RNG seed (deterministic unit tests).
pub fn sample_gust_seeded(
    x0: i32,
    y0: i32,
    tx: i32,
    ty: i32,
    k: ProfileKnobs,
    seed: u64,
    ms: i32,
) -> Vec<GustFrame> {
    use rand::rngs::StdRng;
    use rand::SeedableRng;
    let mut rng = StdRng::seed_from_u64(seed);
    sample_gust(x0, y0, tx, ty, k, &mut rng, ms)
}

fn gauss(rng: &mut impl Rng) -> f64 {
    let u1 = rng.gen::<f64>().max(1e-12);
    let u2 = rng.gen::<f64>();
    (-2.0 * u1.ln()).sqrt() * (2.0 * std::f64::consts::PI * u2).cos()
}

/// Move cursor to (tx, ty) with the chosen human-like algorithm.
pub fn humove(tx: i32, ty: i32, algo: Algo, profile: Profile, steps: i32, step_ms: i32) {
    if aborted() {
        return;
    }
    ensure_dpi();
    let p = cursor_pos();
    let dist = distance(p.x as f64, p.y as f64, tx as f64, ty as f64);
    let (n_steps, ms, k) = resolve_steps_ms(profile, dist, steps, step_ms);
    match algo {
        // Default: few-pulse #1â€“#6 (Fitts, ballistic, corrections, drift, gaps, end damp).
        Algo::Gust => move_ballistic(tx, ty, n_steps, ms, k.noise_mul),
        Algo::Bezier => move_bezier(tx, ty, n_steps, ms, k),
        Algo::Natural => move_natural(tx, ty, n_steps, ms, k),
        Algo::Hybrid => move_hybrid(tx, ty, n_steps, ms, k),
        Algo::Linear => move_linear(tx, ty, n_steps, ms),
        Algo::MinJerk => move_minjerk(tx, ty, n_steps, ms, k),
    }
}

/// Default motor path: few-pulse series #1â€“#6 complete.
fn move_ballistic(tx: i32, ty: i32, n_steps: i32, step_ms: i32, noise_mul: f64) {
    let p = cursor_pos();
    let samples = sample_few_pulse_path(p.x, p.y, tx, ty, n_steps, step_ms, noise_mul, None);
    if samples.is_empty() {
        set_cursor_pos(tx, ty);
        return;
    }
    for s in samples {
        if aborted() {
            return;
        }
        set_cursor_pos(s.x, s.y);
        // Item #5: sleep the irregular per-sample gap (dt_ms == delay_ms).
        sleep_ms(s.dt_ms.max(1) as u64);
    }
    // Residual snap only â€” not counted as a velocity pulse.
    set_cursor_pos(tx, ty);
}

/// Legacy gust I/O wrapper (kept for callers/tests that sample gust kinematics directly).
#[allow(dead_code)]
fn move_gust(tx: i32, ty: i32, ms: i32, k: ProfileKnobs) {
    let mut rng = rand::thread_rng();
    let p = cursor_pos();
    for f in sample_gust(p.x, p.y, tx, ty, k, &mut rng, ms) {
        if aborted() {
            return;
        }
        set_cursor_pos(f.x, f.y);
        sleep_ms(f.delay_ms as u64);
    }
    set_cursor_pos(tx, ty);
}

fn move_bezier(tx: i32, ty: i32, n_steps: i32, ms: i32, k: ProfileKnobs) {
    let mut rng = rand::thread_rng();
    let p = cursor_pos();
    let dist = distance_i(p.x, p.y, tx, ty);
    if dist < 1.5 {
        set_cursor_pos(tx, ty);
        return;
    }
    let do_overshoot = overshoot_eligible(dist) && rng.gen::<f64>() < k.overshoot_p;
    let o_amt = if do_overshoot {
        bezier_overshoot_amt(dist, k.overshoot_amt)
    } else {
        0.0
    };
    let pts = sample_bezier(p.x, p.y, tx, ty, n_steps, k.curve_mul, o_amt);
    for (idx, &(nx, ny)) in pts.iter().enumerate() {
        if aborted() {
            return;
        }
        set_cursor_pos(nx, ny);
        let t = (idx + 1) as f64 / n_steps.max(1) as f64;
        sleep_ms(((ms as f64) * bezier_speed_mul(t)).round().max(1.0) as u64);
    }
    if do_overshoot {
        let settle = (n_steps / 4).clamp(4, 8);
        let cur = cursor_pos();
        for i in 1..=settle {
            if aborted() {
                return;
            }
            let t = i as f64 / settle as f64;
            let e = smoothstep(t);
            let nx = cur.x as f64 + (tx - cur.x) as f64 * e;
            let ny = cur.y as f64 + (ty - cur.y) as f64 * e;
            set_cursor_pos(nx.round() as i32, ny.round() as i32);
            sleep_ms(((ms as f64) * 1.25).round().max(6.0) as u64);
        }
    }
    set_cursor_pos(tx, ty);
}

fn move_natural(tx: i32, ty: i32, n_steps: i32, ms: i32, k: ProfileKnobs) {
    let mut rng = rand::thread_rng();
    let p = cursor_pos();
    let x0 = p.x as f64;
    let y0 = p.y as f64;
    let dist = distance(x0, y0, tx as f64, ty as f64);
    if dist < 1.5 {
        set_cursor_pos(tx, ty);
        return;
    }
    let (ux, uy, px, py) = unit_perp(tx as f64 - x0, ty as f64 - y0, dist);
    let mut arc = (dist * 0.16).clamp(18.0, 110.0) * k.curve_mul;
    if rng.gen::<f64>() < 0.5 {
        arc = -arc;
    }
    let noise_amp = (2.2 * k.noise_mul).max(0.4);
    let do_overshoot = overshoot_eligible(dist) && rng.gen::<f64>() < k.overshoot_p;
    let o_amt = if do_overshoot {
        (dist * 0.025).clamp(5.0, 16.0) * k.overshoot_amt
    } else {
        0.0
    };
    let end_x = tx as f64 + ux * o_amt;
    let end_y = ty as f64 + uy * o_amt;
    for i in 1..=n_steps {
        if aborted() {
            return;
        }
        let t = i as f64 / n_steps as f64;
        let e = smoothstep(t);
        let bow = (std::f64::consts::PI * e).sin() * arc;
        let mut nx = x0 + (end_x - x0) * e + px * bow;
        let mut ny = y0 + (end_y - y0) * e + py * bow;
        if t < 0.95 {
            nx += (rng.gen::<f64>() * 2.0 - 1.0) * noise_amp;
            ny += (rng.gen::<f64>() * 2.0 - 1.0) * noise_amp;
        }
        set_cursor_pos(nx.round() as i32, ny.round() as i32);
        let mut speed = 1.25 - 0.55 * (std::f64::consts::PI * t).sin();
        if t > 0.85 {
            speed = speed.max(1.4);
        }
        sleep_ms(((ms as f64) * speed).round().max(1.0) as u64);
    }
    set_cursor_pos(tx, ty);
}

fn move_minjerk(tx: i32, ty: i32, n_steps: i32, ms: i32, k: ProfileKnobs) {
    let mut rng = rand::thread_rng();
    let p = cursor_pos();
    let x0 = p.x as f64;
    let y0 = p.y as f64;
    let dx = tx as f64 - x0;
    let dy = ty as f64 - y0;
    let dist = distance(x0, y0, tx as f64, ty as f64);
    if dist < 1.5 {
        set_cursor_pos(tx, ty);
        return;
    }
    let (_, _, px, py) = unit_perp(dx, dy, dist);
    let side = if rng.gen::<f64>() < 0.5 { 1.0 } else { -1.0 };
    let curve = (dist * 0.10).clamp(10.0, 70.0) * k.curve_mul * side;
    let c1x = x0 + dx * 0.3 + px * curve;
    let c1y = y0 + dy * 0.3 + py * curve;
    let c2x = x0 + dx * 0.7 + px * (curve * 0.4);
    let c2y = y0 + dy * 0.7 + py * (curve * 0.4);
    let tamp = k.noise_mul;
    for i in 1..=n_steps {
        if aborted() {
            return;
        }
        let t = i as f64 / n_steps as f64;
        let e = min_jerk_ease(t);
        let (mut bx, mut by) =
            cubic_bezier_2d(e, (x0, y0), (c1x, c1y), (c2x, c2y), (tx as f64, ty as f64));
        if t < 0.97 {
            bx += gauss(&mut rng) * tamp * 0.35;
            by += gauss(&mut rng) * tamp * 0.35;
        }
        set_cursor_pos(bx.round() as i32, by.round() as i32);
        sleep_ms(ms as u64);
    }
    set_cursor_pos(tx, ty);
}

fn move_hybrid(tx: i32, ty: i32, n_steps: i32, ms: i32, k: ProfileKnobs) {
    let mut rng = rand::thread_rng();
    let p = cursor_pos();
    let x0 = p.x as f64;
    let y0 = p.y as f64;
    let dx = tx as f64 - x0;
    let dy = ty as f64 - y0;
    let dist = distance(x0, y0, tx as f64, ty as f64);
    if dist < 1.5 {
        set_cursor_pos(tx, ty);
        return;
    }
    let (ux, uy, _, _) = unit_perp(dx, dy, dist);
    let do_overshoot = overshoot_eligible(dist) && rng.gen::<f64>() < k.overshoot_p;
    let o_amt = if do_overshoot {
        (dist * 0.015).clamp(3.0, 10.0) * k.overshoot_amt
    } else {
        0.0
    };
    let mid_x = (x0 + dx * 0.85 + ux * o_amt * 0.3).round() as i32;
    let mid_y = (y0 + dy * 0.85 + uy * o_amt * 0.3).round() as i32;
    move_gust(mid_x, mid_y, ((ms as f64) * 0.9).round().max(1.0) as i32, k);
    if do_overshoot {
        let ox = (tx as f64 + ux * o_amt).round() as i32;
        let oy = (ty as f64 + uy * o_amt).round() as i32;
        let settle = ((n_steps as f64) * 0.35).round().max(8.0) as i32;
        move_minjerk(ox, oy, (settle / 2).max(4), ms, k);
    }
    let settle2 = ((n_steps as f64) * 0.4).round().max(8.0) as i32;
    move_minjerk(
        tx,
        ty,
        settle2,
        ((ms as f64) * 1.1).round().max(1.0) as i32,
        k,
    );
    set_cursor_pos(tx, ty);
}

fn move_linear(tx: i32, ty: i32, n_steps: i32, ms: i32) {
    let p = cursor_pos();
    let x0 = p.x as f64;
    let y0 = p.y as f64;
    for i in 1..=n_steps.max(1) {
        if aborted() {
            return;
        }
        let t = i as f64 / n_steps.max(1) as f64;
        let e = smoothstep(t);
        set_cursor_pos(
            (x0 + (tx as f64 - x0) * e).round() as i32,
            (y0 + (ty as f64 - y0) * e).round() as i32,
        );
        sleep_ms(ms.max(1) as u64);
    }
    set_cursor_pos(tx, ty);
}

#[allow(dead_code)]
pub fn current() -> Point {
    cursor_pos()
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn distance_3_4_5() {
        assert!((distance(0.0, 0.0, 3.0, 4.0) - 5.0).abs() < 1e-12);
        assert!((distance_i(100, 100, 100, 100)).abs() < 1e-12);
    }

    #[test]
    fn auto_base_steps_clamps() {
        // round(18 + dist/45) then clamp 12..=48. Non-negative dist is never below 18.
        assert_eq!(auto_base_steps(0.0), 18);
        assert_eq!(auto_base_steps(45.0), 19);
        assert_eq!(auto_base_steps(270.0), 24);
        assert_eq!(auto_base_steps(1350.0), 48);
        assert_eq!(auto_base_steps(10_000.0), 48);
        assert_eq!(auto_base_steps(-400.0), 12);
    }

    #[test]
    fn plan_steps_profile_and_override() {
        // Auto timing is Fitts-derived: step_ms = fitts_total / n_steps.
        let a0 = plan_steps(Profile::Average, 0.0, 0, 0);
        assert_eq!(a0.n_steps, 18);
        let t0 = fitts_duration_for_profile(Profile::Average, 0.0);
        assert_eq!(a0.step_ms, (t0 as f64 / 18.0).round().max(1.0) as i32);

        let g0 = plan_steps(Profile::Granny, 0.0, 0, 0);
        assert_eq!(g0.n_steps, 24); // 18 * 1.35 = 24.3 â†’ 24
        let tg = fitts_duration_for_profile(Profile::Granny, 0.0);
        assert_eq!(g0.step_ms, (tg as f64 / 24.0).round().max(1.0) as i32);

        let r0 = plan_steps(Profile::Gamer, 0.0, 0, 0);
        assert_eq!(r0.n_steps, 14); // 18 * 0.75 = 13.5 â†’ 14
        let tr = fitts_duration_for_profile(Profile::Gamer, 0.0);
        assert_eq!(r0.step_ms, (tr as f64 / 14.0).round().max(1.0) as i32);

        // Explicit step_ms still wins (profile-mul on the override).
        let ov = plan_steps(Profile::Granny, 100.0, 20, 10);
        assert_eq!(ov.n_steps, 27); // 20 * 1.35
        assert_eq!(ov.step_ms, 22); // 10 * 2.2
    }

    #[test]
    fn fitts_duration_grows_with_distance() {
        let w = DEFAULT_TARGET_WIDTH_PX;
        let t100 = fitts_duration_ms(100.0, w, FITTS_A_MS, FITTS_B_MS);
        let t400 = fitts_duration_ms(400.0, w, FITTS_A_MS, FITTS_B_MS);
        let t800 = fitts_duration_ms(800.0, w, FITTS_A_MS, FITTS_B_MS);
        assert!(t100 < t400, "{t100} vs {t400}");
        assert!(t400 < t800, "{t400} vs {t800}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn fitts_duration_shrinks_as_width_grows() {
        let t_narrow = fitts_duration_ms(400.0, 8.0, FITTS_A_MS, FITTS_B_MS);
        let t_wide = fitts_duration_ms(400.0, 64.0, FITTS_A_MS, FITTS_B_MS);
        assert!(t_narrow > t_wide, "narrow={t_narrow} wide={t_wide}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn fitts_profile_granny_slower_than_gamer() {
        let d = 400.0;
        let g = fitts_duration_for_profile(Profile::Granny, d);
        let a = fitts_duration_for_profile(Profile::Average, d);
        let r = fitts_duration_for_profile(Profile::Gamer, d);
        assert!(g >= a, "granny={g} average={a}");
        assert!(a >= r, "average={a} gamer={r}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn fitts_zero_dist_and_bad_inputs_are_sane() {
        let floor = fitts_duration_ms(0.0, DEFAULT_TARGET_WIDTH_PX, FITTS_A_MS, FITTS_B_MS);
        assert_eq!(floor, FITTS_A_MS.round() as i32);
        assert!(floor >= FITTS_MIN_MS);
        // W<=0 falls back to default width; D<0 â†’ 0.
        let bad_w = fitts_duration_ms(100.0, 0.0, FITTS_A_MS, FITTS_B_MS);
        let ok_w = fitts_duration_ms(100.0, DEFAULT_TARGET_WIDTH_PX, FITTS_A_MS, FITTS_B_MS);
        assert_eq!(bad_w, ok_w);
        let neg = fitts_duration_ms(-50.0, 24.0, FITTS_A_MS, FITTS_B_MS);
        assert_eq!(neg, floor);
        let nan = fitts_duration_ms(f64::NAN, f64::NAN, f64::NAN, f64::NAN);
        assert!(nan >= FITTS_MIN_MS && nan <= FITTS_MAX_MS);
        // Need ID high enough that a+b*ID exceeds the ceiling (1e12 is still under max).
        let huge = fitts_duration_ms(1.0e15, 1.0, FITTS_A_MS, FITTS_B_MS);
        assert_eq!(huge, FITTS_MAX_MS);
    }

    #[test]
    fn plan_steps_auto_uses_fitts_total() {
        let dist = 450.0;
        let plan = plan_steps(Profile::Average, dist, 0, 0);
        let total = fitts_duration_for_profile(Profile::Average, dist);
        let approx = plan.n_steps * plan.step_ms;
        // Rounding of total/n_steps can drift by ~n_steps ms.
        assert!(
            (approx - total).abs() <= plan.n_steps,
            "approx={approx} fitts={total} steps={} ms={}",
            plan.n_steps,
            plan.step_ms
        );
        // Explicit steps with auto step_ms still Fitts-times the move.
        let ov_steps = plan_steps(Profile::Average, dist, 30, 0);
        assert_eq!(ov_steps.n_steps, 30);
        let approx2 = ov_steps.n_steps * ov_steps.step_ms;
        assert!((approx2 - total).abs() <= ov_steps.n_steps);
    }

    #[test]
    fn bezier_sample_count_and_end() {
        let n = 24;
        let pts = sample_bezier(100, 100, 500, 300, n, 1.0, 0.0);
        assert_eq!(pts.len(), n as usize);
        let last = *pts.last().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(last, (500, 300));
        let mut with_start = vec![(100, 100)];
        with_start.extend(pts);
        let eu = distance_i(100, 100, 500, 300);
        let len = polyline_length(&with_start);
        assert!(len + 1e-6 >= eu, "curved path {len} shorter than euclid {eu}");
        assert!(len > eu * 1.01, "expected bow: len={len} eu={eu}");
    }

    #[test]

    }

    #[test]
    fn ease_endpoints() {
        assert!((smoothstep(0.0)).abs() < 1e-12);
        assert!((smoothstep(1.0) - 1.0).abs() < 1e-12);
        assert!((min_jerk_ease(0.0)).abs() < 1e-12);
        assert!((min_jerk_ease(1.0) - 1.0).abs() < 1e-12);
        assert!(min_jerk_ease(0.5) > 0.4 && min_jerk_ease(0.5) < 0.6);
    }

    #[test]
    fn algo_profile_parse() {
        assert_eq!(Algo::parse("wind"), Algo::Gust);
        assert_eq!(Algo::parse("MINJERK"), Algo::MinJerk);
        assert_eq!(Algo::parse("nope"), Algo::Gust);
        assert_eq!(Profile::parse("granny"), Profile::Granny);
        assert_eq!(Profile::parse("gamer"), Profile::Gamer);
        assert_eq!(Profile::parse(""), Profile::Average);
    }

    #[test]
    fn ballistic_bell_peaks_before_midpoint() {
        let peak_u = ballistic_peak_u();
        assert!(peak_u > 0.05 && peak_u < 0.5, "peak_u={peak_u}");
        // Discrete search on a fine grid.
        let mut best_u = 0.0;
        let mut best_v = -1.0;
        for i in 1..1000 {
            let u = i as f64 / 1000.0;
            let v = ballistic_bell(u);
            if v > best_v {
                best_v = v;
                best_u = u;
            }
        }
        assert!(best_u < 0.5, "discrete peak {best_u} not before midpoint");
        assert!((best_u - peak_u).abs() < 0.03, "grid={best_u} analytic={peak_u}");
        assert_eq!(ballistic_bell(0.0), 0.0);
        assert_eq!(ballistic_bell(1.0), 0.0);
        // Rise window shorter than fall window (asymmetric).
        assert!(peak_u < 1.0 - peak_u);
    }

    #[test]
    fn ballistic_pulse_exactly_one_on_typical_moves() {
        // Pure #2 helper (single segment) still emits exactly one pulse.
        for dist in [300_i32, 450, 600, 800] {
            let plan = plan_steps(Profile::Average, dist as f64, 0, 0);
            let samples =
                sample_ballistic_pulse(0, 0, dist, 0, plan.n_steps, plan.step_ms);
            assert!(!samples.is_empty(), "dist={dist}");
            let pulses = count_speed_pulses(&samples);
            assert_eq!(pulses, 1, "dist={dist} pulses={pulses} n={}", samples.len());
            let last = samples.last().unwrap();
            assert_eq!((last.x, last.y), (dist, 0), "must land on target");
            let imax = samples
                .iter()
                .enumerate()
                .max_by(|(_, a), (_, b)| a.speed.partial_cmp(&b.speed).unwrap())
                .map(|(i, _)| i)
                .unwrap();
            assert!(
                samples[imax].u < 0.5,
                "dist={dist} peak u={}",
                samples[imax].u
            );
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn few_pulse_count_one_to_three_on_typical_moves() {
        for dist in [300_i32, 450, 600, 800] {
            let plan = plan_steps(Profile::Average, dist as f64, 0, 0);
            let samples =
                sample_few_pulse_path(0, 0, dist, 0, plan.n_steps, plan.step_ms, 1.0, Some(7));
            assert!(!samples.is_empty(), "dist={dist}");
            let pulses = count_speed_pulses(&samples);
            assert!(
                (1..=3).contains(&pulses),
                "dist={dist} pulses={pulses} (want 1..=3)"
            );
            let last = samples.last().unwrap();
            assert_eq!((last.x, last.y), (dist, 0), "must land on target");
            let bal_peak = pulse_peak_speed(&samples, 0);
            assert!(bal_peak > 0.0, "dist={dist} missing ballistic");
            for pi in 1..=2u8 {
                let cpeak = pulse_peak_speed(&samples, pi);
                if cpeak > 0.0 {
                    assert!(
                        cpeak < bal_peak,
                        "dist={dist} correction {pi} peak {cpeak} >= ballistic {bal_peak}"
                    );
                }
            }
            // Step count stays within Fitts plan (no extra budget).
            assert!(
                samples.len() as i32 <= plan.n_steps,
                "dist={dist} samples={} plan={}",
                samples.len(),
                plan.n_steps
            );
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn few_pulse_skips_corrections_when_already_close() {

    }

    #[test]
    fn few_pulse_lateral_drift_mean_reverts_seeded() {
        let dist = 450_i32;
        let plan = plan_steps(Profile::Average, dist as f64, 0, 0);
        let samples = sample_few_pulse_path(
            0,
            0,
            dist,
            0,
            plan.n_steps,
            plan.step_ms,
            1.0,
            Some(42),
        );
        assert!(!samples.is_empty());
        let pulses = count_speed_pulses(&samples);
        assert!(
            (1..=3).contains(&pulses),
            "pulses={pulses}"
        );
        let last = samples.last().unwrap();
        assert_eq!((last.x, last.y), (dist, 0));
        assert_eq!(last.lateral_px, 0.0);

        let n = samples.len();
        let mid = &samples[n / 4 .. (3 * n) / 4];
        let mid_max = mid
            .iter()
            .map(|s| s.lateral_px.abs())
            .fold(0.0_f64, f64::max);
        assert!(
            mid_max > 0.4,
            "expected non-zero mid-move lateral, max={mid_max}"
        );
        let mid_mean: f64 =
            mid.iter().map(|s| s.lateral_px.abs()).sum::<f64>() / mid.len().max(1) as f64;
        let tail = &samples[(4 * n) / 5 ..];
        let tail_mean: f64 =
            tail.iter().map(|s| s.lateral_px.abs()).sum::<f64>() / tail.len().max(1) as f64;
        assert!(
            tail_mean < mid_mean,
            "drift should shrink toward the line near the end: mid={mid_mean} tail={tail_mean}"
        );

        // Zero noise_mul â†’ no lateral (and same pulse count class).
        let quiet = sample_few_pulse_path(
            0,
            0,
            dist,
            0,
            plan.n_steps,
            plan.step_ms,
            0.0,
            Some(42),
        );
        assert!(quiet.iter().all(|s| s.lateral_px.abs() < 1e-9));
        let pq = count_speed_pulses(&quiet);
        assert!((1..=3).contains(&pq));
        // Along-track speed peaks unchanged by drift (same seed path structure).
        assert_eq!(pulses, pq);
    }

    #[test]
    fn few_pulse_drift_scales_with_noise_mul() {
        let dist = 600_i32;
        let plan = plan_steps(Profile::Average, dist as f64, 0, 0);
        let soft = sample_few_pulse_path(
            0, 0, dist, 0, plan.n_steps, plan.step_ms, 0.45, Some(99),
        );
        let loud = sample_few_pulse_path(
            0, 0, dist, 0, plan.n_steps, plan.step_ms, 1.8, Some(99),
        );
        let soft_max = soft.iter().map(|s| s.lateral_px.abs()).fold(0.0, f64::max);
        let loud_max = loud.iter().map(|s| s.lateral_px.abs()).fold(0.0, f64::max);
        assert!(
            loud_max >= soft_max,
            "loud={loud_max} soft={soft_max}"
        );
        assert!((1..=3).contains(&count_speed_pulses(&soft)));
        assert!((1..=3).contains(&count_speed_pulses(&loud)));
    }

    #[test]
    fn few_pulse_irregular_gaps_seeded() {
        let dist = 500_i32;
        let plan = plan_steps(Profile::Average, dist as f64, 0, 0);
        let samples = sample_few_pulse_path(
            0,
            0,
            dist,
            0,
            plan.n_steps,
            plan.step_ms,
            1.0,
            Some(123),
        );
        assert!(samples.len() >= 6);
        let pulses = count_speed_pulses(&samples);
        assert!((1..=3).contains(&pulses), "pulses={pulses}");
        assert_eq!(
            (samples.last().unwrap().x, samples.last().unwrap().y),
            (dist, 0)
        );
        let dts: Vec<i32> = samples.iter().map(|s| s.dt_ms).collect();
        assert!(dts.iter().all(|&d| d >= GAP_MIN_MS && d <= GAP_MAX_MS));
        assert!(
            dts.iter().any(|&d| d != dts[0]),
            "all dts equal (metronome): {dts:?}"
        );
        let distinct: std::collections::BTreeSet<_> = dts.iter().copied().collect();
        assert!(
            distinct.len() >= 3,
            "want several distinct gaps, got {distinct:?}"
        );
        assert!(samples.iter().all(|s| s.delay_ms == s.dt_ms));
        let mean = dts.iter().sum::<i32>() as f64 / dts.len() as f64;
        assert!(
            (mean - plan.step_ms as f64).abs() <= (plan.step_ms as f64 * 0.2).max(2.0),
            "mean dt={mean} planned step_ms={}",
            plan.step_ms
        );
        let sum = dts.iter().sum::<i32>();
        let target = plan.step_ms * samples.len() as i32;
        assert!(
            (sum - target).abs() <= samples.len() as i32 + 2,
            "sum={sum} target={target}"
        );
        let mid_max = samples
            .iter()
            .map(|s| s.lateral_px.abs())
            .fold(0.0_f64, f64::max);
        assert!(mid_max > 0.2, "expected lateral drift, max={mid_max}");
        let var = dts
            .iter()
            .map(|&d| {
                let x = d as f64 - mean;
                x * x
            })
            .sum::<f64>()
            / dts.len() as f64;
        let cv = var.sqrt() / mean.max(1e-9);
        assert!(cv > 0.05, "cv too small (near metronome): {cv}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn few_pulse_end_damp_slows_last_pixels() {
        let dist = 600_i32;
        let plan = plan_steps(Profile::Average, dist as f64, 0, 0);
        let samples = sample_few_pulse_path(
            0,
            0,
            dist,
            0,
            plan.n_steps,
            plan.step_ms,
            1.0,
            Some(77),
        );
        assert!(samples.len() >= 10);
        let pulses = count_speed_pulses(&samples);
        assert!((1..=3).contains(&pulses), "pulses={pulses}");
        assert_eq!(
            (samples.last().unwrap().x, samples.last().unwrap().y),
            (dist, 0)
        );

        let n = samples.len();
        let mid = &samples[n / 5 .. (3 * n) / 5];
        let tail = &samples[(4 * n) / 5 ..];
        let mid_speed: f64 =
            mid.iter().map(|s| s.speed.abs()).sum::<f64>() / mid.len().max(1) as f64;
        let tail_speed: f64 =
            tail.iter().map(|s| s.speed.abs()).sum::<f64>() / tail.len().max(1) as f64;
        assert!(
            tail_speed < mid_speed,
            "end damp: tail speed {tail_speed} should be < mid {mid_speed}"
        );

        // Per-step displacement also slower in the tail (last pixels).
        let mut mid_disp = 0.0;
        let mut mid_n = 0usize;
        for w in mid.windows(2) {
            mid_disp += distance_i(w[0].x, w[0].y, w[1].x, w[1].y);
            mid_n += 1;
        }
        let mut tail_disp = 0.0;
        let mut tail_n = 0usize;
        for w in tail.windows(2) {
            tail_disp += distance_i(w[0].x, w[0].y, w[1].x, w[1].y);
            tail_n += 1;
        }
        if mid_n > 0 && tail_n > 0 {
            let mid_d = mid_disp / mid_n as f64;
            let tail_d = tail_disp / tail_n as f64;
            // Soft check: tail mean step should not exceed mid by much; prefer lower.
            assert!(
                tail_d <= mid_d * 1.15,
                "tail disp/step {tail_d} vs mid {mid_d}"
            );
        }

        // #5 irregular gaps still present
        let dts: Vec<i32> = samples.iter().map(|s| s.dt_ms).collect();
        let distinct: std::collections::BTreeSet<_> = dts.iter().copied().collect();
        assert!(distinct.len() >= 3, "gaps still irregular: {distinct:?}");

        // #4 drift still present
        let mid_lat = samples
            .iter()
            .map(|s| s.lateral_px.abs())
            .fold(0.0_f64, f64::max);
        assert!(mid_lat > 0.2, "drift still present, max={mid_lat}");
    }
}
