// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
//! Human-like typing via SendInput UNICODE.
//!
//! **Hard stop:** Chromium `input[type=password]` fields may reject synthetic
//! UNICODE / scancode / clipboard keys. Do not burn retries â€” escalate to human
//! or CDP. Never log secrets.

use crate::native::{send_unicode_char, send_vk, sleep_ms};
use rand::Rng;

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Pace {
    Everyday,
    Careful,
    Brisk,
}

impl Pace {
    pub fn parse(s: &str) -> Self {
        match s.to_ascii_lowercase().as_str() {
            "careful" => Pace::Careful,
            "brisk" => Pace::Brisk,
            _ => Pace::Everyday,
        }
    }
}

struct Prof {
    burst_min: u64,
    burst_max: u64,
    think_chance: f64,
    think_min: u64,
    think_max: u64,
    word_gap_min: u64,
    word_gap_max: u64,
    punct_min: u64,
    punct_max: u64,
}

fn profile(p: Pace) -> Prof {
    match p {
        Pace::Careful => Prof {
            burst_min: 160,
            burst_max: 280,
            think_chance: 0.18,
            think_min: 350,
            think_max: 900,
            word_gap_min: 280,
            word_gap_max: 550,
            punct_min: 300,
            punct_max: 700,
        },
        Pace::Brisk => Prof {
            burst_min: 55,
            burst_max: 120,
            think_chance: 0.06,
            think_min: 180,
            think_max: 400,
            word_gap_min: 100,
            word_gap_max: 220,
            punct_min: 120,
            punct_max: 280,
        },
        Pace::Everyday => Prof {
            burst_min: 110,
            burst_max: 210,
            think_chance: 0.12,
            think_min: 280,
            think_max: 700,
            word_gap_min: 200,
            word_gap_max: 450,
            punct_min: 220,
            punct_max: 520,
        },
    }
}

fn send_char(ch: char) {
    if ch == '\n' || ch == '\r' {
        // VK_RETURN = 0x0D
        send_vk(0x0D, false);
        sleep_ms(20);
        send_vk(0x0D, true);
        return;
    }
    if ch == '\t' {
        send_vk(0x09, false);
        sleep_ms(20);
        send_vk(0x09, true);
        return;
    }
    let mut buf = [0u16; 2];
    let encoded = ch.encode_utf16(&mut buf);
    for &u in encoded.iter() {
        send_unicode_char(u, false);
        sleep_ms(8);
        send_unicode_char(u, true);
    }
}

fn backspace() {
    send_vk(0x08, false);
    sleep_ms(15);
    send_vk(0x08, true);
}

fn nearby_typo(ch: char, rng: &mut impl Rng) -> char {
    let c = ch.to_ascii_lowercase();
    let neighbors: &[(&str, &str)] = &[
        ("a", "sqw"),
        ("b", "vngh"),
        ("c", "xvd"),
        ("d", "sfec"),
        ("e", "rdsw"),
        ("f", "dgcr"),
        ("g", "fhtb"),
        ("h", "gjyn"),
        ("i", "uokj"),
        ("j", "hkui"),
        ("k", "jloi"),
        ("l", "kop"),
        ("m", "njk"),
        ("n", "bmhj"),
        ("o", "iplk"),
        ("p", "ol"),
        ("q", "wa"),
        ("r", "etf"),
        ("s", "adwx"),
        ("t", "ryg"),
        ("u", "yiji"),
        ("v", "cb"),
        ("w", "qeas"),
        ("x", "zsd"),
        ("y", "tuh"),
        ("z", "ax"),
    ];
    let pool = neighbors
        .iter()
        .find(|(k, _)| k.chars().next() == Some(c))
        .map(|(_, v)| *v)
        .unwrap_or("abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz");
    let opts: Vec<char> = pool.chars().collect();
    let wrong = opts[rng.gen_range(0..opts.len())];
    if ch.is_uppercase() {
        wrong.to_ascii_uppercase()
    } else {
        wrong
    }
}

fn sleep_iki(prof: &Prof, ch: char, next: Option<char>, rng: &mut impl Rng) {
    let mut iki = rng.gen_range(prof.burst_min..=prof.burst_max);
    if rng.gen::<f64>() < prof.think_chance {
        iki = rng.gen_range(prof.think_min..=prof.think_max);
    }
    if ch.is_whitespace() {
        iki = rng.gen_range(prof.word_gap_min..=prof.word_gap_max);
        if rng.gen::<f64>() < 0.15 {
            iki += rng.gen_range(120..=400);
        }
    }
    if ".,;:!?".contains(ch) {
        iki = rng.gen_range(prof.punct_min..=prof.punct_max);
    }
    if next == Some(ch) && !ch.is_whitespace() {
        iki += rng.gen_range(30..=90);
    }
    if !ch.is_whitespace() && rng.gen::<f64>() < 0.04 {
        iki += rng.gen_range(150..=350);
    }
    sleep_ms(iki.max(35));
}

/// Type `text` with human-like inter-key intervals. Optional light typos.
///
/// Returns a summary line (no secret echo of `text` contents beyond length).
pub fn type_human(text: &str, pace: Pace, typo_chance: f64, no_typos: bool) -> String {
    let prof = profile(pace);
    let mut rng = rand::thread_rng();
    let chars: Vec<char> = text.chars().collect();
    let start = std::time::Instant::now();
    let mut typo_count = 0usize;
    let n = chars.len();

    for (i, &ch) in chars.iter().enumerate() {
        if crate::override_guard::aborted() {
            break;
        }
        let next = chars.get(i + 1).copied();
        let do_typo = !no_typos
            && !ch.is_whitespace()
            && ch.is_ascii_alphabetic()
            && rng.gen::<f64>() < typo_chance
            && (typo_count < 1 || (n > 80 && typo_count < 2));

        if do_typo {
            let n_wrong = if rng.gen::<f64>() < 0.35 { 2 } else { 1 };
            for _ in 0..n_wrong {
                let bad = nearby_typo(ch, &mut rng);
                send_char(bad);
                sleep_iki(&prof, bad, Some(ch), &mut rng);
            }
            sleep_ms(rng.gen_range(120..=280));
            for _ in 0..n_wrong {
                backspace();
                sleep_ms(rng.gen_range(80..=180));
            }
            sleep_ms(rng.gen_range(100..=220));
            typo_count += 1;
        }

        send_char(ch);
        if i + 1 < n {
            sleep_iki(&prof, ch, next, &mut rng);
        }
    }

    let elapsed = start.elapsed().as_millis();
    let wpm = if elapsed > 0 {
        ((n as f64 / 5.0) / (elapsed as f64 / 60000.0) * 10.0).round() / 10.0
    } else {
        0.0
    };
    format!(
        "typed chars={} pace={:?} typos={} elapsed_ms={} approx_wpm={}  (password fields may reject synthetic keys)",
        n,
        pace,
        typo_count,
        elapsed,
        wpm
    )
}


===== FILE: src\uia.rs =====
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
//! UI Automation helpers for chrome ComboBox dropdowns (Brave lessons).
//!
//! - Arrow click at `X + Width - 12`, `Y + Height / 2`
//! - Use **raw UIA screen pixels** (do not wrongly convert logicalâ†”physical on 100% DPI)
//! - After open: **do not** Refresh / SetForeground (collapses Brave chrome menus)
//! - Lasting Brave option name contains `forever` (not "Always allow")
//! - Combo search is **window-scoped** (`process_name` + `window_title_match`).
//!   Never fall back to the first desktop ComboBox on a name miss.
//!   Click only when the filtered result is exclusive.

#[cfg(all(windows, feature = "win32"))]
use crate::click::{click_at, ClickButton};
#[cfg(all(windows, feature = "win32"))]
use crate::native::sleep_ms;
#[cfg(all(windows, feature = "win32"))]
use crate::path::{Algo, Profile};

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct DropdownOpts {
    pub process_name: String,
    pub window_title_match: String,
    pub combo_name_contains: String,
    pub arrow_inset_px: i32,
    pub hover_ms: u64,
}

impl Default for DropdownOpts {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            process_name: "brave".into(),
            window_title_match: String::new(),
            combo_name_contains: String::new(),
            arrow_inset_px: 12,
            hover_ms: 280,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct ArrowPoint {
    pub x: i32,
    pub y: i32,
    pub rect_x: i32,
    pub rect_y: i32,
    pub width: i32,
    pub height: i32,
    pub name: String,
}

/// One ComboBox from a scoped UIA search (no HWND; safe for unit tests).
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct ComboHit {
    pub name: String,
    pub process: String,
    pub window_title: String,
    pub x: i32,
    pub y: i32,
    pub width: i32,
    pub height: i32,
}

/// Compute combo arrow click point from a UIA bounding rect (raw screen pixels).
pub fn arrow_from_rect(x: f64, y: f64, w: f64, h: f64, inset: i32) -> (i32, i32) {
    let ax = (x + w - inset as f64).round() as i32;
    let ay = (y + h / 2.0).round() as i32;
    (ax, ay)
}

/// Case-insensitive substring. Empty needle matches everything.
pub fn contains_ci(hay: &str, needle: &str) -> bool {
    if needle.is_empty() {
        return true;
    }
    hay.to_ascii_lowercase()
        .contains(&needle.to_ascii_lowercase())
}

/// Process filter: empty = any process. Compare file stems, ignore `.exe`, case-insensitive.
pub fn process_matches(proc_name: &str, filter: &str) -> bool {
    if filter.is_empty() {
        return true;
    }
    let p = proc_name
        .rsplit(['\\', '/'])
        .next()
        .unwrap_or(proc_name)
        .trim_end_matches(".exe")
        .trim_end_matches(".EXE")
        .to_ascii_lowercase();
    let f = filter
        .rsplit(['\\', '/'])
        .next()
        .unwrap_or(filter)
        .trim_end_matches(".exe")
        .trim_end_matches(".EXE")
        .to_ascii_lowercase();
    p == f
}

/// Grok Bot chrome / similar: never click even if it is the only hit.
pub fn looks_like_unsafe_combo(name: &str) -> bool {
    let n = name.to_ascii_lowercase();
    n.contains("on-demand") || n.contains("monthly limit")
}

/// Fixture ComboBox: `SM-Remember` inside window `SM-REG-FIXTURE`.
pub fn is_fixture_combo(hit: &ComboHit) -> bool {
    contains_ci(&hit.name, "SM-Remember")
        && contains_ci(&hit.window_title, "SM-REG-FIXTURE")
        && !looks_like_unsafe_combo(&hit.name)
}

/// Apply process / title / name filters to already-collected hits.
pub fn filter_combo_hits<'a>(hits: &'a [ComboHit], opts: &DropdownOpts) -> Vec<&'a ComboHit> {
    hits.iter()
        .filter(|h| {
            contains_ci(&h.name, &opts.combo_name_contains)
                && process_matches(&h.process, &opts.process_name)
                && contains_ci(&h.window_title, &opts.window_title_match)
        })
        .collect()
}

/// Click path: exactly one hit, else refuse. No first-combo fallback.
pub fn pick_exclusive_combo<'a>(hits: &'a [ComboHit]) -> Result<&'a ComboHit, String> {
    match hits.len() {
        0 => Err("ComboBox not found".into()),
        1 => {
            let hit = &hits[0];
            if looks_like_unsafe_combo(&hit.name) {
                Err(format!(
                    "refusing to click unsafe ComboBox: '{}'",
                    hit.name
                ))
            } else {
                Ok(hit)
            }
        }
        n => Err(format!(
            "ComboBox not exclusive: {n} matches; refusing click"
        )),
    }
}

/// One interactive Name match under a scoped root (no HWND; safe for unit tests).
/// Used by `sm click-name`.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct NameHit {
    pub name: String,
    /// UIA control type word: Button, Hyperlink, ListItem, MenuItem, â€¦
    pub control_type: String,
    pub process: String,
    pub window_title: String,
    pub x: i32,
    pub y: i32,
    pub width: i32,
    pub height: i32,
}

/// Control-type words accepted by `click-name` (login pills / links / list rows / menus).
pub fn is_click_name_type_word(word: &str) -> bool {
    matches!(
        word,
        "Button" | "Hyperlink" | "ListItem" | "MenuItem"
    )
}

/// UIA ControlType Ids accepted by `click-name` (50000 Button, 50005 Hyperlink,
/// 50007 ListItem, 50011 MenuItem).
pub fn is_click_name_type_id(kind: i32) -> bool {
    matches!(kind, 50000 | 50005 | 50007 | 50011)
}

/// Grok Bot chrome or known unsafe combo names: never click.
pub fn looks_like_unsafe_chrome(name: &str, window_title: &str) -> bool {
    looks_like_unsafe_combo(name)
        || contains_ci(name, "Grok Bot")
        || contains_ci(window_title, "Grok Bot")
}

/// Keep hits whose Name contains `needle` (case-insensitive). Empty needle matches nothing
/// for click-name (caller must pass a real string).
pub fn filter_name_hits<'a>(hits: &'a [NameHit], needle: &str) -> Vec<&'a NameHit> {
    if needle.is_empty() {
        return Vec::new();
    }
    hits.iter()
        .filter(|h| contains_ci(&h.name, needle) && is_click_name_type_word(&h.control_type))
        .collect()
}

/// Exactly one safe hit, else refuse (0 / >1 / Grok Bot / unsafe).
pub fn pick_exclusive_name<'a>(hits: &'a [NameHit]) -> Result<&'a NameHit, String> {
    match hits.len() {
        0 => Err("click-name: no match".into()),
        1 => {
            let hit = &hits[0];
            if looks_like_unsafe_chrome(&hit.name, &hit.window_title) {
                Err(format!(
                    "refusing to click unsafe chrome: name='{}' window='{}'",
                    hit.name, hit.window_title
                ))
            } else {
                Ok(hit)
            }
        }
        n => Err(format!(
            "click-name: not exclusive: {n} matches; refusing click"
        )),
    }
}

/// Center of a NameHit bounding rect (raw screen pixels).
pub fn name_hit_center(hit: &NameHit) -> (i32, i32) {
    (
        hit.x + hit.width / 2,
        hit.y + hit.height / 2,
    )
}

/// Parseable probe text (no click). Used by `sm dropdown probe` and tests.
pub fn format_combo_probe(hits: &[ComboHit]) -> String {
    let exclusive = hits.len() == 1 && !looks_like_unsafe_combo(&hits[0].name);
    let mut lines = vec![format!(
        "combo count={} exclusive={}",
        hits.len(),
        exclusive
    )];
    for h in hits {
        lines.push(format!(
            "combo name='{}' process='{}' window='{}' rect={},{} {}x{}",
            h.name, h.process, h.window_title, h.x, h.y, h.width, h.height
        ));
    }
    lines.join("\n")
}


#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct MinimizeHit {
    pub hwnd: isize,
    pub cx: i32,
    pub cy: i32,
    pub names: String,
}

pub fn exact_name_indexes(names: &[String], want: &str) -> Vec<usize> {
    names
        .iter()
        .enumerate()
        .filter(|(_, name)| name.as_str() == want)
        .map(|(i, _)| i)
        .collect()
}

pub fn names_line(names: &[String]) -> String {
    if names.is_empty() {
        "none".into()
    } else {
        names.join(",")
    }
}


#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct ScreenRect {
    pub x: i32,
    pub y: i32,
    pub w: i32,
    pub h: i32,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct TitleBarHit {
    pub hwnd: isize,
    pub title: ScreenRect,
    pub blocks: Vec<ScreenRect>,
    pub found: String,
}

pub fn point_in_rect(x: i32, y: i32, r: &ScreenRect) -> bool {
    r.w > 0 && r.h > 0 && x >= r.x && y >= r.y && x < r.x + r.w && y < r.y + r.h
}

/// One point inside the title rect and outside every caption-button rect.
/// No saved corner. None means there is no safe point, so the caller must not drag.
pub fn clear_title_point(title: &ScreenRect, blocks: &[ScreenRect]) -> Option<(i32, i32)> {
    if title.w < 16 || title.h < 8 {
        return None;
    }
    let y = title.y + title.h / 2;
    if !point_in_rect(title.x + 1, y, title) {
        return None;
    }
    let left = title.x + 1;
    let right = title.x + title.w - 1;
    let mut best_s = 0;
    let mut best_e = 0;
    let mut run_s: Option<i32> = None;
    let consider = |s: i32, e: i32, best_s: &mut i32, best_e: &mut i32| {
        if e - s > *best_e - *best_s {
            *best_s = s;
            *best_e = e;
        }
    };
    for x in left..right {
        let blocked = blocks.iter().any(|b| point_in_rect(x, y, b));
        if !blocked {
            if run_s.is_none() {
                run_s = Some(x);
            }
        } else if let Some(s) = run_s.take() {
            consider(s, x, &mut best_s, &mut best_e);
        }
    }
    if let Some(s) = run_s {
        consider(s, right, &mut best_s, &mut best_e);
    }
    if best_e - best_s < 8 {
        return None;
    }
    Some((best_s + (best_e - best_s) / 2, y))
}


#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct ScrollBarHit {
    pub hwnd: isize,
    pub bar: ScreenRect,
    pub thumb: ScreenRect,
    pub milli: Option<i32>,
    pub found: String,
}

/// Start and end of a short thumb drag that stays inside the bar. No saved corner.
pub fn thumb_drag_ends(bar: &ScreenRect, thumb: &ScreenRect) -> Option<(i32, i32, i32, i32)> {
    let sx = thumb.x + thumb.w / 2;
    let sy = thumb.y + thumb.h / 2;
    if !point_in_rect(sx, sy, bar) {
        return None;
    }
    let top = bar.y + 3;
    let bot = bar.y + bar.h - 3;
    if sy < top || sy > bot {
        return None;
    }
    let room_up = sy - top;
    let room_down = bot - sy;
    let step = 28;
    let dy = if room_down >= 8 && room_down >= room_up {
        step.min(room_down)
    } else if room_up >= 8 {
        -(step.min(room_up))
    } else {
        return None;
    };
    let ey = sy + dy;
    if !point_in_rect(sx, ey, bar) {
        return None;
    }
    Some((sx, sy, sx, ey))
}
