#[cfg(all(windows, feature = "win32"))]
mod win_uia {
    use super::*;
    use windows::core::{BSTR, VARIANT};
    use windows::Win32::Foundation::{CloseHandle, RECT};
    use windows::Win32::System::Com::{
        CoCreateInstance, CoInitializeEx, CLSCTX_INPROC_SERVER, COINIT_APARTMENTTHREADED,
    };
    use windows::Win32::System::Threading::{
        OpenProcess, QueryFullProcessImageNameW, PROCESS_NAME_WIN32,
        PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION,
    };
    use windows::Win32::UI::Accessibility::{
        CUIAutomation, IUIAutomation, IUIAutomationElement, IUIAutomationElementArray,
        TreeScope_Descendants, TreeScope_Children,
        UIA_ButtonControlTypeId, UIA_ComboBoxControlTypeId, UIA_EditControlTypeId, UIA_HyperlinkControlTypeId, UIA_ListItemControlTypeId,
        UIA_MenuItemControlTypeId, UIA_TitleBarControlTypeId, UIA_ScrollBarControlTypeId,
        UIA_ThumbControlTypeId, UIA_CheckBoxControlTypeId, UIA_RadioButtonControlTypeId, UIA_RangeValuePatternId, UIA_ScrollPatternId,
        UIA_TogglePatternId, UIA_SelectionItemPatternId, IUIAutomationRangeValuePattern, IUIAutomationTogglePattern, IUIAutomationSelectionItemPattern,
        UIA_ValuePatternId, IUIAutomationValuePattern,
        UIA_NamePropertyId, UIA_ControlTypePropertyId,
        UIA_BoundingRectanglePropertyId,
    };

    fn init_com() -> windows::core::Result<()> {
        unsafe {
            let hr = CoInitializeEx(None, COINIT_APARTMENTTHREADED);
            // RPC_E_CHANGED_MODE / S_FALSE are ok if already initialized
            let _ = hr;
            Ok(())
        }
    }

    fn automation() -> windows::core::Result<IUIAutomation> {
        init_com()?;
        unsafe { CoCreateInstance(&CUIAutomation, None, CLSCTX_INPROC_SERVER) }
    }

    fn element_name(el: &IUIAutomationElement) -> String {
        unsafe {
            el.CurrentName()
                .map(|b| b.to_string())
                .unwrap_or_default()
        }
    }

    fn element_rect(el: &IUIAutomationElement) -> Option<(f64, f64, f64, f64)> {
        unsafe {
            let v = el.GetCurrentPropertyValue(UIA_BoundingRectanglePropertyId).ok()?;
            // Bounding rect comes as double[4]: left, top, width, height
            // In windows crate, VARIANT handling varies; use CurrentBoundingRectangle if available.
            let _ = v;
            let r = el.CurrentBoundingRectangle().ok()?;
            Some((r.left as f64, r.top as f64, (r.right - r.left) as f64, (r.bottom - r.top) as f64))
        }
    }

    fn process_name_from_pid(pid: u32) -> String {
        if pid == 0 {
            return String::new();
        }
        unsafe {
            let handle = match OpenProcess(PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION, false, pid) {
                Ok(h) => h,
                Err(_) => return String::new(),
            };
            let mut buf = [0u16; 512];
            let mut size = buf.len() as u32;
            let pw = windows::core::PWSTR(buf.as_mut_ptr());
            let res = QueryFullProcessImageNameW(handle, PROCESS_NAME_WIN32, pw, &mut size);
            let _ = CloseHandle(handle);
            if res.is_err() {
                return String::new();
            }
            let n = (size as usize).min(buf.len());
            let path = String::from_utf16_lossy(&buf[..n]);
            std::path::Path::new(&path)
                .file_stem()
                .and_then(|s| s.to_str())
                .unwrap_or("")
                .to_string()
        }
    }

    fn element_pid(el: &IUIAutomationElement) -> u32 {
        unsafe { el.CurrentProcessId().ok().map(|v| v as u32).unwrap_or(0) }
    }

    struct FoundCombo {
        el: IUIAutomationElement,
        hit: ComboHit,
    }

    fn collect_combos_under(
        auto: &IUIAutomation,
        root: &IUIAutomationElement,
        window_title: &str,
        process: &str,
        name_contains: &str,
        out: &mut Vec<FoundCombo>,
    ) -> windows::core::Result<()> {
        unsafe {
            let cond = auto.CreatePropertyCondition(
                UIA_ControlTypePropertyId,
                &VARIANT::from(UIA_ComboBoxControlTypeId.0 as i32),
            )?;
            let arr = root.FindAll(TreeScope_Descendants, &cond)?;
            let n = arr.Length().unwrap_or(0);
            for i in 0..n {
                let el = arr.GetElement(i)?;
                let name = element_name(&el);
                if !contains_ci(&name, name_contains) {
                    continue;
                }
                let (x, y, w, h) = element_rect(&el).unwrap_or((0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0));
                out.push(FoundCombo {
                    el,
                    hit: ComboHit {
                        name,
                        process: process.to_string(),
                        window_title: window_title.to_string(),
                        x: x as i32,
                        y: y as i32,
                        width: w as i32,
                        height: h as i32,
                    },
                });
            }
            Ok(())
        }
    }

    /// Window-scoped combo search. Honors process + title. No first-combo fallback.
    fn collect_combos(auto: &IUIAutomation, opts: &DropdownOpts) -> Result<Vec<FoundCombo>, String> {
        let desktop = desktop_root(auto).map_err(|e| format!("root: {e}"))?;
        let scoped = !opts.process_name.is_empty() || !opts.window_title_match.is_empty();
        let mut found = Vec::new();
        if scoped {
            let kids = unsafe {
                let cond = auto
                    .CreateTrueCondition()
                    .map_err(|e| format!("true cond: {e}"))?;
                desktop
                    .FindAll(TreeScope_Children, &cond)
                    .map_err(|e| format!("desktop children: {e}"))?
            };
            let n = unsafe { kids.Length().unwrap_or(0) };
            let mut windows = 0u32;
            for i in 0..n {
                let el = unsafe { kids.GetElement(i).map_err(|e| format!("child: {e}"))? };
                let title = element_name(&el);
                if !contains_ci(&title, &opts.window_title_match) {
                    continue;
                }
                let proc = process_name_from_pid(element_pid(&el));
                if !process_matches(&proc, &opts.process_name) {
                    continue;
                }
                windows += 1;
                collect_combos_under(
                    auto,
                    &el,
                    &title,
                    &proc,
                    &opts.combo_name_contains,
                    &mut found,
                )
                .map_err(|e| format!("find combo: {e}"))?;
            }
            if windows == 0 {
                return Err(format!(
                    "window not found process='{}' title_match='{}'",
                    opts.process_name, opts.window_title_match
                ));
            }
        } else {
            collect_combos_under(
                auto,
                &desktop,
                "",
                "",
                &opts.combo_name_contains,
                &mut found,
            )
            .map_err(|e| format!("find combo: {e}"))?;
        }
        Ok(found)
    }

    fn exclusive_combo(found: &[FoundCombo]) -> Result<&FoundCombo, String> {
        let hits: Vec<ComboHit> = found.iter().map(|f| f.hit.clone()).collect();
        let picked = pick_exclusive_combo(&hits)?;
        found
            .iter()
            .find(|f| {
                f.hit.name == picked.name
                    && f.hit.process == picked.process
                    && f.hit.window_title == picked.window_title
                    && f.hit.x == picked.x
                    && f.hit.y == picked.y
            })
            .ok_or_else(|| "ComboBox not found".to_string())
    }

    fn find_by_type_name(
        auto: &IUIAutomation,
        root: &IUIAutomationElement,
        control_type: i32,
        name_contains: &str,
    ) -> windows::core::Result<Option<IUIAutomationElement>> {
        unsafe {
            let cond = auto.CreatePropertyCondition(
                UIA_ControlTypePropertyId,
                &VARIANT::from(control_type),
            )?;
            let arr = root.FindAll(TreeScope_Descendants, &cond)?;
            let n = arr.Length().unwrap_or(0);
            let needle = name_contains.to_ascii_lowercase();
            for i in 0..n {
                let el = arr.GetElement(i)?;
                let name = element_name(&el).to_ascii_lowercase();
                if name.contains(&needle) {
                    return Ok(Some(el));
                }
            }
            Ok(None)
        }
    }

    fn desktop_root(auto: &IUIAutomation) -> windows::core::Result<IUIAutomationElement> {
        unsafe { auto.GetRootElement() }
    }

    pub fn probe_combos(opts: &DropdownOpts) -> Result<String, String> {
        let auto = automation().map_err(|e| format!("UIA init: {e}"))?;
        let found = collect_combos(&auto, opts)?;
        let hits: Vec<ComboHit> = found.iter().map(|f| f.hit.clone()).collect();
        Ok(format_combo_probe(&hits))
    }

    pub fn open_dropdown(opts: &DropdownOpts) -> Result<String, String> {
        let auto = automation().map_err(|e| format!("UIA init: {e}"))?;
        let found = collect_combos(&auto, opts)?;
        let combo = exclusive_combo(&found)?;
        if looks_like_unsafe_combo(&combo.hit.name) {
            return Err(format!(
                "refusing to click unsafe ComboBox: '{}'",
                combo.hit.name
            ));
        }
        let name = combo.hit.name.clone();
        let (x, y, w, h) = element_rect(&combo.el).ok_or("empty combo rect")?;
        if w < 8.0 || h < 8.0 {
            return Err(format!("ComboBox rect empty: {w}x{h}"));
        }
        let (ax, ay) = arrow_from_rect(x, y, w, h, opts.arrow_inset_px);
        let msg = format!(
            "dropdown open: '{name}' process='{}' window='{}' arrow={ax},{ay} rect={},{} {}x{}",
            combo.hit.process,
            combo.hit.window_title,
            x as i32,
            y as i32,
            w as i32,
            h as i32
        );
        // Motor click â€” raw screen pixels
        let _ = click_at(
            ax,
            ay,
            ClickButton::Left,
            Algo::Gust,
            Profile::Average,
            opts.hover_ms,
            false,
        );
        // Do NOT Refresh / SetForeground after open (Brave chrome menu collapses).
        sleep_ms(500);
        Ok(msg)
    }

    pub fn select_item(item_contains: &str, opts: &DropdownOpts) -> Result<String, String> {
        let auto = automation().map_err(|e| format!("UIA init: {e}"))?;
        let root = desktop_root(&auto).map_err(|e| format!("root: {e}"))?;
        let needle = item_contains;
        let el = find_by_type_name(&auto, &root, UIA_ListItemControlTypeId.0 as i32, needle)
            .ok()
            .flatten()
            .or_else(|| {
                find_by_type_name(&auto, &root, UIA_MenuItemControlTypeId.0 as i32, needle)
                    .ok()
                    .flatten()
            })
            .ok_or_else(|| format!("Dropdown item not found: {item_contains}"))?;
        let name = element_name(&el);
        let (x, y, w, h) = element_rect(&el).ok_or("empty item rect")?;
        let cx = (x + w / 2.0).round() as i32;
        let cy = (y + h / 2.0).round() as i32;
        let _ = click_at(
            cx,
            cy,
            ClickButton::Left,
            Algo::Gust,
            Profile::Average,
            opts.hover_ms,
            false,
        );
        sleep_ms(400);
        Ok(format!("dropdown select: '{name}' at {cx},{cy}"))
    }

    pub fn click_button(button_contains: &str, opts: &DropdownOpts) -> Result<String, String> {
        let auto = automation().map_err(|e| format!("UIA init: {e}"))?;
        let root = desktop_root(&auto).map_err(|e| format!("root: {e}"))?;
        let el = find_by_type_name(&auto, &root, UIA_ButtonControlTypeId.0 as i32, button_contains)
            .map_err(|e| format!("find button: {e}"))?
            .ok_or_else(|| format!("Button not found: {button_contains}"))?;
        let name = element_name(&el);
        let (x, y, w, h) = element_rect(&el).ok_or("empty button rect")?;
        let cx = (x + w / 2.0).round() as i32;
        let cy = (y + h / 2.0).round() as i32;
        let _ = click_at(
            cx,
            cy,
            ClickButton::Left,
            Algo::Gust,
            Profile::Average,
            opts.hover_ms,
            false,
        );
        sleep_ms(400);
        Ok(format!("dropdown ClickButton: '{name}' at {cx},{cy}"))
    }

    /// Resolve root: `--expect-hwnd` via ElementFromHandle, else foreground window.
    fn click_name_root(
        auto: &IUIAutomation,
        expect_hwnd: Option<isize>,
    ) -> Result<(IUIAutomationElement, String, String), String> {
        use windows::Win32::Foundation::HWND;
        use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::GetForegroundWindow;
        let hwnd: HWND = if let Some(h) = expect_hwnd {
            if h == 0 {
                return Err("click-name: --expect-hwnd must be non-zero".into());
            }
            HWND(h as *mut std::ffi::c_void)
        } else {
            let fg = unsafe { GetForegroundWindow() };
            if fg.0.is_null() {
                return Err("click-name: no foreground window".into());
            }
            fg
        };
        let root = unsafe {
            auto.ElementFromHandle(hwnd)
                .map_err(|e| format!("click-name ElementFromHandle: {e}"))?
        };
        let title = element_name(&root);
        if looks_like_unsafe_chrome("", &title) {
            return Err(format!(
                "refusing to search unsafe chrome window: '{title}'"
            ));
        }
        let proc = process_name_from_pid(element_pid(&root));
        Ok((root, title, proc))
    }

    fn collect_name_hits_under(
        auto: &IUIAutomation,
        root: &IUIAutomationElement,
        window_title: &str,
        process: &str,
        needle: &str,
    ) -> Result<Vec<NameHit>, String> {
        let kinds = [
            UIA_ButtonControlTypeId.0 as i32,
            UIA_HyperlinkControlTypeId.0 as i32,
            UIA_ListItemControlTypeId.0 as i32,
            UIA_MenuItemControlTypeId.0 as i32,
        ];
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        for kind in kinds {
            unsafe {
                let cond = auto
                    .CreatePropertyCondition(UIA_ControlTypePropertyId, &VARIANT::from(kind))
                    .map_err(|e| format!("click-name cond: {e}"))?;
                let arr = root
                    .FindAll(TreeScope_Descendants, &cond)
                    .map_err(|e| format!("click-name FindAll: {e}"))?;
                let n = arr.Length().unwrap_or(0);
                for i in 0..n {
                    let el = arr.GetElement(i).map_err(|e| format!("click-name el: {e}"))?;
                    let name = element_name(&el);
                    if !contains_ci(&name, needle) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    let (x, y, w, h) = match element_rect(&el) {
                        Some(r) if r.2 >= 4.0 && r.3 >= 4.0 => r,
                        _ => continue,
                    };
                    out.push(NameHit {
                        name,
                        control_type: type_word(kind).to_string(),
                        process: process.to_string(),
                        window_title: window_title.to_string(),
                        x: x as i32,
                        y: y as i32,
                        width: w as i32,
                        height: h as i32,
                    });
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(out)
    }

    /// Find exactly one interactive Name match under hwnd/foreground. Does not click.
    pub fn resolve_click_name(
        contains: &str,
        expect_hwnd: Option<isize>,
    ) -> Result<NameHit, String> {
        let needle = contains.trim();
        if needle.is_empty() {
            return Err("click-name: --contains must be non-empty".into());
        }
        let auto = automation().map_err(|e| format!("UIA init: {e}"))?;
        let (root, title, proc) = click_name_root(&auto, expect_hwnd)?;
        let hits = collect_name_hits_under(&auto, &root, &title, &proc, needle)?;
        let filtered: Vec<NameHit> = filter_name_hits(&hits, needle)
            .into_iter()
            .cloned()
            .collect();
        let picked = pick_exclusive_name(&filtered)?;
        Ok(picked.clone())
    }

    /// Resolve + human-move click at the exclusive Name match center.
    pub fn click_name(
        contains: &str,
        expect_hwnd: Option<isize>,
        hover_ms: u64,
    ) -> Result<String, String> {
        let hit = resolve_click_name(contains, expect_hwnd)?;
        let (cx, cy) = name_hit_center(&hit);
        let _ = click_at(
            cx,
            cy,
            ClickButton::Left,
            Algo::Gust,
            Profile::Average,
            hover_ms,
            false,
        );
        sleep_ms(400);
        Ok(format!(
            "click-name: '{}' type={} at {cx},{cy} process='{}' window='{}' rect={},{} {}x{}",
            hit.name,
            hit.control_type,
            hit.process,
            hit.window_title,
            hit.x,
            hit.y,
            hit.width,
            hit.height
        ))
    }

    pub fn forever_allow(opts: &DropdownOpts) -> Result<String, String> {
        let mut o = opts.clone();
        if o.combo_name_contains.is_empty() {
            o.combo_name_contains = "Remember".into();
        }
        let a = open_dropdown(&o)?;
        let b = select_item("forever", &o)?;
        let c = click_button("Allow", &o)?;
        Ok(format!("{a}\n{b}\n{c}"))
    }


    pub fn front_minimize() -> Result<super::MinimizeHit, String> {
        use windows::Win32::Foundation::HWND;
        use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::GetForegroundWindow;
        let auto = automation().map_err(|e| format!("UIA init: {e}"))?;
        let hwnd: HWND = unsafe { GetForegroundWindow() };
        if hwnd.0.is_null() {
            return Err("no front window, not clicked".into());
        }
        let root = unsafe {
            auto.ElementFromHandle(hwnd)
                .map_err(|e| format!("window element: {e}"))?
        };
        let buttons = unsafe {
            let cond = auto
                .CreatePropertyCondition(
                    UIA_ControlTypePropertyId,
                    &VARIANT::from(UIA_ButtonControlTypeId.0 as i32),
                )
                .map_err(|e| format!("button cond: {e}"))?;
            root.FindAll(TreeScope_Descendants, &cond)
                .map_err(|e| format!("find buttons: {e}"))?
        };
        let n = unsafe { buttons.Length().unwrap_or(0) };
        let mut names: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        let mut centers: Vec<(i32, i32)> = Vec::new();
        for i in 0..n {
            let el = unsafe { buttons.GetElement(i).map_err(|e| format!("button: {e}"))? };
            let name = element_name(&el);
            if let Some((x, y, w, h)) = element_rect(&el) {
                if name == "Minimize" && w > 0.0 && h > 0.0 {
                    centers.push(((x + w / 2.0).round() as i32, (y + h / 2.0).round() as i32));
                }
            }
            names.push(name);
        }
        let show = super::names_line(&names);
        let hits = super::exact_name_indexes(&names, "Minimize");
        if hits.len() != 1 || centers.len() != 1 {
            return Err(format!("Minimize count={} names={show}", hits.len()));
        }
        let now = unsafe { GetForegroundWindow() };
        if now != hwnd {
            return Err(format!("window not front, not clicked names={show}"));
        }
        Ok(super::MinimizeHit {
            hwnd: hwnd.0 as isize,
            cx: centers[0].0,
            cy: centers[0].1,
            names: show,
        })
    }

    pub fn hwnd_is_front(token: isize) -> bool {
        use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::GetForegroundWindow;
        let now = unsafe { GetForegroundWindow() };
        now.0 as isize == token && token != 0
    }

    pub fn window_is_iconic(token: isize) -> bool {
        use windows::Win32::Foundation::HWND;
        use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::IsIconic;
        if token == 0 {
            return false;
        }
        let hwnd = HWND(token as *mut std::ffi::c_void);
        unsafe { IsIconic(hwnd).as_bool() }
    }


    pub fn front_named(want: &str) -> Result<super::MinimizeHit, String> {
        use windows::Win32::Foundation::HWND;
        use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::GetForegroundWindow;
        let auto = automation().map_err(|e| format!("UIA init: {e}"))?;
        let hwnd: HWND = unsafe { GetForegroundWindow() };
        if hwnd.0.is_null() {
            return Err("no front window, not clicked".into());
        }
        let root = unsafe {
            auto.ElementFromHandle(hwnd)
                .map_err(|e| format!("window element: {e}"))?
        };
        let buttons = unsafe {
            let cond = auto
                .CreatePropertyCondition(
                    UIA_ControlTypePropertyId,
                    &VARIANT::from(UIA_ButtonControlTypeId.0 as i32),
                )
                .map_err(|e| format!("button cond: {e}"))?;
            root.FindAll(TreeScope_Descendants, &cond)
                .map_err(|e| format!("find buttons: {e}"))?
        };
        let n = unsafe { buttons.Length().unwrap_or(0) };
        let mut names: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        let mut centers: Vec<(i32, i32)> = Vec::new();
        for i in 0..n {
            let el = unsafe { buttons.GetElement(i).map_err(|e| format!("button: {e}"))? };
            let name = element_name(&el);
            if let Some((x, y, w, h)) = element_rect(&el) {
                if name == want && w > 0.0 && h > 0.0 {
                    centers.push(((x + w / 2.0).round() as i32, (y + h / 2.0).round() as i32));
                }
            }
            names.push(name);
        }
        let show = super::names_line(&names);
        let hits = super::exact_name_indexes(&names, want);
        if hits.len() != 1 || centers.len() != 1 {
            return Err(format!("{want} count={} names={show}", hits.len()));
        }
        let now = unsafe { GetForegroundWindow() };
        if now != hwnd {
            return Err(format!("window not front, not clicked names={show}"));
        }
        Ok(super::MinimizeHit {
            hwnd: hwnd.0 as isize,
            cx: centers[0].0,
            cy: centers[0].1,
            names: show,
        })
    }

    pub fn window_is_zoomed(token: isize) -> bool {
        use windows::Win32::Foundation::HWND;
        use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::IsZoomed;
        if token == 0 {
            return false;
        }
        let hwnd = HWND(token as *mut std::ffi::c_void);
        unsafe { IsZoomed(hwnd).as_bool() }
    }


    fn rect_px(r: (f64, f64, f64, f64)) -> super::ScreenRect {
        super::ScreenRect {
            x: r.0.round() as i32,
            y: r.1.round() as i32,
            w: r.2.round() as i32,
            h: r.3.round() as i32,
        }
    }

    pub fn front_title_bar() -> Result<super::TitleBarHit, String> {
        use windows::Win32::Foundation::HWND;
        use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::GetForegroundWindow;
        let auto = automation().map_err(|e| format!("UIA init: {e}"))?;
        let hwnd: HWND = unsafe { GetForegroundWindow() };
        if hwnd.0.is_null() {
            return Err("no front window, not dragged".into());
        }
        let root = unsafe {
            auto.ElementFromHandle(hwnd)
                .map_err(|e| format!("window element: {e}"))?
        };
        let titles = unsafe {
            let cond = auto
                .CreatePropertyCondition(
                    UIA_ControlTypePropertyId,
                    &VARIANT::from(UIA_TitleBarControlTypeId.0 as i32),
                )
                .map_err(|e| format!("title cond: {e}"))?;
            root.FindAll(TreeScope_Descendants, &cond)
                .map_err(|e| format!("find title: {e}"))?
        };
        let n = unsafe { titles.Length().unwrap_or(0) };
        let mut shown: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        let mut rects: Vec<super::ScreenRect> = Vec::new();
        for i in 0..n {
            let el = unsafe { titles.GetElement(i).map_err(|e| format!("title: {e}"))? };
            let name = element_name(&el);
            if let Some(raw) = element_rect(&el) {
                let px = rect_px(raw);
                shown.push(format!(
                    "{name} {},{} {}x{}",
                    px.x, px.y, px.w, px.h
                ));
                if px.w > 0 && px.h > 0 {
                    rects.push(px);
                }
            } else {
                shown.push(format!("{name} no-rect"));
            }
        }
        let found = if shown.is_empty() {
            "none".to_string()
        } else {
            shown.join("; ")
        };
        if n != 1 || rects.len() != 1 {
            return Err(format!("TitleBar count={n} found={found}"));
        }
        let buttons = unsafe {
            let cond = auto
                .CreatePropertyCondition(
                    UIA_ControlTypePropertyId,
                    &VARIANT::from(UIA_ButtonControlTypeId.0 as i32),
                )
                .map_err(|e| format!("button cond: {e}"))?;
            root.FindAll(TreeScope_Descendants, &cond)
                .map_err(|e| format!("find buttons: {e}"))?
        };
        let bn = unsafe { buttons.Length().unwrap_or(0) };
        let mut blocks: Vec<super::ScreenRect> = Vec::new();
        let mut bnames: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        for i in 0..bn {
            let el = unsafe { buttons.GetElement(i).map_err(|e| format!("button: {e}"))? };
            let name = element_name(&el);
            let caption = name == "Minimize" || name == "Maximize" || name == "Restore" || name == "Close";
            if let Some(raw) = element_rect(&el) {
                let px = rect_px(raw);
                if caption && px.w > 0 && px.h > 0 {
                    blocks.push(px);
                }
            }
            bnames.push(name);
        }
        let now = unsafe { GetForegroundWindow() };
        if now != hwnd {
            return Err(format!("window not front, not dragged found={found}"));
        }
        Ok(super::TitleBarHit {
            hwnd: hwnd.0 as isize,
            title: rects[0],
            blocks,
            found: format!("title={found} buttons={}", super::names_line(&bnames)),
        })
    }

    pub fn window_box(token: isize) -> Result<super::ScreenRect, String> {
        use windows::Win32::Foundation::{HWND, RECT};
        use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::GetWindowRect;
        if token == 0 {
            return Err("no window".into());
        }
        let hwnd = HWND(token as *mut std::ffi::c_void);
        let mut r = RECT::default();
        unsafe { GetWindowRect(hwnd, &mut r).map_err(|e| format!("window rect: {e}"))? };
        Ok(super::ScreenRect {
            x: r.left,
            y: r.top,
            w: r.right - r.left,
            h: r.bottom - r.top,
        })
    }

    pub fn screen_bounds() -> super::ScreenRect {
        use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::{
            GetSystemMetrics, SM_CXVIRTUALSCREEN, SM_CYVIRTUALSCREEN, SM_XVIRTUALSCREEN,
            SM_YVIRTUALSCREEN,
        };
        unsafe {
            super::ScreenRect {
                x: GetSystemMetrics(SM_XVIRTUALSCREEN),
                y: GetSystemMetrics(SM_YVIRTUALSCREEN),
                w: GetSystemMetrics(SM_CXVIRTUALSCREEN),
                h: GetSystemMetrics(SM_CYVIRTUALSCREEN),
            }
        }
    }



    fn range_milli(el: &IUIAutomationElement) -> Option<i32> {
        unsafe {
            let pat = el
                .GetCurrentPatternAs::<IUIAutomationRangeValuePattern>(UIA_RangeValuePatternId)
                .ok()?;
            let v = pat.CurrentValue().ok()?;
            Some((v * 1000.0).round() as i32)
        }
    }

    fn type_word(kind: i32) -> &'static str {
        match kind {
            50000 => "Button",
            50002 => "CheckBox",
            50003 => "ComboBox",
            50004 => "Edit",
            50005 => "Hyperlink",
            50007 => "ListItem",
            50011 => "MenuItem",
            50013 => "RadioButton",
            50014 => "ScrollBar",
            50020 => "Text",
            50025 => "Custom",
            50026 => "Group",
            50027 => "Thumb",
            50032 => "Window",
            50033 => "Pane",
            _ => "Other",
        }
    }

    pub fn front_vertical_bar() -> Result<super::ScrollBarHit, String> {
        use windows::Win32::Foundation::HWND;
        use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::{
            GetClassNameW, GetDlgItem, GetForegroundWindow, GetWindowLongW, GWL_STYLE,
        };
        let auto = automation().map_err(|e| format!("UIA init: {e}"))?;
        let hwnd: HWND = unsafe { GetForegroundWindow() };
        if hwnd.0.is_null() {
            return Err("no front window, not dragged".into());
        }
        let edit = unsafe {
            GetDlgItem(hwnd, 301).map_err(|e| format!("no edit 301: {e}, not dragged"))?
        };
        if edit.0.is_null() {
            return Err("no edit 301, not dragged".into());
        }
        let mut class_buf = [0u16; 64];
        let nclass = unsafe { GetClassNameW(edit, &mut class_buf) };
        let class = if nclass > 0 {
            String::from_utf16_lossy(&class_buf[..nclass as usize])
        } else {
            String::new()
        };
        if !class.eq_ignore_ascii_case("Edit") {
            return Err(format!("child 301 class={class}, not dragged"));
        }
        let style = unsafe { GetWindowLongW(edit, GWL_STYLE) };
        if style & 0x0004 == 0 {
            return Err(format!("child 301 not multiline style={style}, not dragged"));
        }
        let edit_el = unsafe {
            auto.ElementFromHandle(edit)
                .map_err(|e| format!("edit element: {e}"))?
        };
        let scroll_pattern = unsafe { edit_el.GetCurrentPattern(UIA_ScrollPatternId).is_ok() };
        let edit_rect = element_rect(&edit_el).map(rect_px);
        let mut shown: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        let mut bars: Vec<(super::ScreenRect, IUIAutomationElement)> = Vec::new();
        let mut thumbs: Vec<super::ScreenRect> = Vec::new();
        let mut consider = |el: IUIAutomationElement| {
            let kind = unsafe { el.CurrentControlType().ok().map(|c| c.0).unwrap_or(0) };
            let name = element_name(&el);
            let word = type_word(kind);
            if let Some(raw) = element_rect(&el) {
                let px = rect_px(raw);
                shown.push(format!("{word} {name} {},{} {}x{}", px.x, px.y, px.w, px.h));
                if kind == UIA_ScrollBarControlTypeId.0 && px.h > px.w && px.w > 0 && px.h > 0 {
                    bars.push((px, el));
                } else if kind == UIA_ThumbControlTypeId.0 && px.w > 0 && px.h > 0 {
                    let cx = px.x + px.w / 2;
                    let cy = px.y + px.h / 2;
                    let inside = match edit_rect {
                        Some(r) => super::point_in_rect(cx, cy, &r),
                        None => true,
                    };
                    if inside {
                        thumbs.push(px);
                    }
                }
            } else {
                shown.push(format!("{word} {name} no-rect"));
            }
        };
        if let Ok(walker) = unsafe { auto.RawViewWalker() } {
            let mut pending: Vec<(IUIAutomationElement, i32)> = Vec::new();
            if let Ok(first) = unsafe { walker.GetFirstChildElement(&edit_el) } {
                pending.push((first, 1));
            }
            let mut seen = 0i32;
            while let Some((el, depth)) = pending.pop() {
                seen += 1;
                if seen > 80 {
                    break;
                }
                let child = if depth < 5 {
                    unsafe { walker.GetFirstChildElement(&el).ok() }
                } else {
                    None
                };
                let sib = unsafe { walker.GetNextSiblingElement(&el).ok() };
                consider(el);
                if let Some(c) = child {
                    pending.push((c, depth + 1));
                }
                if let Some(s) = sib {
                    pending.push((s, depth));
                }
            }
        }
        let find_kind = |kind: i32| -> Result<Vec<IUIAutomationElement>, String> {
            let cond = unsafe {
                auto.CreatePropertyCondition(
                    UIA_ControlTypePropertyId,
                    &VARIANT::from(kind),
                )
                .map_err(|e| format!("cond: {e}"))?
            };
            let found = unsafe {
                edit_el
                    .FindAll(TreeScope_Descendants, &cond)
                    .map_err(|e| format!("find: {e}"))?
            };
            let n = unsafe { found.Length().unwrap_or(0) };
            let mut out = Vec::new();
            for i in 0..n {
                out.push(unsafe { found.GetElement(i).map_err(|e| format!("el: {e}"))? });
            }
            Ok(out)
        };
        for el in find_kind(UIA_ScrollBarControlTypeId.0)? {
            consider(el);
        }
        for el in find_kind(UIA_ThumbControlTypeId.0)? {
            consider(el);
        }
        let mut seen_thumb = Vec::new();
        for r in thumbs.drain(..) {
            if !seen_thumb.contains(&r) {
                seen_thumb.push(r);
            }
        }
        thumbs = seen_thumb;
        let mut seen_bar = Vec::new();
        for pair in bars.drain(..) {
            if !seen_bar.iter().any(|(r, _)| *r == pair.0) {
                seen_bar.push(pair);
            }
        }
        bars = seen_bar;
        let line = if shown.is_empty() {
            "none".to_string()
        } else {
            shown.join("; ")
        };
        let note = format!("edit=301 scrollpattern={scroll_pattern} under={line}");
        if thumbs.len() != 1 || bars.len() != 1 {
            if thumbs.is_empty() && scroll_pattern {
                return Err(format!("ScrollPattern only, no thumb, not dragged {note}"));
            }
            return Err(format!(
                "thumb count={} bar count={} {note}",
                thumbs.len(),
                bars.len()
            ));
        }
        let thumb = thumbs[0];
        let cx = thumb.x + thumb.w / 2;
        let cy = thumb.y + thumb.h / 2;
        let mut hit_bar: Option<(super::ScreenRect, IUIAutomationElement)> = None;
        for (rect, el) in bars {
            if super::point_in_rect(cx, cy, &rect) {
                if hit_bar.is_some() {
                    return Err(format!("thumb in more than one bar, not dragged {note}"));
                }
                hit_bar = Some((rect, el));
            }
        }
        let (bar, bar_el) = hit_bar.ok_or_else(|| format!("thumb not inside the bar, not dragged {note}"))?;
        let now = unsafe { GetForegroundWindow() };
        if now != hwnd {
            return Err(format!("window not front, not dragged {note}"));
        }
        Ok(super::ScrollBarHit {
            hwnd: hwnd.0 as isize,
            bar,
            thumb,
            milli: range_milli(&bar_el),
            found: note,
        })
    }


    fn toggle_word(el: &IUIAutomationElement) -> String {
        unsafe {
            let pat = match el.GetCurrentPatternAs::<IUIAutomationTogglePattern>(UIA_TogglePatternId) {
                Ok(p) => p,
                Err(_) => return "unknown".into(),
            };
            match pat.CurrentToggleState() {
                Ok(s) if s.0 == 0 => "off".into(),
                Ok(s) if s.0 == 1 => "on".into(),
                Ok(s) if s.0 == 2 => "indeterminate".into(),
                _ => "unknown".into(),
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn front_oak() -> Result<super::CheckPick, String> {
        use windows::Win32::Foundation::HWND;
        use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::GetForegroundWindow;
        let auto = automation().map_err(|e| format!("UIA init: {e}"))?;
        let hwnd: HWND = unsafe { GetForegroundWindow() };
        if hwnd.0.is_null() {
            return Err("no front window, not clicked".into());
        }
        let root = unsafe {
            auto.ElementFromHandle(hwnd)
                .map_err(|e| format!("window element: {e}"))?
        };
        let mut shown: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        let mut oaks: Vec<(i32, i32, String)> = Vec::new();
        let mut pines: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        let mut seen: Vec<(String, i32, i32)> = Vec::new();
        let consider = |el: &IUIAutomationElement, shown: &mut Vec<String>, oaks: &mut Vec<(i32, i32, String)>, pines: &mut Vec<String>, seen: &mut Vec<(String, i32, i32)>| {
            let kind = unsafe { el.CurrentControlType().ok().map(|c| c.0).unwrap_or(0) };
            let word = if kind == UIA_CheckBoxControlTypeId.0 {
                "CheckBox"
            } else if kind == UIA_ButtonControlTypeId.0 {
                "Button"
            } else {
                type_word(kind)
            };
            let name = element_name(el);
            let state = if kind == UIA_CheckBoxControlTypeId.0 {
                toggle_word(el)
            } else {
                "-".to_string()
            };
            let (cx, cy) = if let Some(raw) = element_rect(el) {
                let px = rect_px(raw);
                shown.push(format!(
                    "{word} {name} {state} {},{} {}x{}",
                    px.x, px.y, px.w, px.h
                ));
                (px.x + px.w / 2, px.y + px.h / 2)
            } else {
                shown.push(format!("{word} {name} {state} no-rect"));
                return;
            };
            if seen.iter().any(|(n, x, y)| n == &name && *x == cx && *y == cy) {
                return;
            }
            seen.push((name.clone(), cx, cy));
            if kind == UIA_CheckBoxControlTypeId.0 && name == "Oak" {
                oaks.push((cx, cy, state));
            } else if kind == UIA_CheckBoxControlTypeId.0 && name == "Pine" {
                pines.push(state);
            }
        };
        let kinds = [UIA_CheckBoxControlTypeId.0, UIA_ButtonControlTypeId.0];
        for kind in kinds {
            let cond = unsafe {
                auto.CreatePropertyCondition(UIA_ControlTypePropertyId, &VARIANT::from(kind))
                    .map_err(|e| format!("cond: {e}"))?
            };
            let found = unsafe {
                root.FindAll(TreeScope_Descendants, &cond)
                    .map_err(|e| format!("find: {e}"))?
            };
            let n = unsafe { found.Length().unwrap_or(0) };
            for i in 0..n {
                let el = unsafe { found.GetElement(i).map_err(|e| format!("el: {e}"))? };
                consider(&el, &mut shown, &mut oaks, &mut pines, &mut seen);
            }
        }
        let line = if shown.is_empty() { "none".into() } else { shown.join("; ") };
        if oaks.len() != 1 {
            return Err(format!("Oak count={} found={line}", oaks.len()));
        }
        if pines.len() != 1 {
            return Err(format!("Pine count={} found={line}", pines.len()));
        }
        let now = unsafe { GetForegroundWindow() };
        if now != hwnd {
            return Err(format!("window not front, not clicked found={line}"));
        }
        Ok(super::CheckPick {
            hwnd: hwnd.0 as isize,
            cx: oaks[0].0,
            cy: oaks[0].1,
            oak: oaks[0].2.clone(),
            pine: pines[0].clone(),
            found: line,
        })
    }


    fn selected_word(el: &IUIAutomationElement) -> String {
        unsafe {
            let pat = match el.GetCurrentPatternAs::<IUIAutomationSelectionItemPattern>(UIA_SelectionItemPatternId) {
                Ok(p) => p,
                Err(_) => return "unknown".into(),
            };
            match pat.CurrentIsSelected() {
                Ok(v) if v.as_bool() => "on".into(),
                Ok(_) => "off".into(),
                Err(_) => "unknown".into(),
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn front_radio() -> Result<super::RadioPick, String> {
        use windows::Win32::Foundation::HWND;
        use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::GetForegroundWindow;
        let auto = automation().map_err(|e| format!("UIA init: {e}"))?;
        let hwnd: HWND = unsafe { GetForegroundWindow() };
        if hwnd.0.is_null() {
            return Err("no front window, not clicked".into());
        }
        let root = unsafe {
            auto.ElementFromHandle(hwnd)
                .map_err(|e| format!("window element: {e}"))?
        };
        let mut shown: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        let mut alphas: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        let mut betas: Vec<(i32, i32, String)> = Vec::new();
        let mut oaks: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        let mut pines: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        let mut seen: Vec<(String, i32, i32)> = Vec::new();
        let consider = |el: &IUIAutomationElement,
                        shown: &mut Vec<String>,
                        alphas: &mut Vec<String>,
                        betas: &mut Vec<(i32, i32, String)>,
                        oaks: &mut Vec<String>,
                        pines: &mut Vec<String>,
                        seen: &mut Vec<(String, i32, i32)>| {
            let kind = unsafe { el.CurrentControlType().ok().map(|c| c.0).unwrap_or(0) };
            let word = if kind == UIA_RadioButtonControlTypeId.0 {
                "RadioButton"
            } else if kind == UIA_CheckBoxControlTypeId.0 {
                "CheckBox"
            } else if kind == UIA_ButtonControlTypeId.0 {
                "Button"
            } else {
                type_word(kind)
            };
            let name = element_name(el);
            let state = if kind == UIA_RadioButtonControlTypeId.0 {
                selected_word(el)
            } else if kind == UIA_CheckBoxControlTypeId.0 {
                toggle_word(el)
            } else {
                "-".to_string()
            };
            let (cx, cy) = if let Some(raw) = element_rect(el) {
                let px = rect_px(raw);
                shown.push(format!(
                    "{word} {name} {state} {},{} {}x{}",
                    px.x, px.y, px.w, px.h
                ));
                (px.x + px.w / 2, px.y + px.h / 2)
            } else {
                shown.push(format!("{word} {name} {state} no-rect"));
                return;
            };
            if seen.iter().any(|(n, x, y)| n == &name && *x == cx && *y == cy) {
                return;
            }
            seen.push((name.clone(), cx, cy));
            if kind == UIA_RadioButtonControlTypeId.0 && name == "Alpha" {
                alphas.push(state);
            } else if kind == UIA_RadioButtonControlTypeId.0 && name == "Beta" {
                betas.push((cx, cy, state));
            } else if kind == UIA_CheckBoxControlTypeId.0 && name == "Oak" {
                oaks.push(state);
            } else if kind == UIA_CheckBoxControlTypeId.0 && name == "Pine" {
                pines.push(state);
            }
        };
        for kind in [
            UIA_RadioButtonControlTypeId.0,
            UIA_CheckBoxControlTypeId.0,
            UIA_ButtonControlTypeId.0,
        ] {
            let cond = unsafe {
                auto.CreatePropertyCondition(UIA_ControlTypePropertyId, &VARIANT::from(kind))
                    .map_err(|e| format!("cond: {e}"))?
            };
            let found = unsafe {
                root.FindAll(TreeScope_Descendants, &cond)
                    .map_err(|e| format!("find: {e}"))?
            };
            let n = unsafe { found.Length().unwrap_or(0) };
            for i in 0..n {
                let el = unsafe { found.GetElement(i).map_err(|e| format!("el: {e}"))? };
                consider(
                    &el,
                    &mut shown,
                    &mut alphas,
                    &mut betas,
                    &mut oaks,
                    &mut pines,
                    &mut seen,
                );
            }
        }
        let line = if shown.is_empty() { "none".into() } else { shown.join("; ") };
        if betas.len() != 1 {
            return Err(format!("Beta count={} found={line}", betas.len()));
        }
        if alphas.len() != 1 || oaks.len() != 1 || pines.len() != 1 {
            return Err(format!(
                "Alpha count={} Oak count={} Pine count={} found={line}",
                alphas.len(),
                oaks.len(),
                pines.len()
            ));
        }
        let now = unsafe { GetForegroundWindow() };
        if now != hwnd {
            return Err(format!("window not front, not clicked found={line}"));
        }
        Ok(super::RadioPick {
            hwnd: hwnd.0 as isize,
            cx: betas[0].0,
            cy: betas[0].1,
            alpha: alphas[0].clone(),
            beta: betas[0].2.clone(),
            oak: oaks[0].clone(),
            pine: pines[0].clone(),
            found: line,
        })
    }


    fn child_label(token: isize, id: i32) -> Result<String, String> {
        use windows::Win32::Foundation::HWND;
        use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::{GetDlgItem, GetWindowTextW};
        if token == 0 {
            return Err(format!("no child {id}"));
        }
        let parent = HWND(token as *mut std::ffi::c_void);
        let child = unsafe {
            GetDlgItem(parent, id).map_err(|e| format!("no child {id}: {e}"))?
        };
        if child.0.is_null() {
            return Err(format!("no child {id}"));
        }
        let mut buf = [0u16; 128];
        let n = unsafe { GetWindowTextW(child, &mut buf) };
        if n < 0 {
            return Err(format!("child {id} text unreadable"));
        }
        let n = n as usize;
        Ok(String::from_utf16_lossy(&buf[..n.min(buf.len())]))
    }

    pub fn front_apply() -> Result<super::ApplyPick, String> {
        use windows::Win32::Foundation::HWND;
        use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::GetForegroundWindow;
        let auto = automation().map_err(|e| format!("UIA init: {e}"))?;
        let hwnd: HWND = unsafe { GetForegroundWindow() };
        if hwnd.0.is_null() {
            return Err("no front window, not clicked".into());
        }
        let label = child_label(hwnd.0 as isize, 602)?;
        let root = unsafe {
            auto.ElementFromHandle(hwnd)
                .map_err(|e| format!("window element: {e}"))?
        };
        let mut shown: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        let mut hits: Vec<(i32, i32)> = Vec::new();
        let mut seen: Vec<(String, i32, i32)> = Vec::new();
        let found = find_all_of_type(
            &auto,
            &root,
            UIA_ButtonControlTypeId.0 as i32,
            "cond",
            "find",
        )?;
        let n = unsafe { found.Length().unwrap_or(0) };
        for i in 0..n {
            let el = unsafe { found.GetElement(i).map_err(|e| format!("el: {e}"))? };
            let kind = unsafe { el.CurrentControlType().ok().map(|c| c.0).unwrap_or(0) };
            if kind != UIA_ButtonControlTypeId.0 {
                continue;
            }
            let name = element_name(&el);
            let (cx, cy, line) = if let Some(raw) = element_rect(&el) {
                let px = rect_px(raw);
                let cx = px.x + px.w / 2;
                let cy = px.y + px.h / 2;
                (cx, cy, format!("Button {name} {},{} {}x{}", px.x, px.y, px.w, px.h))
            } else {
                shown.push(format!("Button {name} no-rect"));
                continue;
            };
            shown.push(line);
            if seen.iter().any(|(nm, x, y)| nm == &name && *x == cx && *y == cy) {
                continue;
            }
            seen.push((name.clone(), cx, cy));
            if name == "Apply" {
                hits.push((cx, cy));
            }
        }
        let line = if shown.is_empty() { "none".into() } else { shown.join("; ") };
        if hits.len() != 1 {
            return Err(format!("Apply count={} label={label} found={line}", hits.len()));
        }
        let now = unsafe { GetForegroundWindow() };
        if now != hwnd {
            return Err(format!("window not front, not clicked label={label} found={line}"));
        }
        Ok(super::ApplyPick {
            hwnd: hwnd.0 as isize,
            cx: hits[0].0,
            cy: hits[0].1,
            label,
            found: line,
        })
    }


    fn window_text(hwnd: windows::Win32::Foundation::HWND) -> String {
        use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::GetWindowTextW;
        if hwnd.0.is_null() {
            return String::new();
        }
        let mut buf = [0u16; 128];
        let n = unsafe { GetWindowTextW(hwnd, &mut buf) };
        if n <= 0 {
            return String::new();
        }
        String::from_utf16_lossy(&buf[..(n as usize).min(buf.len())])
    }

    fn list_selection(hwnd: windows::Win32::Foundation::HWND) -> String {
        use windows::Win32::Foundation::{LPARAM, WPARAM};
        use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::SendMessageW;
        unsafe {
            let cur = SendMessageW(hwnd, 0x0147, WPARAM(0), LPARAM(0));
            if cur.0 < 0 {
                return String::new();
            }
            let mut buf = [0u16; 128];
            let n = SendMessageW(
                hwnd,
                0x0148,
                WPARAM(cur.0 as usize),
                LPARAM(buf.as_mut_ptr() as isize),
            );
            if n.0 <= 0 {
                return String::new();
            }
            let n = (n.0 as usize).min(buf.len());
            String::from_utf16_lossy(&buf[..n])
        }
    }

    fn value_pattern(el: &IUIAutomationElement) -> String {
        unsafe {
            let Ok(pat) = el.GetCurrentPatternAs::<IUIAutomationValuePattern>(UIA_ValuePatternId) else {
                return String::new();
            };
            pat.CurrentValue().map(|b| b.to_string()).unwrap_or_default()
        }
    }

    fn shown_text(el: &IUIAutomationElement, parent: windows::Win32::Foundation::HWND) -> String {
        use windows::Win32::Foundation::RECT;
        use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::{GetClassNameW, GetWindow, GetWindowRect, GW_CHILD, GW_HWNDNEXT};
        unsafe {
            if let Ok(hwnd) = el.CurrentNativeWindowHandle() {
                let text = window_text(hwnd);
                if !text.is_empty() {
                    return text;
                }
                let listed = list_selection(hwnd);
                if !listed.is_empty() {
                    return listed;
                }
            }
        }
        let via = value_pattern(el);
        if !via.is_empty() {
            return via;
        }
        let name = element_name(el);
        if !name.is_empty() {
            return name;
        }
        let Some(raw) = element_rect(el) else {
            return String::new();
        };
        let px = rect_px(raw);
        unsafe {
            let Ok(mut child) = GetWindow(parent, GW_CHILD) else {
                return String::new();
            };
            for _ in 0..80 {
                if child.0.is_null() {
                    break;
                }
                let mut cls = [0u16; 64];
                let n = GetClassNameW(child, &mut cls);
                let class = if n > 0 {
                    String::from_utf16_lossy(&cls[..n as usize])
                } else {
                    String::new()
                };
                if class.eq_ignore_ascii_case("ComboBox") {
                    let mut r = RECT::default();
                    let _ = GetWindowRect(child, &mut r);
                    let cx = (r.left + r.right) / 2;
                    let cy = (r.top + r.bottom) / 2;
                    if super::point_in_rect(cx, cy, &px) || (r.left == px.x && r.top == px.y) {
                        let text = window_text(child);
                        if !text.is_empty() {
                            return text;
                        }
                        let listed = list_selection(child);
                        if !listed.is_empty() {
                            return listed;
                        }
                    }
                }
                match GetWindow(child, GW_HWNDNEXT) {
                    Ok(next) => child = next,
                    Err(_) => break,
                }
            }
        }
        String::new()
    }

    fn child_line(auto: &IUIAutomation, el: &IUIAutomationElement) -> String {
        let Ok(walker) = (unsafe { auto.RawViewWalker() }) else {
            return "no-children".into();
        };
        let mut parts: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        let mut cur = unsafe { walker.GetFirstChildElement(el).ok() };
        let mut n = 0;
        while let Some(child) = cur {
            n += 1;
            if n > 16 {
                break;
            }
            let kind = unsafe { child.CurrentControlType().ok().map(|c| c.0).unwrap_or(0) };
            let name = element_name(&child);
            parts.push(format!("{} {name}", type_word(kind)));
            cur = unsafe { walker.GetNextSiblingElement(&child).ok() };
        }
        if parts.is_empty() {
            "none".into()
        } else {
            parts.join("; ")
        }
    }

    /// FindAll descendants matching a UIA control-type id.
    fn find_all_of_type(
        auto: &IUIAutomation,
        root: &IUIAutomationElement,
        type_id: i32,
        cond_msg: &str,
        find_msg: &str,
    ) -> Result<IUIAutomationElementArray, String> {
        let cond = unsafe {
            auto.CreatePropertyCondition(
                UIA_ControlTypePropertyId,
                &VARIANT::from(type_id),
            )
            .map_err(|e| format!("{cond_msg}: {e}"))?
        };
        unsafe {
            root.FindAll(TreeScope_Descendants, &cond)
                .map_err(|e| format!("{find_msg}: {e}"))
        }
    }

    fn buttons_under(
        auto: &IUIAutomation,
        root: &IUIAutomationElement,
    ) -> Result<Vec<(String, i32, i32)>, String> {
        let found = find_all_of_type(
            auto,
            root,
            UIA_ButtonControlTypeId.0 as i32,
            "button cond",
            "find buttons",
        )?;
        let n = unsafe { found.Length().unwrap_or(0) };
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        for i in 0..n {
            let el = unsafe { found.GetElement(i).map_err(|e| format!("button: {e}"))? };
            let name = element_name(&el);
            if let Some(raw) = element_rect(&el) {
                let px = rect_px(raw);
                if px.w > 0 && px.h > 0 {
                    out.push((name, px.x + px.w / 2, px.y + px.h / 2));
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(out)
    }

    pub fn front_combo() -> Result<super::ComboPick, String> {
        use windows::Win32::Foundation::HWND;
        use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::GetForegroundWindow;
        let auto = automation().map_err(|e| format!("UIA init: {e}"))?;
        let hwnd: HWND = unsafe { GetForegroundWindow() };
        if hwnd.0.is_null() {
            return Err("no front window, not clicked".into());
        }
        let root = unsafe {
            auto.ElementFromHandle(hwnd)
                .map_err(|e| format!("window element: {e}"))?
        };
        let found = find_all_of_type(
            &auto,
            &root,
            UIA_ComboBoxControlTypeId.0 as i32,
            "combo cond",
            "find combo",
        )?;
        let n = unsafe { found.Length().unwrap_or(0) };
        let mut shown: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        let mut combos: Vec<(IUIAutomationElement, super::ScreenRect, String, u32)> = Vec::new();
        for i in 0..n {
            let el = unsafe { found.GetElement(i).map_err(|e| format!("combo: {e}"))? };
            let value = shown_text(&el, hwnd);
            let pid = element_pid(&el);
            if let Some(raw) = element_rect(&el) {
                let px = rect_px(raw);
                shown.push(format!("ComboBox {value} {},{} {}x{}", px.x, px.y, px.w, px.h));
                if px.w > 0 && px.h > 0 {
                    combos.push((el, px, value, pid));
                }
            } else {
                shown.push(format!("ComboBox {value} no-rect"));
            }
        }
        let line = if shown.is_empty() { "none".into() } else { shown.join("; ") };
        if combos.len() != 1 {
            return Err(format!("ComboBox count={} found={line}", combos.len()));
        }
        let (combo, rect, value, pid) = combos.pop().unwrap();
        let mut buttons = buttons_under(&auto, &combo)?;
        if buttons.is_empty() {
            let window_buttons = buttons_under(&auto, &root)?;
            buttons = window_buttons
                .into_iter()
                .filter(|(_, x, y)| super::point_in_rect(*x, *y, &rect))
                .collect();
        }
        let mut uniq: Vec<(String, i32, i32)> = Vec::new();
        for b in buttons {
            if !uniq.iter().any(|(n, x, y)| n == &b.0 && *x == b.1 && *y == b.2) {
                uniq.push(b);
            }
        }
        let kids = child_line(&auto, &combo);
        if uniq.len() != 1 {
            return Err(format!(
                "drop count={} children={kids} found={line}",
                uniq.len()
            ));
        }
        let (button, cx, cy) = uniq.pop().unwrap();
        if button == "Close"
            || button == "Ping"
            || button == "Minimize"
            || button == "Maximize"
            || button == "Restore"
            || button == "Restore Down"
        {
            return Err(format!("drop button is {button}, not clicked children={kids}"));
        }
        let now = unsafe { GetForegroundWindow() };
        if now != hwnd {
            return Err(format!("window not front, not clicked found={line}"));
        }
        Ok(super::ComboPick {
            hwnd: hwnd.0 as isize,
            cx,
            cy,
            button,
            value,
            pid,
            found: format!("{line} children={kids}"),
        })
    }

    fn named_item_hits(
        root: &IUIAutomationElement,
        pid: u32,
        auto: &IUIAutomation,
        type_id: i32,
        want: &str,
        cond_msg: &str,
        find_msg: &str,
        el_msg: &str,
    ) -> Result<(Vec<(i32, i32)>, String), String> {
        let found = find_all_of_type(auto, root, type_id, cond_msg, find_msg)?;
        let n = unsafe { found.Length().unwrap_or(0) };
        let mut names: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        let mut hits: Vec<(i32, i32)> = Vec::new();
        for i in 0..n {
            if names.len() > 40 {
                break;
            }
            let el = unsafe { found.GetElement(i).map_err(|e| format!("{el_msg}: {e}"))? };
            if pid != 0 && element_pid(&el) != pid {
                continue;
            }
            let name = element_name(&el);
            if let Some(raw) = element_rect(&el) {
                let px = rect_px(raw);
                names.push(format!("{name} {},{} {}x{}", px.x, px.y, px.w, px.h));
                if name == want && px.w > 0 && px.h > 0 {
                    let pt = (px.x + px.w / 2, px.y + px.h / 2);
                    if !hits.contains(&pt) {
                        hits.push(pt);
                    }
                }
            } else {
                names.push(format!("{name} no-rect"));
            }
        }
        let line = if names.is_empty() { "none".into() } else { names.join("; ") };
        Ok((hits, line))
    }

    fn list_hits(root: &IUIAutomationElement, pid: u32, auto: &IUIAutomation) -> Result<(Vec<(i32, i32)>, String), String> {
        named_item_hits(
            root,
            pid,
            auto,
            UIA_ListItemControlTypeId.0 as i32,
            "Green",
            "item cond",
            "find items",
            "item",
        )
    }

    pub fn green_item(pid: u32, token: isize) -> Result<(i32, i32, String), String> {
        if pid == 0 {
            return Err("no process, not clicked".into());
        }
        use windows::Win32::Foundation::HWND;
        use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::GetForegroundWindow;
        let auto = automation().map_err(|e| format!("UIA init: {e}"))?;
        if token != 0 {
            let hwnd = HWND(token as *mut std::ffi::c_void);
            if let Ok(win) = unsafe { auto.ElementFromHandle(hwnd) } {
                let (greens, line) = list_hits(&win, pid, &auto)?;
                if greens.len() == 1 {
                    return Ok((greens[0].0, greens[0].1, line));
                }
                if greens.len() > 1 {
                    return Err(format!("Green count={} items={line}", greens.len()));
                }
            }
        }
        let front = unsafe { GetForegroundWindow() };
        if !front.0.is_null() {
            if let Ok(win) = unsafe { auto.ElementFromHandle(front) } {
                let (greens, line) = list_hits(&win, pid, &auto)?;
                if greens.len() == 1 {
                    return Ok((greens[0].0, greens[0].1, format!("front {line}")));
                }
                if greens.len() > 1 {
                    return Err(format!("Green count={} items={line}", greens.len()));
                }
            }
        }
        let root = desktop_root(&auto).map_err(|e| format!("root: {e}"))?;
        let (greens, line) = list_hits(&root, pid, &auto)?;
        if greens.len() != 1 {
            return Err(format!("Green count={} items={line}", greens.len()));
        }
        Ok((greens[0].0, greens[0].1, format!("desktop {line}")))
    }


    fn menu_hits(
        auto: &IUIAutomation,
        root: &IUIAutomationElement,
        pid: u32,
        want: &str,
    ) -> Result<(Vec<(i32, i32)>, String), String> {
        named_item_hits(
            root,
            pid,
            auto,
            UIA_MenuItemControlTypeId.0 as i32,
            want,
            "menu cond",
            "find menu",
            "menu item",
        )
    }

    pub fn front_child(id: i32) -> Result<String, String> {
        use windows::Win32::Foundation::HWND;
        use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::GetForegroundWindow;
        let hwnd: HWND = unsafe { GetForegroundWindow() };
        if hwnd.0.is_null() {
            return Err("no front window, not clicked".into());
        }
        child_label(hwnd.0 as isize, id)
    }

    pub fn front_file() -> Result<super::MenuPick, String> {
        use windows::Win32::Foundation::HWND;
        use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::GetForegroundWindow;
        let auto = automation().map_err(|e| format!("UIA init: {e}"))?;
        let hwnd: HWND = unsafe { GetForegroundWindow() };
        if hwnd.0.is_null() {
            return Err("no front window, not clicked".into());
        }
        let root = unsafe {
            auto.ElementFromHandle(hwnd)
                .map_err(|e| format!("window element: {e}"))?
        };
        let (hits, line) = menu_hits(&auto, &root, 0, "File")?;
        if hits.len() != 1 {
            let kids = child_line(&auto, &root);
            return Err(format!(
                "File count={} items={line} children={kids}",
                hits.len()
            ));
        }
        let pid = element_pid(&root);
        let now = unsafe { GetForegroundWindow() };
        if now != hwnd {
            return Err(format!("window not front, not clicked items={line}"));
        }
        Ok(super::MenuPick {
            hwnd: hwnd.0 as isize,
            cx: hits[0].0,
            cy: hits[0].1,
            name: "File".into(),
            pid,
            found: line,
        })
    }

    pub fn mark_item(pid: u32, token: isize) -> Result<(i32, i32, String), String> {
        if pid == 0 {
            return Err("no process, not clicked".into());
        }
        use windows::Win32::Foundation::HWND;
        use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::GetForegroundWindow;
        let auto = automation().map_err(|e| format!("UIA init: {e}"))?;
        if token != 0 {
            let hwnd = HWND(token as *mut std::ffi::c_void);
            if let Ok(win) = unsafe { auto.ElementFromHandle(hwnd) } {
                let (hits, line) = menu_hits(&auto, &win, pid, "Mark")?;
                if hits.len() == 1 {
                    return Ok((hits[0].0, hits[0].1, line));
                }
                if hits.len() > 1 {
                    return Err(format!("Mark count={} items={line}", hits.len()));
                }
            }
        }
        let front = unsafe { GetForegroundWindow() };
        if !front.0.is_null() {
            if let Ok(win) = unsafe { auto.ElementFromHandle(front) } {
                let (hits, line) = menu_hits(&auto, &win, pid, "Mark")?;
                if hits.len() == 1 {
                    return Ok((hits[0].0, hits[0].1, format!("front {line}")));
                }
                if hits.len() > 1 {
                    return Err(format!("Mark count={} items={line}", hits.len()));
                }
            }
        }
        let root = desktop_root(&auto).map_err(|e| format!("root: {e}"))?;
        let (hits, line) = menu_hits(&auto, &root, pid, "Mark")?;
        if hits.len() != 1 {
            return Err(format!("Mark count={} items={line}", hits.len()));
        }
        Ok((hits[0].0, hits[0].1, format!("desktop {line}")))
    }


    pub fn front_ping() -> Result<super::MinimizeHit, String> {
        use windows::Win32::Foundation::HWND;
        use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::GetForegroundWindow;
        let auto = automation().map_err(|e| format!("UIA init: {e}"))?;
        let hwnd: HWND = unsafe { GetForegroundWindow() };
        if hwnd.0.is_null() {
            return Err("no front window, not clicked".into());
        }
        let root = unsafe {
            auto.ElementFromHandle(hwnd)
                .map_err(|e| format!("window element: {e}"))?
        };
        let found = find_all_of_type(
            &auto,
            &root,
            UIA_ButtonControlTypeId.0 as i32,
            "cond",
            "find",
        )?;
        let n = unsafe { found.Length().unwrap_or(0) };
        let mut shown: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        let mut hits: Vec<(i32, i32)> = Vec::new();
        for i in 0..n {
            let el = unsafe { found.GetElement(i).map_err(|e| format!("el: {e}"))? };
            let kind = unsafe { el.CurrentControlType().ok().map(|c| c.0).unwrap_or(0) };
            if kind != UIA_ButtonControlTypeId.0 {
                continue;
            }
            let name = element_name(&el);
            let Some(raw) = element_rect(&el) else {
                shown.push(format!("Button {name} no-rect"));
                continue;
            };
            let px = rect_px(raw);
            if px.w <= 0 || px.h <= 0 {
                shown.push(format!("Button {name} empty"));
                continue;
            }
            let cx = px.x + px.w / 2;
            let cy = px.y + px.h / 2;
            shown.push(format!("Button {name} {},{} {}x{}", px.x, px.y, px.w, px.h));
            if name == "Ping" && !hits.contains(&(cx, cy)) {
                hits.push((cx, cy));
            }
        }
        let line = if shown.is_empty() { "none".into() } else { shown.join("; ") };
        if hits.len() != 1 {
            return Err(format!("Ping count={} found={line}", hits.len()));
        }
        let now = unsafe { GetForegroundWindow() };
        if now != hwnd {
            return Err(format!("window not front, not clicked found={line}"));
        }
        Ok(super::MinimizeHit {
            hwnd: hwnd.0 as isize,
            cx: hits[0].0,
            cy: hits[0].1,
            names: line,
        })
    }


    fn window_style(hwnd: windows::Win32::Foundation::HWND) -> i32 {
        use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::{GetWindowLongW, GWL_STYLE};
        unsafe { GetWindowLongW(hwnd, GWL_STYLE) }
    }

    fn edit_window_text(hwnd: windows::Win32::Foundation::HWND) -> String {
        use windows::Win32::Foundation::{LPARAM, WPARAM};
        use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::SendMessageW;
        if hwnd.0.is_null() {
            return String::new();
        }
        unsafe {
            let n = SendMessageW(hwnd, 0x000E, WPARAM(0), LPARAM(0));
            if n.0 <= 0 {
                return String::new();
            }
            let len = ((n.0 as usize).saturating_add(1)).min(4096);
            let mut buf = vec![0u16; len];
            let got = SendMessageW(
                hwnd,
                0x000D,
                WPARAM(len),
                LPARAM(buf.as_mut_ptr() as isize),
            );
            if got.0 <= 0 {
                return String::new();
            }
            let n = (got.0 as usize).min(buf.len());
            String::from_utf16_lossy(&buf[..n])
        }
    }

    pub fn edit_text(token: isize) -> Result<String, String> {
        use windows::Win32::Foundation::HWND;
        if token == 0 {
            return Err("no edit".into());
        }
        Ok(edit_window_text(HWND(token as *mut std::ffi::c_void)))
    }

    pub fn edit_focused(token: isize) -> bool {
        use windows::Win32::Foundation::HWND;
        if token == 0 {
            return false;
        }
        let Ok(auto) = automation() else {
            return false;
        };
        let hwnd = HWND(token as *mut std::ffi::c_void);
        let Ok(el) = (unsafe { auto.ElementFromHandle(hwnd) }) else {
            return false;
        };
        unsafe { el.CurrentHasKeyboardFocus().map(|v| v.as_bool()).unwrap_or(false) }
    }

    pub fn front_edit() -> Result<super::EditPick, String> {
        use windows::Win32::Foundation::HWND;
        use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::GetForegroundWindow;
        let auto = automation().map_err(|e| format!("UIA init: {e}"))?;
        let hwnd: HWND = unsafe { GetForegroundWindow() };
        if hwnd.0.is_null() {
            return Err("no front window, not typed".into());
        }
        let root = unsafe {
            auto.ElementFromHandle(hwnd)
                .map_err(|e| format!("window element: {e}"))?
        };
        let cond = unsafe {
            auto.CreatePropertyCondition(
                UIA_ControlTypePropertyId,
                &VARIANT::from(UIA_EditControlTypeId.0 as i32),
            )
            .map_err(|e| format!("edit cond: {e}"))?
        };
        let found = unsafe {
            root.FindAll(TreeScope_Descendants, &cond)
                .map_err(|e| format!("find edit: {e}"))?
        };
        let n = unsafe { found.Length().unwrap_or(0) };
        let mut shown: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        let mut hits: Vec<(isize, i32, i32, String)> = Vec::new();
        for i in 0..n {
            let el = unsafe { found.GetElement(i).map_err(|e| format!("edit: {e}"))? };
            let name = element_name(&el);
            let Some(raw) = element_rect(&el) else {
                shown.push(format!("Edit {name} no-rect"));
                continue;
            };
            let px = rect_px(raw);
            let native = unsafe { el.CurrentNativeWindowHandle().ok() };
            let edit_hwnd = native.filter(|h| !h.0.is_null());
            let style = edit_hwnd.map(|h| window_style(h)).unwrap_or(0);
            let multi = (style as u32) & 0x0004 != 0;
            shown.push(format!(
                "Edit {name} {},{} {}x{} multi={multi}",
                px.x, px.y, px.w, px.h
            ));
            if multi && px.w > 0 && px.h > 0 {
                if let Some(h) = edit_hwnd {
                    let cx = px.x + px.w / 2;
                    let cy = px.y + px.h / 2;
                    if !hits.iter().any(|row| row.0 == h.0 as isize) {
                        hits.push((h.0 as isize, cx, cy, name));
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        let line = if shown.is_empty() { "none".into() } else { shown.join("; ") };
        if hits.len() != 1 {
            return Err(format!("Edit count={} found={line}", hits.len()));
        }
        let now = unsafe { GetForegroundWindow() };
        if now != hwnd {
            return Err(format!("window not front, not typed found={line}"));
        }
        let text = edit_window_text(HWND(hits[0].0 as *mut std::ffi::c_void));
        Ok(super::EditPick {
            hwnd: hwnd.0 as isize,
            edit: hits[0].0,
            cx: hits[0].1,
            cy: hits[0].2,
            text,
            found: line,
        })
    }

    // silence unused imports on some SDK shapes
    #[allow(dead_code)]
    fn _touch() {
        let _ = (TreeScope_Children, UIA_NamePropertyId, UIA_BoundingRectanglePropertyId, BSTR::default(), RECT::default());
    }
}

#[cfg(not(all(windows, feature = "win32")))]
pub fn front_child(_id: i32) -> Result<String, String> {

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;





    #[test]
    fn thumb_drag_stays_inside_the_bar() {
        let bar = ScreenRect { x: 500, y: 100, w: 16, h: 200 };
        let thumb = ScreenRect { x: 501, y: 120, w: 14, h: 40 };
        let (sx, sy, ex, ey) = thumb_drag_ends(&bar, &thumb).expect("drag");
        assert_eq!((sx, sy), (508, 140));
        assert_eq!(ex, sx);
        assert!(ey > sy);
        assert!(point_in_rect(sx, sy, &bar));
        assert!(point_in_rect(ex, ey, &bar));
        let tiny = ScreenRect { x: 500, y: 100, w: 16, h: 8 };
        let cramped = ScreenRect { x: 501, y: 101, w: 14, h: 6 };
        assert!(thumb_drag_ends(&tiny, &cramped).is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn title_point_avoids_caption_buttons() {
        let title = ScreenRect { x: 100, y: 40, w: 400, h: 30 };
        let blocks = vec![
            ScreenRect { x: 430, y: 42, w: 20, h: 22 },
            ScreenRect { x: 454, y: 42, w: 20, h: 22 },
            ScreenRect { x: 478, y: 42, w: 20, h: 22 },
        ];
        let (x, y) = clear_title_point(&title, &blocks).expect("point");
        assert!(point_in_rect(x, y, &title));
        for b in &blocks {
            assert!(!point_in_rect(x, y, b));
        }
        let covered = vec![ScreenRect { x: 100, y: 40, w: 400, h: 30 }];
        assert!(clear_title_point(&title, &covered).is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn maximize_name_is_not_restore() {
        let names = vec!["Restore".to_string(), "Close".to_string()];
        assert!(exact_name_indexes(&names, "Maximize").is_empty());
        let one = vec!["Restore Down".to_string()];
        assert_eq!(exact_name_indexes(&one, "Restore Down"), vec![0]);
        assert!(exact_name_indexes(&one, "Restore").is_empty());
        assert!(exact_name_indexes(&one, "Maximize").is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn minimize_name_must_be_exact_and_alone() {
        let one = vec!["Close".to_string(), "Minimize".to_string(), "Maximize".to_string()];
        assert_eq!(exact_name_indexes(&one, "Minimize"), vec![1]);
        let none = vec!["Close".to_string()];
        assert!(exact_name_indexes(&none, "Minimize").is_empty());
        assert_eq!(names_line(&none), "Close");
        let two = vec!["Minimize".to_string(), "Minimize".to_string()];
        assert_eq!(exact_name_indexes(&two, "Minimize").len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(names_line(&[]), "none");
    }

    #[test]
    fn arrow_from_brave_remember_rect() {

    }

    #[test]
    fn default_inset_is_twelve() {
        assert_eq!(DropdownOpts::default().arrow_inset_px, 12);
    }

    fn hit(name: &str, process: &str, window: &str) -> ComboHit {
        ComboHit {
            name: name.into(),
            process: process.into(),
            window_title: window.into(),
            x: 0,
            y: 0,
            width: 152,
            height: 32,
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn process_matches_stem_and_exe() {
        assert!(process_matches("powershell", "powershell"));
        assert!(process_matches("powershell.exe", "powershell"));
        assert!(process_matches("BRAVE", "brave"));
        assert!(process_matches("", ""));
        assert!(!process_matches("brave", "powershell"));
        assert!(!process_matches("powershell", "brave"));
    }

    }
