===== FILE: src\uipi.rs =====
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
//! UIPI tagging (order 17d).
//!
//! Before the first button-down (the same places as the hit-test: `click`/`drag` pre-check
//! before any input, other verbs at their first button-down), sm reads the integrity level of
//! the process that owns the root window under the press point:
//! `GetWindowThreadProcessId` -> `OpenProcess(PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION)` ->
//! `OpenProcessToken(TOKEN_QUERY)` -> `GetTokenInformation(TokenIntegrityLevel)` -> last SID
//! sub-authority (RID). Our own token is read the same way.
//!
//! If the target level is HIGHER than ours, Windows' UIPI would silently drop our injected
//! input. sm then sends NO input, prints `UIPI_BLOCKED target=<level> ours=<level> hwnd=.. pid=..`
//! on stderr and exits 9 (`cli::EXIT_UIPI_BLOCKED`). If any query fails (for example access
//! denied on a protected process), integrity is `unknown`: logged, and NOT blocked.
//! Keyboard-only verbs (`key`, `type`) are not gated (they have no press point).

use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};
use std::sync::Mutex;

/// Mandatory integrity levels by RID (winnt.h SECURITY_MANDATORY_*_RID).
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord)]
pub enum Level {
    Untrusted,
    Low,
    Medium,
    MediumPlus,
    High,
    System,
    Protected,
}

impl Level {
    pub fn name(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Level::Untrusted => "untrusted",
            Level::Low => "low",
            Level::Medium => "medium",
            Level::MediumPlus => "medium-plus",
            Level::High => "high",
            Level::System => "system",
            Level::Protected => "protected",
        }
    }
}

/// RID -> level. 0x0000 untrusted, 0x1000 low, 0x2000 medium, 0x2100 medium-plus,
/// 0x3000 high, 0x4000 system, 0x5000 protected process. Values in between round down.
pub fn level_from_rid(rid: u32) -> Level {
    match rid {
        0..=0x0FFF => Level::Untrusted,
        0x1000..=0x1FFF => Level::Low,
        0x2000..=0x20FF => Level::Medium,
        0x2100..=0x2FFF => Level::MediumPlus,
        0x3000..=0x3FFF => Level::High,
        0x4000..=0x4FFF => Level::System,
        _ => Level::Protected,
    }
}

/// Integrity of one process: known RID or unknown (reason).
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Integrity {
    Known(u32),
    Unknown(String),
}

impl Integrity {
    pub fn label(&self) -> String {
        match self {
            Integrity::Known(r) => format!("{}(0x{r:04X})", level_from_rid(*r).name()),
            Integrity::Unknown(_) => "unknown".into(),
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Decision {
    /// Target equal or lower: input allowed.
    Allow,
    /// Target higher than us: UIPI would drop our input. Block (exit 9).
    Block,
    /// A query failed: logged as integrity=unknown, never blocks.
    AllowUnknown,
}

/// Pure compare: block only when both levels are known and the target's is higher.
pub fn decide(target: &Integrity, ours: &Integrity) -> Decision {
    match (target, ours) {
        (Integrity::Known(t), Integrity::Known(o)) => {
            if level_from_rid(*t) > level_from_rid(*o) {
                Decision::Block
            } else {
                Decision::Allow
            }
        }
        _ => Decision::AllowUnknown,
    }
}

/// One probe result.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Probe {
    pub hwnd: isize,
    pub pid: u32,
    pub title: String,
    pub target: Integrity,
    pub ours: Integrity,
}

impl Probe {
    pub fn decision(&self) -> Decision {
        decide(&self.target, &self.ours)
    }
    pub fn line(&self, stage: &str) -> String {
        let reason = match (&self.target, &self.ours) {
            (Integrity::Unknown(r), _) => format!(" reason='{r}'"),
            (_, Integrity::Unknown(r)) => format!(" reason='ours: {r}'"),
            _ => String::new(),
        };
        format!(
            "uipi stage={stage} hwnd={} pid={} title='{}' integrity={} ours={} decision={:?}{reason}",
            self.hwnd,
            self.pid,
            self.title,
            self.target.label(),
            self.ours.label(),
            self.decision()
        )
    }
    pub fn blocked_line(&self) -> String {
        let lv = |i: &Integrity| match i {
            Integrity::Known(r) => level_from_rid(*r).name().to_string(),
            Integrity::Unknown(_) => "unknown".into(),
        };
        format!(
            "UIPI_BLOCKED target={} ours={} hwnd={} pid={} title='{}'; no input sent (exit 9)",
            lv(&self.target),
            lv(&self.ours),
            self.hwnd,
            self.pid,
            self.title
        )
    }
}

static BLOCKED: AtomicBool = AtomicBool::new(false);
static MSG: Mutex<Option<String>> = Mutex::new(None);

pub fn reset() {
    BLOCKED.store(false, Ordering::Relaxed);
    if let Ok(mut m) = MSG.lock() {
        *m = None;
    }
}

/// True once a UIPI block stopped the command (no input is sent after it).
pub fn blocked() -> bool {
    BLOCKED.load(Ordering::Relaxed)
}

/// Stderr text for exit 9.
pub fn message() -> String {
    MSG.lock()
        .ok()
        .and_then(|m| m.clone())
        .unwrap_or_else(|| "UIPI_BLOCKED (exit 9)".into())
}

/// Gate one press point: probe the root window's integrity. Err = UIPI_BLOCKED text.
/// Unknown integrity is logged (stderr warning + hit-test log lines) and allowed.
pub fn gate(x: i32, y: i32, stage: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
    if blocked() {
        return Err(message());
    }
    let p = probe_point(x, y);
    let line = p.line(stage);
    crate::hittest::push_line(line.clone());
    match p.decision() {
        Decision::Allow => Ok(()),
        Decision::AllowUnknown => {
            eprintln!("warning: {line} (integrity unknown: not blocked)");
            Ok(())
        }
        Decision::Block => {
            let m = p.blocked_line();
            BLOCKED.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed);
            if let Ok(mut g) = MSG.lock() {
                *g = Some(m.clone());
            }
            Err(m)
        }
    }
}

/// `sm integrity` output line (read-only).
pub fn verb_line(p: &Probe) -> String {
    let ours_lower = match (&p.target, &p.ours) {
        (Integrity::Known(t), Integrity::Known(o)) => {
            (level_from_rid(*o) < level_from_rid(*t)).to_string()
        }
        _ => "unknown".into(),
    };
    let rid = match &p.target {
        Integrity::Known(r) => format!("0x{r:04X}"),
        Integrity::Unknown(_) => "n/a".into(),
    };
    let reason = match &p.target {
        Integrity::Unknown(r) => format!(" reason='{r}'"),
        _ => String::new(),
    };
    format!(
        "integrity hwnd={} pid={} title='{}' level={} rid={rid} ours={} ours_lower={ours_lower} would_block={}{reason}",
        p.hwnd,
        p.pid,
        p.title,
        match &p.target {
            Integrity::Known(r) => level_from_rid(*r).name().to_string(),
            Integrity::Unknown(_) => "unknown".into(),
        },
        p.ours.label(),
        p.decision() == Decision::Block
    )
}

// ---- Windows queries ------------------------------------------------------------

#[cfg(all(windows, feature = "win32"))]
fn token_rid(process: windows::Win32::Foundation::HANDLE) -> Integrity {
    use windows::Win32::Foundation::{CloseHandle, HANDLE};
    use windows::Win32::Security::{
        GetSidSubAuthority, GetSidSubAuthorityCount, GetTokenInformation, TokenIntegrityLevel,
        TOKEN_MANDATORY_LABEL, TOKEN_QUERY,
    };
    use windows::Win32::System::Threading::OpenProcessToken;
    unsafe {
        let mut tok = HANDLE::default();
        if let Err(e) = OpenProcessToken(process, TOKEN_QUERY, &mut tok) {
            return Integrity::Unknown(format!("OpenProcessToken: {e}"));
        }
        let mut buf = [0u64; 16]; // 128 bytes, 8-aligned
        let mut ret = 0u32;
        let r = GetTokenInformation(
            tok,
            TokenIntegrityLevel,
            Some(buf.as_mut_ptr() as *mut core::ffi::c_void),
            std::mem::size_of_val(&buf) as u32,
            &mut ret,
        );
        let out = match r {
            Err(e) => Integrity::Unknown(format!("GetTokenInformation: {e}")),
            Ok(()) => {
                let label = &*(buf.as_ptr() as *const TOKEN_MANDATORY_LABEL);
                let sid = label.Label.Sid;
                let cnt = GetSidSubAuthorityCount(sid);
                if cnt.is_null() || *cnt == 0 {
                    Integrity::Unknown("integrity SID has no sub-authority".into())
                } else {
                    let rid = GetSidSubAuthority(sid, (*cnt - 1) as u32);
                    if rid.is_null() {
                        Integrity::Unknown("GetSidSubAuthority failed".into())
                    } else {
                        Integrity::Known(*rid)
                    }
                }
            }
        };
        let _ = CloseHandle(tok);
        out
    }
}

#[cfg(all(windows, feature = "win32"))]
pub fn ours() -> Integrity {
    use windows::Win32::System::Threading::GetCurrentProcess;
    unsafe { token_rid(GetCurrentProcess()) }
}

/// Integrity of the process owning `hwnd` (read-only queries).
#[cfg(all(windows, feature = "win32"))]
pub fn probe_hwnd(hwnd: isize) -> Probe {
    use windows::Win32::Foundation::{CloseHandle, HWND};
    use windows::Win32::System::Threading::{OpenProcess, PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION};
    use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::{GetWindowThreadProcessId, InternalGetWindowText, IsWindow};
    let h = HWND(hwnd as *mut core::ffi::c_void);
    let ours = ours();
    unsafe {
        if hwnd == 0 || !IsWindow(h).as_bool() {
            return Probe { hwnd, pid: 0, title: String::new(), target: Integrity::Unknown("no window".into()), ours };
        }
        let mut buf = [0u16; 256];
        let n = InternalGetWindowText(h, &mut buf);
        let title = String::from_utf16_lossy(&buf[..n.max(0) as usize]);
        let mut pid = 0u32;
        GetWindowThreadProcessId(h, Some(&mut pid));
        let target = if pid == 0 {
            Integrity::Unknown("GetWindowThreadProcessId gave pid 0".into())
        } else {
            match OpenProcess(PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION, false, pid) {
                Err(e) => Integrity::Unknown(format!("OpenProcess: {e}")),
                Ok(ph) => {
                    let t = token_rid(ph);
                    let _ = CloseHandle(ph);
                    t
                }
            }
        };
        Probe { hwnd, pid, title, target, ours }
    }
}

/// Root window under a screen point (WindowFromPoint -> GetAncestor GA_ROOT).
#[cfg(all(windows, feature = "win32"))]
pub fn root_at(x: i32, y: i32) -> isize {
    use windows::Win32::Foundation::POINT;
    use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::{GetAncestor, WindowFromPoint, GA_ROOT};
    crate::native::ensure_dpi();
    unsafe {
        let hit = WindowFromPoint(POINT { x, y });
        if hit.0.is_null() {
            0
        } else {
            GetAncestor(hit, GA_ROOT).0 as isize
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(not(all(windows, feature = "win32")))]
pub fn ours() -> Integrity {
    Integrity::Unknown("needs Windows".into())
}

#[cfg(not(all(windows, feature = "win32")))]
pub fn probe_hwnd(hwnd: isize) -> Probe {
    Probe { hwnd, pid: 0, title: String::new(), target: Integrity::Unknown("needs Windows".into()), ours: ours() }
}

#[cfg(not(all(windows, feature = "win32")))]
pub fn root_at(_x: i32, _y: i32) -> isize {
    0
}

pub fn probe_point(x: i32, y: i32) -> Probe {
    probe_hwnd(root_at(x, y))
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn rid_to_level_mapping() {
        assert_eq!(level_from_rid(0x0000), Level::Untrusted);
        assert_eq!(level_from_rid(0x1000), Level::Low);
        assert_eq!(level_from_rid(0x2000), Level::Medium);
        assert_eq!(level_from_rid(0x2100), Level::MediumPlus);
        assert_eq!(level_from_rid(0x3000), Level::High);
        assert_eq!(level_from_rid(0x4000), Level::System);
        assert_eq!(level_from_rid(0x5000), Level::Protected);
        assert_eq!(level_from_rid(0x2010), Level::Medium);
        assert_eq!(Level::MediumPlus.name(), "medium-plus");
        assert_eq!(Integrity::Known(0x2000).label(), "medium(0x2000)");
    }

    #[test]
    fn higher_blocks_equal_or_lower_or_unknown_does_not() {
        let m = Integrity::Known(0x2000);
        let h = Integrity::Known(0x3000);
        let l = Integrity::Known(0x1000);
        let s = Integrity::Known(0x4000);
        let u = Integrity::Unknown("access denied".into());
        assert_eq!(decide(&h, &m), Decision::Block);
        assert_eq!(decide(&s, &m), Decision::Block);
        assert_eq!(decide(&Integrity::Known(0x2100), &m), Decision::Block);
        assert_eq!(decide(&m, &m), Decision::Allow);
        assert_eq!(decide(&l, &m), Decision::Allow);
        assert_eq!(decide(&m, &h), Decision::Allow);
        assert_eq!(decide(&u, &m), Decision::AllowUnknown);
        assert_eq!(decide(&h, &u), Decision::AllowUnknown);
        let p = Probe { hwnd: 5, pid: 42, title: "Admin".into(), target: h.clone(), ours: m.clone() };
        assert_eq!(p.blocked_line(), "UIPI_BLOCKED target=high ours=medium hwnd=5 pid=42 title='Admin'; no input sent (exit 9)");
        assert!(verb_line(&p).contains("level=high rid=0x3000 ours=medium(0x2000) ours_lower=true would_block=true"));
        let q = Probe { hwnd: 5, pid: 42, title: "x".into(), target: u, ours: m };
        assert!(verb_line(&q).contains("level=unknown rid=n/a"));
        assert!(verb_line(&q).contains("ours_lower=unknown would_block=false"));
        assert!(q.line("down").contains("integrity=unknown") && q.line("down").contains("decision=AllowUnknown"));
    }
}


===== FILE: src\window_drop.rs =====
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
//! Drag from one window into another.
//!
//! Two windows, side by side. Not a use case.
//! Reuses click --button drag. No second command.
//! The drop point must not be covered. A drop on a window behind
//! another is not a pass. Neither window is closed or minimized.
//! Pass when the item is on the other window.

pub fn drop_not_covered(top_is_target: bool) -> bool {
    top_is_target
}

pub fn arrived(started_on_a: bool, ended_on_b: bool) -> bool {
    started_on_a && ended_on_b
}

pub fn both_still_open(alive: bool, a_iconic: bool, b_iconic: bool) -> bool {
    alive && !a_iconic && !b_iconic
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn covered_drop_is_a_fail() {
        assert!(drop_not_covered(true));
        assert!(!drop_not_covered(false));
    }

    #[test]
    fn item_has_to_arrive_and_both_stay_open() {
        assert!(arrived(true, true));
        assert!(!arrived(true, false));
        assert!(both_still_open(true, false, false));
        assert!(!both_still_open(true, true, false));
        assert!(!both_still_open(false, false, false));
    }
}


===== FILE: src\winevents.rs =====
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
//! WinEvent listener (order 13B). REFERENCE-ONLY: it records what the window
//! manager announced; it never decides an action and never changes an exit code.
//!
//! An event means the WM STARTED something (move/size, minimize, snap preview,
//! foreground change), not that it settled. The verdict still comes from a PNG plus
//! DWM extended frame bounds read AFTER the action.
//!
//! Hooks: `SetWinEventHook(min, max, NULL, cb, 0, 0, WINEVENT_OUTOFCONTEXT |
//! WINEVENT_SKIPOWNPROCESS)` for the ranges in [`HOOK_RANGES`], installed on a
//! dedicated thread with its own GetMessage loop (out-of-context callbacks are
//! delivered through that loop). Same RAII pattern as `override_guard`: `start`
//! returns once the hooks are live; Drop posts WM_QUIT, joins the thread, and the
//! thread calls UnhookWinEvent (also on error / panic unwind).
//!
//! Filter: idObject == OBJID_WINDOW (0) and idChild == CHILDID_SELF (0), and the
//! event is one of the named ones. The callback only reads the title
//! (InternalGetWindowText, sends no message) and DWM frame bounds, pushes a record
//! into a mutex list, and returns; the file is written after the command.

use std::sync::Mutex;

pub const EVENT_SYSTEM_FOREGROUND: u32 = 0x0003;
pub const EVENT_SYSTEM_MOVESIZESTART: u32 = 0x000A;
pub const EVENT_SYSTEM_MOVESIZEEND: u32 = 0x000B;
pub const EVENT_SYSTEM_SWITCHSTART: u32 = 0x0014;
pub const EVENT_SYSTEM_SWITCHEND: u32 = 0x0015;
pub const EVENT_SYSTEM_MINIMIZESTART: u32 = 0x0016;
pub const EVENT_SYSTEM_MINIMIZEEND: u32 = 0x0017;
/// Snap layout / arrangement preview (Windows spelling: ARRANGMENTPREVIEW).
pub const EVENT_SYSTEM_ARRANGMENTPREVIEW: u32 = 0x8016;

/// (min, max) ranges passed to SetWinEventHook; one hook per range.
pub const HOOK_RANGES: [(u32, u32); 4] = [
    (EVENT_SYSTEM_FOREGROUND, EVENT_SYSTEM_FOREGROUND),
    (EVENT_SYSTEM_MOVESIZESTART, EVENT_SYSTEM_MOVESIZEEND),
    (EVENT_SYSTEM_SWITCHSTART, EVENT_SYSTEM_MINIMIZEEND),
    (EVENT_SYSTEM_ARRANGMENTPREVIEW, EVENT_SYSTEM_ARRANGMENTPREVIEW),
];

/// Trailing listen time after the command so late MOVESIZEEND / FOREGROUND land.
pub const TAIL_MS: u64 = 300;

/// Name of a kept event, None = not one of ours.
pub fn event_name(e: u32) -> Option<&'static str> {
    Some(match e {
        EVENT_SYSTEM_FOREGROUND => "FOREGROUND",
        EVENT_SYSTEM_MOVESIZESTART => "MOVESIZESTART",
        EVENT_SYSTEM_MOVESIZEEND => "MOVESIZEEND",
        EVENT_SYSTEM_SWITCHSTART => "SWITCHSTART",
        EVENT_SYSTEM_SWITCHEND => "SWITCHEND",
        EVENT_SYSTEM_MINIMIZESTART => "MINIMIZESTART",
        EVENT_SYSTEM_MINIMIZEEND => "MINIMIZEEND",
        EVENT_SYSTEM_ARRANGMENTPREVIEW => "ARRANGMENTPREVIEW",
        _ => return None,
    })
}

/// Keep only window-level events: idObject == OBJID_WINDOW (0), idChild == CHILDID_SELF (0).
pub fn keep_event(event: u32, id_object: i32, id_child: i32) -> bool {
    id_object == 0 && id_child == 0 && event_name(event).is_some()
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct EvRec {
    /// dwmsEventTime (GetTickCount ms).
    pub t: u32,
    pub event: u32,
    pub hwnd: isize,
    pub title: String,
    /// DWMWA_EXTENDED_FRAME_BOUNDS at callback time (l, t, r, b).
    pub frame: Option<(i32, i32, i32, i32)>,
}

/// `+<ms since listen start> t=<tick> <NAME> hwnd=<n> title='<s>' frame=(l,t,r,b)|frame=n/a`
pub fn format_line(r: &EvRec, t0: u32) -> String {
    let rel = r.t.wrapping_sub(t0) as i32;
    let frame = match r.frame {
        Some((l, t, rr, b)) => format!("frame=({l},{t},{rr},{b})"),
        None => "frame=n/a".into(),
    };
    format!(
        "+{rel} t={} {} hwnd={} title='{}' {frame}",
        r.t,
        event_name(r.event).unwrap_or("?"),
        r.hwnd,
        r.title
    )
}

static EVENTS: Mutex<Vec<EvRec>> = Mutex::new(Vec::new());
const MAX_EVENTS: usize = 20_000;

fn push(r: EvRec) {
    if let Ok(mut v) = EVENTS.lock() {
        if v.len() < MAX_EVENTS {
            v.push(r);
        }
    }
}

fn take() -> Vec<EvRec> {
    EVENTS
        .lock()
        .map(|mut v| std::mem::take(&mut *v))
        .unwrap_or_default()
}

/// Write the events file (after the command).
pub fn write_events(
    path: &str,
    what: &str,
    t0: u32,
    hooks: usize,
    recs: &[EvRec],
    extra: &[String],
) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    use std::io::Write;
    let mut f = std::fs::File::create(path)?;
    writeln!(f, "# sm --events : WinEvents during `{what}` (reference-only)")?;
    writeln!(
        f,
        "# an event means STARTED, not settled; the verdict is PNG + DWM frame bounds after the action"
    )?;
    writeln!(
        f,
        "# hooks={hooks}/4 OUTOFCONTEXT|SKIPOWNPROCESS; FOREGROUND 0x0003, MOVESIZESTART/END 0x000A/0x000B, SWITCHSTART/END 0x0014/0x0015, MINIMIZESTART/END 0x0016/0x0017, ARRANGMENTPREVIEW 0x8016; idObject=0 idChild=0 only"
    )?;
    writeln!(f, "# +ms t=<tick> EVENT hwnd=<n> title='<s>' frame=(l,t,r,b)|n/a   (t0={t0})")?;
    for r in recs {
        writeln!(f, "{}", format_line(r, t0))?;
    }
    for x in extra {
        writeln!(f, "# {x}")?;
    }
    writeln!(f, "events={}", recs.len())?;
    Ok(())
}

// ---- Windows implementation ---------------------------------------------------

#[cfg(all(windows, feature = "win32"))]
mod imp {
    use super::*;
    use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU32, Ordering};
    use std::sync::mpsc;
    use std::sync::Arc;
    use std::thread::JoinHandle;
    use windows::Win32::Foundation::{HMODULE, HWND, LPARAM, RECT, WPARAM};
    use windows::Win32::Graphics::Dwm::{DwmGetWindowAttribute, DWMWA_EXTENDED_FRAME_BOUNDS};
    use windows::Win32::System::SystemInformation::GetTickCount;
    use windows::Win32::System::Threading::GetCurrentThreadId;
    use windows::Win32::UI::Accessibility::{SetWinEventHook, UnhookWinEvent, HWINEVENTHOOK};
    use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::{
        GetMessageW, InternalGetWindowText, PostThreadMessageW, MSG, WINEVENT_OUTOFCONTEXT,
        WINEVENT_SKIPOWNPROCESS, WM_QUIT,
    };

    fn frame_of(h: HWND) -> Option<(i32, i32, i32, i32)> {
        if h.0.is_null() {
            return None;
        }
        let mut r = RECT::default();
        unsafe {
            DwmGetWindowAttribute(
                h,
                DWMWA_EXTENDED_FRAME_BOUNDS,
                &mut r as *mut RECT as *mut core::ffi::c_void,
                std::mem::size_of::<RECT>() as u32,
            )
            .ok()?;
        }
        Some((r.left, r.top, r.right, r.bottom))
    }

    fn title_of(h: HWND) -> String {
        if h.0.is_null() {
            return String::new();
        }
        let mut buf = [0u16; 256];
        let n = unsafe { InternalGetWindowText(h, &mut buf) };
        String::from_utf16_lossy(&buf[..n.max(0) as usize])
    }

    /// Quick callback: filter, gather, push, return.
    unsafe extern "system" fn on_event(
        _hook: HWINEVENTHOOK,
        event: u32,
        hwnd: HWND,
        id_object: i32,
        id_child: i32,
        _thread: u32,
        t: u32,
    ) {
        if !keep_event(event, id_object, id_child) {
            return;
        }
        push(EvRec {
            t,
            event,
            hwnd: hwnd.0 as isize,
            title: title_of(hwnd),
            frame: frame_of(hwnd),
        });
    }

    /// RAII listener. Drop = WM_QUIT + join; the thread unhooks.
    pub struct EventWatch {
        tid: Arc<AtomicU32>,
        handle: Option<JoinHandle<()>>,
        pub t0: u32,
        pub hooks: usize,
    }

    impl EventWatch {
        pub fn start() -> Self {
            let _ = take();
            let tid = Arc::new(AtomicU32::new(0));
            let tid_thread = tid.clone();
            let (ready_tx, ready_rx) = mpsc::channel::<(usize, u32)>();
            let handle = std::thread::spawn(move || unsafe {
                tid_thread.store(GetCurrentThreadId(), Ordering::Relaxed);
                let mut hooks: Vec<HWINEVENTHOOK> = Vec::new();
                for (lo, hi) in HOOK_RANGES {
                    let h = SetWinEventHook(
                        lo,
                        hi,
                        HMODULE::default(),
                        Some(on_event),
                        0,
                        0,
                        WINEVENT_OUTOFCONTEXT | WINEVENT_SKIPOWNPROCESS,
                    );
                    if !h.is_invalid() {
                        hooks.push(h);
                    }
                }
                let _ = ready_tx.send((hooks.len(), GetTickCount()));
                if !hooks.is_empty() {
                    let mut msg = MSG::default();
                    while GetMessageW(&mut msg, HWND::default(), 0, 0).0 > 0 {}
                }
                for h in hooks {
                    let _ = UnhookWinEvent(h);
                }
            });
            let (hooks, t0) = ready_rx.recv().unwrap_or((0, 0));
            EventWatch {
                tid,
                handle: Some(handle),
                t0,
                hooks,
            }
        }

        /// Stop listening and return the gathered events.
        pub fn finish(mut self) -> (u32, usize, Vec<EvRec>) {
            self.stop();
            (self.t0, self.hooks, take())
        }

        fn stop(&mut self) {
            let tid = self.tid.load(Ordering::Relaxed);
            if tid != 0 {
                unsafe {
                    let _ = PostThreadMessageW(tid, WM_QUIT, WPARAM(0), LPARAM(0));
                }
            }
            if let Some(h) = self.handle.take() {
                let _ = h.join();
            }
        }
    }

    impl Drop for EventWatch {
        fn drop(&mut self) {
            self.stop();
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(not(all(windows, feature = "win32")))]
mod imp {
    use super::*;
    pub struct EventWatch {
        pub t0: u32,
        pub hooks: usize,
    }
    impl EventWatch {
        pub fn start() -> Self {
            let _ = take();
            EventWatch { t0: 0, hooks: 0 }
        }
        pub fn finish(self) -> (u32, usize, Vec<EvRec>) {
            (0, 0, take())
        }
    }
}

pub use imp::EventWatch;

/// `sm winevents --ms N --out <file>`: listen only (no input), then write.
pub fn listen(ms: u64, out: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
    let ms = ms.min(600_000);
    let w = EventWatch::start();
    if w.hooks == 0 {
        return Err("winevents: SetWinEventHook failed (0 hooks)".into());
    }
    std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(ms));
    let (t0, hooks, recs) = w.finish();
    write_events(out, &format!("winevents --ms {ms}"), t0, hooks, &recs, &[])
        .map_err(|e| format!("winevents: write {out}: {e}"))?;
    Ok(format!("winevents done ms={ms} events={} hooks={hooks} out={out}", recs.len()))
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn names_and_filter() {
        assert_eq!(event_name(0x0003), Some("FOREGROUND"));
        assert_eq!(event_name(0x000A), Some("MOVESIZESTART"));
        assert_eq!(event_name(0x0016), Some("MINIMIZESTART"));
        assert_eq!(event_name(0x8016), Some("ARRANGMENTPREVIEW"));
        assert_eq!(event_name(0x8005), None); // EVENT_OBJECT_FOCUS not ours
        assert!(keep_event(0x000B, 0, 0));
        assert!(!keep_event(0x000B, -4, 0)); // OBJID_CLIENT
        assert!(!keep_event(0x000B, 0, 3)); // child element
        assert!(!keep_event(0x0001, 0, 0)); // EVENT_SYSTEM_SOUND not hooked
        // every named event sits inside one hook range
        for e in [0x3u32, 0xA, 0xB, 0x14, 0x15, 0x16, 0x17, 0x8016] {
            assert!(HOOK_RANGES.iter().any(|(lo, hi)| e >= *lo && e <= *hi));
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn line_format() {
        let r = EvRec {
            t: 1_000_250,
            event: EVENT_SYSTEM_MOVESIZESTART,
            hwnd: 393774,
            title: "SM-LIVE-A".into(),
            frame: Some((48, 429, 574, 842)),
        };
        assert_eq!(
            format_line(&r, 1_000_000),
            "+250 t=1000250 MOVESIZESTART hwnd=393774 title='SM-LIVE-A' frame=(48,429,574,842)"
        );
        let r2 = EvRec { frame: None, ..r };
        assert!(format_line(&r2, 1_000_000).ends_with("frame=n/a"));
    }
}

===== FILE: PRINCIPLES.md =====
# Smooth Moves — principles

## What this is
Motor control for Windows GUIs: human-like pointer paths, clicks, wheel, window drag, optional idle fidget. The **hands**.

## What this is not
Not a web-page brain. Not triage/snap/heuristics. Not open-ended research.

## When to use
GUI-only surfaces where you already know the target. Structure/API first for web facts.

## Frozen

## Rust port notes
- Crate is motor-only (same boundary).
- `cfg(windows)` gates Win32; stubs exist for non-Windows compile checks.
- PowerShell package remains the behavioral reference — do not delete or replace it from this crate.

## State checks (Order 17)
- Prefer **region or hwnd** for settle / PNG diff / fingerprint state checks.
- Whole-screen fingerprint is layout-only (easy miss); do not treat it as the default proof.
- Foreground-only motor. No silent money, mail, or credential surface clicks.
